// FILE: DRAFT_firestore.rules

rules_version = '2';
service cloud.firestore {
  match /databases/{database}/documents {
    // Pillar 4.1: Default-deny global safety net
    match /{document=**} {
      allow read, write: if false;
    }

    // Core Reusable Primitives
    function incoming() {
      return request.resource.data;
    }

    function existing() {
      return resource.data;
    }

    function isSignedIn() {
      return request.auth != null;
    }

    function isVerified() {
      return isSignedIn() && request.auth.token.email_verified == true;
    }

    function isBootstrappedAdmin() {
      return isVerified() && request.auth.token.email == 'zebeneasfye5@gmail.com';
    }

    function isAdmin() {
      return isBootstrappedAdmin() || (
        isVerified() && exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/admins/$(request.auth.uid))
      );
    }

    function isValidId(id) {
      return id is string && id.size() >= 1 && id.size() <= 128 && id.matches('^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+$');
    }

    function isValidStr(val, minLen, maxLen) {
      return val is string && val.size() >= minLen && val.size() <= maxLen;
    }

    function isOwnerUid(uid) {
      return isVerified() && request.auth.uid == uid;
    }

    // Validation Helpers
    function isValidUserProfile(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['uid', 'displayName', 'preferredRole', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly(['uid', 'displayName', 'preferredRole', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && isValidId(data.uid)
        && isValidStr(data.displayName, 1, 120)
        && (data.preferredRole == 'user' || data.preferredRole == 'inspector')
        && data.createdAt is timestamp
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp;
    }

    function isValidAdminRecord(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['uid', 'email', 'grantedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly(['uid', 'email', 'grantedAt'])
        && isValidId(data.uid)
        && isValidStr(data.email, 3, 160)
        && data.grantedAt is timestamp;
    }

    function isValidVehicle(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['id', 'plateNumber', 'vehicleType', 'makeModel', 'status', 'lat', 'lng', 'visibility', 'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly([
          'id', 'plateNumber', 'vehicleType', 'makeModel', 'year', 'capacity',
          'ownerId', 'ownerName', 'driverId', 'driverName', 'driverPhone',
          'speedLimiterId', 'status', 'inspectionStatus', 'inspectionExpiry',
          'fuelLevel', 'currentSpeed', 'speedLimit', 'lat', 'lng', 'heading',
          'currentLocationName', 'currentLocationImage', 'origin', 'destination',
          'routeId', 'routeName', 'lastUpdate', 'visibility', 'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
        ])
        && isValidId(data.id)
        && isValidStr(data.plateNumber, 1, 40)
        && isValidStr(data.vehicleType, 1, 40)
        && isValidStr(data.makeModel, 1, 120)
        && data.status in ['active', 'on_route', 'in_queue', 'maintenance', 'suspended']
        && data.lat is number && data.lat >= -90 && data.lat <= 90
        && data.lng is number && data.lng >= -180 && data.lng <= 180
        && data.visibility in ['public', 'private']
        && isValidId(data.createdBy)
        && data.createdAt is timestamp
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp
        && (!('year' in data) || (data.year is number && data.year >= 1950 && data.year <= 2100))
        && (!('capacity' in data) || (data.capacity is number && data.capacity >= 0 && data.capacity <= 500))
        && (!('ownerId' in data) || isValidStr(data.ownerId, 0, 128))
        && (!('ownerName' in data) || isValidStr(data.ownerName, 0, 160))
        && (!('driverId' in data) || isValidStr(data.driverId, 0, 128))
        && (!('driverName' in data) || isValidStr(data.driverName, 0, 120))
        && (!('driverPhone' in data) || isValidStr(data.driverPhone, 0, 40))
        && (!('speedLimiterId' in data) || isValidStr(data.speedLimiterId, 0, 64))
        && (!('inspectionStatus' in data) || data.inspectionStatus in ['passed', 'warning', 'expired'])
        && (!('inspectionExpiry' in data) || isValidStr(data.inspectionExpiry, 0, 40))
        && (!('fuelLevel' in data) || (data.fuelLevel is number && data.fuelLevel >= 0 && data.fuelLevel <= 100))
        && (!('currentSpeed' in data) || (data.currentSpeed is number && data.currentSpeed >= 0 && data.currentSpeed <= 300))
        && (!('speedLimit' in data) || (data.speedLimit is number && data.speedLimit >= 10 && data.speedLimit <= 200))
        && (!('heading' in data) || (data.heading is number && data.heading >= 0 && data.heading <= 360))
        && (!('currentLocationName' in data) || isValidStr(data.currentLocationName, 0, 200))
        && (!('currentLocationImage' in data) || isValidStr(data.currentLocationImage, 0, 500))
        && (!('origin' in data) || isValidStr(data.origin, 0, 160))
        && (!('destination' in data) || isValidStr(data.destination, 0, 160))
        && (!('routeId' in data) || isValidStr(data.routeId, 0, 128))
        && (!('routeName' in data) || isValidStr(data.routeName, 0, 200))
        && (!('lastUpdate' in data) || isValidStr(data.lastUpdate, 0, 64));
    }

    function isValidDriver(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['id', 'fullName', 'licenseNumber', 'status', 'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly([
          'id', 'fullName', 'licenseNumber', 'licenseGrade', 'phone',
          'experienceYears', 'rating', 'status', 'penaltyPoints',
          'assignedPlate', 'avatarUrl', 'healthCertExpiry',
          'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
        ])
        && isValidId(data.id)
        && isValidStr(data.fullName, 1, 120)
        && isValidStr(data.licenseNumber, 1, 64)
        && data.status in ['active', 'on_route', 'resting', 'suspended']
        && isValidId(data.createdBy)
        && data.createdAt is timestamp
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp
        && (!('licenseGrade' in data) || isValidStr(data.licenseGrade, 0, 80))
        && (!('phone' in data) || isValidStr(data.phone, 0, 40))
        && (!('experienceYears' in data) || (data.experienceYears is number && data.experienceYears >= 0 && data.experienceYears <= 80))
        && (!('rating' in data) || (data.rating is number && data.rating >= 0 && data.rating <= 5))
        && (!('penaltyPoints' in data) || (data.penaltyPoints is number && data.penaltyPoints >= 0 && data.penaltyPoints <= 1000))
        && (!('assignedPlate' in data) || isValidStr(data.assignedPlate, 0, 40))
        && (!('avatarUrl' in data) || isValidStr(data.avatarUrl, 0, 500))
        && (!('healthCertExpiry' in data) || isValidStr(data.healthCertExpiry, 0, 40));
    }

    function isValidOwner(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['id', 'name', 'type', 'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly([
          'id', 'name', 'type', 'tin', 'phone', 'email', 'city',
          'bankName', 'bankAccountNo', 'telebirrPhone',
          'vehiclesCount', 'activeVehicles', 'fleetRevenueEtb',
          'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
        ])
        && isValidId(data.id)
        && isValidStr(data.name, 1, 160)
        && data.type in ['association', 'company', 'individual', 'cooperative']
        && isValidId(data.createdBy)
        && data.createdAt is timestamp
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp
        && (!('tin' in data) || isValidStr(data.tin, 0, 40))
        && (!('phone' in data) || isValidStr(data.phone, 0, 40))
        && (!('email' in data) || isValidStr(data.email, 0, 120))
        && (!('city' in data) || isValidStr(data.city, 0, 80))
        && (!('bankName' in data) || isValidStr(data.bankName, 0, 80))
        && (!('bankAccountNo' in data) || isValidStr(data.bankAccountNo, 0, 64))
        && (!('telebirrPhone' in data) || isValidStr(data.telebirrPhone, 0, 40))
        && (!('vehiclesCount' in data) || data.vehiclesCount is number)
        && (!('activeVehicles' in data) || data.activeVehicles is number)
        && (!('fleetRevenueEtb' in data) || data.fleetRevenueEtb is number);
    }

    function isValidQueue(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['id', 'terminalName', 'routeName', 'plateNumber', 'turnNumber', 'status', 'visibility', 'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly([
          'id', 'terminalId', 'terminalName', 'routeId', 'routeName',
          'vehicleId', 'plateNumber', 'vehicleType', 'driverName', 'driverPhone',
          'turnNumber', 'entryTime', 'estimatedDeparture', 'status',
          'capacity', 'bookedSeats', 'ticketCode', 'tariffPerSeat',
          'exitFeePaid', 'totalCollectedEtb', 'visibility',
          'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
        ])
        && isValidId(data.id)
        && isValidStr(data.terminalName, 1, 160)
        && isValidStr(data.routeName, 1, 160)
        && isValidStr(data.plateNumber, 1, 40)
        && data.turnNumber is number && data.turnNumber >= 0 && data.turnNumber <= 10000
        && data.status in ['waiting', 'calling', 'boarding', 'dispatched', 'cancelled']
        && data.visibility in ['public', 'private']
        && isValidId(data.createdBy)
        && data.createdAt is timestamp
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp
        && (!('terminalId' in data) || isValidStr(data.terminalId, 0, 80))
        && (!('routeId' in data) || isValidStr(data.routeId, 0, 80))
        && (!('vehicleId' in data) || isValidStr(data.vehicleId, 0, 80))
        && (!('vehicleType' in data) || isValidStr(data.vehicleType, 0, 40))
        && (!('driverName' in data) || isValidStr(data.driverName, 0, 120))
        && (!('driverPhone' in data) || isValidStr(data.driverPhone, 0, 40))
        && (!('entryTime' in data) || isValidStr(data.entryTime, 0, 40))
        && (!('estimatedDeparture' in data) || isValidStr(data.estimatedDeparture, 0, 40))
        && (!('capacity' in data) || data.capacity is number)
        && (!('bookedSeats' in data) || data.bookedSeats is number)
        && (!('ticketCode' in data) || isValidStr(data.ticketCode, 0, 64))
        && (!('tariffPerSeat' in data) || data.tariffPerSeat is number)
        && (!('exitFeePaid' in data) || data.exitFeePaid is bool)
        && (!('totalCollectedEtb' in data) || data.totalCollectedEtb is number);
    }

    function isValidRoute(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['id', 'name', 'origin', 'destination', 'standardTariffBirr', 'visibility', 'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly([
          'id', 'code', 'name', 'origin', 'destination',
          'distanceKm', 'standardTariffBirr', 'estimatedHours',
          'checkpointsCount', 'tollFee', 'roadQuality',
          'visibility', 'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
        ])
        && isValidId(data.id)
        && isValidStr(data.name, 1, 160)
        && isValidStr(data.origin, 1, 160)
        && isValidStr(data.destination, 1, 160)
        && data.standardTariffBirr is number && data.standardTariffBirr >= 0
        && data.visibility in ['public', 'private']
        && isValidId(data.createdBy)
        && data.createdAt is timestamp
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp
        && (!('code' in data) || isValidStr(data.code, 0, 40))
        && (!('distanceKm' in data) || data.distanceKm is number)
        && (!('estimatedHours' in data) || data.estimatedHours is number)
        && (!('checkpointsCount' in data) || data.checkpointsCount is number)
        && (!('tollFee' in data) || data.tollFee is number)
        && (!('roadQuality' in data) || data.roadQuality in ['expressway', 'asphalt_good', 'mountain_corridor', 'gravel']);
    }

    function isValidSafetyReport(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['id', 'trackingNumber', 'vehiclePlate', 'incidentType', 'severity', 'status', 'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly([
          'id', 'trackingNumber', 'vehiclePlate', 'reportedBy', 'reporterPhone',
          'incidentType', 'severity', 'timestamp', 'location', 'description',
          'hasAudio', 'audioNoteDuration', 'photoUrl', 'status',
          'speedRecorded', 'actionTaken', 'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
        ])
        && isValidId(data.id)
        && isValidStr(data.trackingNumber, 1, 64)
        && isValidStr(data.vehiclePlate, 1, 40)
        && data.incidentType in ['overspeed', 'overloading', 'reckless_driving', 'tariff_extortion', 'accident', 'mechanical_issue']
        && data.severity in ['low', 'medium', 'high', 'critical']
        && data.status in ['pending', 'under_investigation', 'resolved', 'fine_issued']
        && isValidId(data.createdBy)
        && data.createdAt is timestamp
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp
        && (!('reportedBy' in data) || isValidStr(data.reportedBy, 0, 120))
        && (!('reporterPhone' in data) || isValidStr(data.reporterPhone, 0, 40))
        && (!('timestamp' in data) || isValidStr(data.timestamp, 0, 64))
        && (!('location' in data) || isValidStr(data.location, 0, 200))
        && (!('description' in data) || isValidStr(data.description, 0, 1500))
        && (!('hasAudio' in data) || data.hasAudio is bool)
        && (!('audioNoteDuration' in data) || isValidStr(data.audioNoteDuration, 0, 20))
        && (!('photoUrl' in data) || isValidStr(data.photoUrl, 0, 500))
        && (!('speedRecorded' in data) || data.speedRecorded is number)
        && (!('actionTaken' in data) || isValidStr(data.actionTaken, 0, 600));
    }

    function isValidPayment(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['id', 'ticketNumber', 'vehiclePlate', 'amountBirr', 'paymentStatus', 'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly([
          'id', 'ticketNumber', 'passengerName', 'passengerPhone',
          'vehiclePlate', 'routeName', 'seatNumber', 'amountBirr',
          'paymentMethod', 'paymentRefNumber', 'paymentStatus', 'issuedAt',
          'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
        ])
        && isValidId(data.id)
        && isValidStr(data.ticketNumber, 1, 64)
        && isValidStr(data.vehiclePlate, 1, 40)
        && data.amountBirr is number && data.amountBirr >= 0
        && data.paymentStatus in ['completed', 'pending', 'refunded']
        && isValidId(data.createdBy)
        && data.createdAt is timestamp
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp
        && (!('passengerName' in data) || isValidStr(data.passengerName, 0, 120))
        && (!('passengerPhone' in data) || isValidStr(data.passengerPhone, 0, 40))
        && (!('routeName' in data) || isValidStr(data.routeName, 0, 160))
        && (!('seatNumber' in data) || data.seatNumber is number)
        && (!('paymentMethod' in data) || data.paymentMethod in ['telebirr', 'cbe_birr', 'mpesa', 'amole', 'cash', 'bank_transfer'])
        && (!('paymentRefNumber' in data) || isValidStr(data.paymentRefNumber, 0, 80))
        && (!('issuedAt' in data) || isValidStr(data.issuedAt, 0, 64));
    }

    function isValidExitFee(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['id', 'vehiclePlate', 'feeAmountBirr', 'receiptNumber', 'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly([
          'id', 'queueTurnId', 'vehiclePlate', 'vehicleType', 'ownerName',
          'driverName', 'routeName', 'feeAmountBirr', 'paymentMethod',
          'paymentRef', 'receiptNumber', 'dispatchedAt', 'clearedByInspector',
          'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
        ])
        && isValidId(data.id)
        && isValidStr(data.vehiclePlate, 1, 40)
        && data.feeAmountBirr is number && data.feeAmountBirr >= 0
        && isValidStr(data.receiptNumber, 1, 80)
        && isValidId(data.createdBy)
        && data.createdAt is timestamp
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp
        && (!('queueTurnId' in data) || isValidStr(data.queueTurnId, 0, 80))
        && (!('vehicleType' in data) || isValidStr(data.vehicleType, 0, 40))
        && (!('ownerName' in data) || isValidStr(data.ownerName, 0, 160))
        && (!('driverName' in data) || isValidStr(data.driverName, 0, 120))
        && (!('routeName' in data) || isValidStr(data.routeName, 0, 160))
        && (!('paymentMethod' in data) || data.paymentMethod in ['telebirr', 'cbe_birr', 'cash', 'bank_transfer'])
        && (!('paymentRef' in data) || isValidStr(data.paymentRef, 0, 80))
        && (!('dispatchedAt' in data) || isValidStr(data.dispatchedAt, 0, 64))
        && (!('clearedByInspector' in data) || isValidStr(data.clearedByInspector, 0, 120));
    }

    function isValidSettlement(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['id', 'settlementNumber', 'ownerName', 'vehiclePlate', 'netPayoutToOwnerBirr', 'status', 'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly([
          'id', 'settlementNumber', 'ownerId', 'ownerName', 'vehiclePlate',
          'routeName', 'periodDate', 'totalPassengersCount', 'grossFaresCollectedBirr',
          'terminalServiceFeeDeductedBirr', 'associationCommissionBirr', 'netPayoutToOwnerBirr',
          'payoutMethod', 'payoutAccountOrPhone', 'transactionRef', 'status',
          'handedOverBy', 'receivedBy', 'settledAt',
          'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
        ])
        && isValidId(data.id)
        && isValidStr(data.settlementNumber, 1, 64)
        && isValidStr(data.ownerName, 1, 160)
        && isValidStr(data.vehiclePlate, 1, 40)
        && data.netPayoutToOwnerBirr is number && data.netPayoutToOwnerBirr >= 0
        && data.status in ['paid', 'pending_handover', 'disputed']
        && isValidId(data.createdBy)
        && data.createdAt is timestamp
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp
        && (!('ownerId' in data) || isValidStr(data.ownerId, 0, 80))
        && (!('routeName' in data) || isValidStr(data.routeName, 0, 160))
        && (!('periodDate' in data) || isValidStr(data.periodDate, 0, 40))
        && (!('totalPassengersCount' in data) || data.totalPassengersCount is number)
        && (!('grossFaresCollectedBirr' in data) || data.grossFaresCollectedBirr is number)
        && (!('terminalServiceFeeDeductedBirr' in data) || data.terminalServiceFeeDeductedBirr is number)
        && (!('associationCommissionBirr' in data) || data.associationCommissionBirr is number)
        && (!('payoutMethod' in data) || data.payoutMethod in ['telebirr', 'bank_account', 'cbe_birr', 'cash_handover'])
        && (!('payoutAccountOrPhone' in data) || isValidStr(data.payoutAccountOrPhone, 0, 80))
        && (!('transactionRef' in data) || isValidStr(data.transactionRef, 0, 80))
        && (!('handedOverBy' in data) || isValidStr(data.handedOverBy, 0, 120))
        && (!('receivedBy' in data) || isValidStr(data.receivedBy, 0, 120))
        && (!('settledAt' in data) || isValidStr(data.settledAt, 0, 64));
    }

    function isValidContract(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['id', 'developerName', 'authorityOfficial', 'isSigned', 'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly([
          'id', 'developerName', 'authorityOfficial', 'contractDate',
          'contractValueBirr', 'warrantyMonths', 'isSigned',
          'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
        ])
        && isValidId(data.id)
        && isValidStr(data.developerName, 1, 160)
        && isValidStr(data.authorityOfficial, 1, 160)
        && data.isSigned is bool
        && isValidId(data.createdBy)
        && data.createdAt is timestamp
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp
        && (!('contractDate' in data) || isValidStr(data.contractDate, 0, 40))
        && (!('contractValueBirr' in data) || data.contractValueBirr is number)
        && (!('warrantyMonths' in data) || data.warrantyMonths is number);
    }

    function isValidEngagement(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['id', 'likeCount', 'visibility', 'updatedBy', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly(['id', 'likeCount', 'visibility', 'updatedBy', 'updatedAt'])
        && isValidId(data.id)
        && data.likeCount is number && data.likeCount >= 0
        && data.visibility == 'public'
        && isValidId(data.updatedBy)
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp;
    }

    // Health Connection Check Document
    match /test/connection {
      allow get: if isSignedIn();
    }

    // Users Collection (PII isolated to owner or admin)
    match /users/{userId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(userId) && (isOwnerUid(userId) || isAdmin());
      allow list: if isBootstrappedAdmin();
      allow create: if isValidId(userId)
        && isOwnerUid(userId)
        && isValidUserProfile(incoming())
        && incoming().uid == request.auth.uid
        && incoming().preferredRole == 'user'
        && incoming().createdAt == request.time
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time;
      allow update: if isValidId(userId)
        && isValidUserProfile(incoming())
        && (isOwnerUid(userId) || isAdmin())
        && incoming().uid == existing().uid
        && incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
        && (
          isAdmin()
          || (incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['displayName', 'updatedAt']))
        );
      allow delete: if isValidId(userId) && isAdmin();
    }

    // Admins Allowlist (Read/Write restricted to admins)
    match /admins/{adminId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(adminId) && (isOwnerUid(adminId) || isAdmin());
      allow list: if isBootstrappedAdmin();
      allow create: if isValidId(adminId)
        && isAdmin()
        && isValidAdminRecord(incoming())
        && incoming().grantedAt == request.time;
      allow update: if isValidId(adminId)
        && isAdmin()
        && isValidAdminRecord(incoming())
        && incoming().uid == existing().uid
        && incoming().grantedAt == existing().grantedAt;
      allow delete: if isValidId(adminId) && isAdmin();
    }

    // Public-Operational Fleet Vehicles
    match /vehicles/{vehicleId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(vehicleId) && isSignedIn()
        && (existing().visibility == 'public' || existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isAdmin());
      allow list: if isSignedIn()
        && (existing().visibility == 'public' || existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isBootstrappedAdmin());
      allow create: if isValidId(vehicleId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidVehicle(incoming())
        && incoming().id == vehicleId
        && incoming().createdBy == request.auth.uid
        && incoming().createdAt == request.time
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time;
      allow update: if isValidId(vehicleId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidVehicle(incoming())
        && incoming().id == existing().id
        && incoming().createdBy == existing().createdBy
        && incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
        && (
          isAdmin()
          || (
            existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid
            && incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly([
              'lat', 'lng', 'heading', 'currentSpeed', 'fuelLevel',
              'currentLocationName', 'currentLocationImage', 'lastUpdate', 'status', 'updatedAt'
            ])
          )
        );
      allow delete: if isValidId(vehicleId) && (isAdmin() || (isVerified() && existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid));
    }

    // Drivers (Contains license/phone PII -> owner or admin)
    match /drivers/{driverId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(driverId) && isVerified() && (existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isAdmin());
      allow list: if isVerified() && (existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isBootstrappedAdmin());
      allow create: if isValidId(driverId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidDriver(incoming())
        && incoming().id == driverId
        && incoming().createdBy == request.auth.uid
        && incoming().createdAt == request.time
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time;
      allow update: if isValidId(driverId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidDriver(incoming())
        && incoming().id == existing().id
        && incoming().createdBy == existing().createdBy
        && incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
        && (
          isAdmin()
          || (
            existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid
            && incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['phone', 'status', 'assignedPlate', 'updatedAt'])
          )
        );
      allow delete: if isValidId(driverId) && (isAdmin() || (isVerified() && existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid));
    }

    // Owners (Contains TIN/bank/phone PII -> owner or admin)
    match /owners/{ownerId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(ownerId) && isVerified() && (existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isAdmin());
      allow list: if isVerified() && (existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isBootstrappedAdmin());
      allow create: if isValidId(ownerId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidOwner(incoming())
        && incoming().id == ownerId
        && incoming().createdBy == request.auth.uid
        && incoming().createdAt == request.time
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time;
      allow update: if isValidId(ownerId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidOwner(incoming())
        && incoming().id == existing().id
        && incoming().createdBy == existing().createdBy
        && incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
        && (
          isAdmin()
          || (
            existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid
            && incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['phone', 'email', 'city', 'vehiclesCount', 'activeVehicles', 'updatedAt'])
          )
        );
      allow delete: if isValidId(ownerId) && (isAdmin() || (isVerified() && existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid));
    }

    // Queue Turns (Publicly visible departures for signed-in passengers; mutations by creator or admin)
    match /queues/{queueId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(queueId) && isSignedIn()
        && (existing().visibility == 'public' || existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isAdmin());
      allow list: if isSignedIn()
        && (existing().visibility == 'public' || existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isBootstrappedAdmin());
      allow create: if isValidId(queueId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidQueue(incoming())
        && incoming().id == queueId
        && incoming().createdBy == request.auth.uid
        && incoming().createdAt == request.time
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time;
      allow update: if isValidId(queueId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidQueue(incoming())
        && incoming().id == existing().id
        && incoming().createdBy == existing().createdBy
        && incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
        && (
          isAdmin()
          || (
            existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid
            && incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['status', 'bookedSeats', 'exitFeePaid', 'totalCollectedEtb', 'updatedAt'])
          )
        );
      allow delete: if isValidId(queueId) && (isAdmin() || (isVerified() && existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid));
    }

    // Routes & Tariffs (Publicly visible for signed-in users; admin/creator managed)
    match /routes/{routeId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(routeId) && isSignedIn()
        && (existing().visibility == 'public' || existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isAdmin());
      allow list: if isSignedIn()
        && (existing().visibility == 'public' || existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isBootstrappedAdmin());
      allow create: if isValidId(routeId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidRoute(incoming())
        && incoming().id == routeId
        && incoming().createdBy == request.auth.uid
        && incoming().createdAt == request.time
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time;
      allow update: if isValidId(routeId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidRoute(incoming())
        && incoming().id == existing().id
        && incoming().createdBy == existing().createdBy
        && incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
        && (
          isAdmin()
          || (
            existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid
            && incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['standardTariffBirr', 'tollFee', 'roadQuality', 'updatedAt'])
          )
        );
      allow delete: if isValidId(routeId) && (isAdmin() || (isVerified() && existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid));
    }

    // Safety Reports (Reporter sees own, Admin sees & resolves all)
    match /safetyReports/{reportId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(reportId) && isVerified() && (existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isAdmin());
      allow list: if isVerified() && (existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isBootstrappedAdmin());
      allow create: if isValidId(reportId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidSafetyReport(incoming())
        && incoming().id == reportId
        && incoming().createdBy == request.auth.uid
        && incoming().status == 'pending'
        && incoming().createdAt == request.time
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time;
      allow update: if isValidId(reportId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidSafetyReport(incoming())
        && incoming().id == existing().id
        && incoming().createdBy == existing().createdBy
        && incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
        && (
          isAdmin()
          || (
            existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid
            && existing().status == 'pending'
            && incoming().status == 'pending'
            && incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['description', 'location', 'updatedAt'])
          )
        );
      allow delete: if isValidId(reportId) && isAdmin();
    }

    // Passenger Payments (Terminal state locking on 'completed' unless admin)
    match /payments/{paymentId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(paymentId) && isVerified() && (existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isAdmin());
      allow list: if isVerified() && (existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isBootstrappedAdmin());
      allow create: if isValidId(paymentId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidPayment(incoming())
        && incoming().id == paymentId
        && incoming().createdBy == request.auth.uid
        && incoming().createdAt == request.time
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time;
      allow update: if isValidId(paymentId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidPayment(incoming())
        && incoming().id == existing().id
        && incoming().createdBy == existing().createdBy
        && incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
        && (
          isAdmin()
          || (
            existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid
            && existing().paymentStatus == 'pending'
            && incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['paymentStatus', 'paymentRefNumber', 'updatedAt'])
          )
        );
      allow delete: if isValidId(paymentId) && isAdmin();
    }

    // Terminal Gate Exit Fees
    match /exitFees/{feeId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(feeId) && isVerified() && (existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isAdmin());
      allow list: if isVerified() && (existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isBootstrappedAdmin());
      allow create: if isValidId(feeId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidExitFee(incoming())
        && incoming().id == feeId
        && incoming().createdBy == request.auth.uid
        && incoming().createdAt == request.time
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time;
      allow update: if isValidId(feeId)
        && isAdmin()
        && isValidExitFee(incoming())
        && incoming().id == existing().id
        && incoming().createdBy == existing().createdBy
        && incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time;
      allow delete: if isValidId(feeId) && isAdmin();
    }

    // Owner Settlements (Terminal state locking on 'paid' unless admin)
    match /settlements/{settlementId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(settlementId) && isVerified() && (existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isAdmin());
      allow list: if isVerified() && (existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isBootstrappedAdmin());
      allow create: if isValidId(settlementId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidSettlement(incoming())
        && incoming().id == settlementId
        && incoming().createdBy == request.auth.uid
        && incoming().createdAt == request.time
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time;
      allow update: if isValidId(settlementId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidSettlement(incoming())
        && incoming().id == existing().id
        && incoming().createdBy == existing().createdBy
        && incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
        && (
          isAdmin()
          || (
            existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid
            && existing().status != 'paid'
            && incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['status', 'transactionRef', 'receivedBy', 'settledAt', 'updatedAt'])
          )
        );
      allow delete: if isValidId(settlementId) && isAdmin();
    }

    // Smart Contract Agreements
    match /contracts/{contractId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(contractId) && isVerified() && (existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isAdmin());
      allow list: if isVerified() && (existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isBootstrappedAdmin());
      allow create: if isValidId(contractId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidContract(incoming())
        && incoming().id == contractId
        && incoming().createdBy == request.auth.uid
        && incoming().createdAt == request.time
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time;
      allow update: if isValidId(contractId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidContract(incoming())
        && incoming().id == existing().id
        && incoming().createdBy == existing().createdBy
        && incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
        && (
          isAdmin()
          || (
            existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid
            && existing().isSigned == false
            && incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['isSigned', 'contractDate', 'updatedAt'])
          )
        );
      allow delete: if isValidId(contractId) && isAdmin();
    }

    // Public App Engagement (Likes)
    match /engagement/{docId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(docId) && isSignedIn() && existing().visibility == 'public';
      allow list: if isSignedIn() && existing().visibility == 'public';
      allow create: if isValidId(docId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidEngagement(incoming())
        && incoming().id == docId
        && incoming().updatedBy == request.auth.uid
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time;
      allow update: if isValidId(docId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidEngagement(incoming())
        && incoming().id == existing().id
        && incoming().updatedBy == request.auth.uid
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
        && incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['likeCount', 'updatedBy', 'updatedAt']);
      allow delete: if isValidId(docId) && isAdmin();
    }
  }
}

        "paymentStatus": { "type": "string", "enum": ["completed", "pending", "refunded"] },

        "createdBy": { "type": "string", "minLength": 1, "maxLength": 128, "pattern": "^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+$" },

    },
    "TerminalExitFee": {

        "clearedByInspector": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 120 },
        "createdBy": { "type": "string", "minLength": 1, "maxLength": 128, "pattern": "^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+$" },
        "createdAt": { "type": "string", "format": "date-time" },

    "OwnerSettlement": {

        "ownerId": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 80 },
        "ownerName": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 160 },
        "vehiclePlate": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 40 },
        "routeName": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 160 },
        "periodDate": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 40 },
        "totalPassengersCount": { "type": "number" },
        "grossFaresCollectedBirr": { "type": "number" },
        "terminalServiceFeeDeductedBirr": { "type": "number" },
        "associationCommissionBirr": { "type": "number" },
        "netPayoutToOwnerBirr": { "type": "number" },
        "payoutMethod": { "type": "string", "enum": ["telebirr", "bank_account", "cbe_birr", "cash_handover"] },
        "payoutAccountOrPhone": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 80 },
        "transactionRef": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 80 },
        "status": { "type": "string", "enum": ["paid", "pending_handover", "disputed"] },
        "handedOverBy": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 120 },
        "receivedBy": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 120 },
        "settledAt": { "type": "string", "maxLength": 64 },
        "createdBy": { "type": "string", "minLength": 1, "maxLength": 128, "pattern": "^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+$" },

    "SmartContract": {

    "AppEngagement": {

// =============================================================================
// FILE: firestore.rules
// =============================================================================

rules_version = '2';
service cloud.firestore {
  match /databases/{database}/documents {
    // Pillar 4.1: Default-deny global safety net
    match /{document=**} {
      allow read, write: if false;
    }

    // Core Reusable Primitives
    function incoming() {
      return request.resource.data;
    }

    function existing() {
      return resource.data;
    }

    function isSignedIn() {
      return request.auth != null;
    }

    function isVerified() {
      return isSignedIn() && request.auth.token.email_verified == true;
    }

    function isBootstrappedAdmin() {
      return isVerified() && request.auth.token.email == 'zebeneasfye5@gmail.com';
    }

    function isAdmin() {
      return isBootstrappedAdmin() || (
        isVerified() && exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/admins/$(request.auth.uid))
      );
    }

    function isValidId(id) {
      return id is string && id.size() >= 1 && id.size() <= 128 && id.matches('^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+$');
    }

    function isValidStr(val, minLen, maxLen) {
      return val is string && val.size() >= minLen && val.size() <= maxLen;
    }

    function isOwnerUid(uid) {
      return isVerified() && request.auth.uid == uid;
    }

    // Validation Helpers
    function isValidUserProfile(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['uid', 'displayName', 'preferredRole', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly(['uid', 'displayName', 'preferredRole', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && isValidId(data.uid)
        && isValidStr(data.displayName, 1, 120)
        && (data.preferredRole == 'user' || data.preferredRole == 'inspector')
        && data.createdAt is timestamp
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp;
    }

    function isValidAdminRecord(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['uid', 'email', 'grantedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly(['uid', 'email', 'grantedAt'])
        && isValidId(data.uid)
        && isValidStr(data.email, 3, 160)
        && data.grantedAt is timestamp;
    }

    function isValidVehicle(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['id', 'plateNumber', 'vehicleType', 'makeModel', 'status', 'lat', 'lng', 'visibility', 'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly([
          'id', 'plateNumber', 'vehicleType', 'makeModel', 'year', 'capacity',
          'ownerId', 'ownerName', 'driverId', 'driverName', 'driverPhone',
          'speedLimiterId', 'status', 'inspectionStatus', 'inspectionExpiry',
          'fuelLevel', 'currentSpeed', 'speedLimit', 'lat', 'lng', 'heading',
          'currentLocationName', 'currentLocationImage', 'origin', 'destination',
          'routeId', 'routeName', 'lastUpdate', 'visibility', 'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
        ])
        && isValidId(data.id)
        && isValidStr(data.plateNumber, 1, 40)
        && isValidStr(data.vehicleType, 1, 40)
        && isValidStr(data.makeModel, 1, 120)
        && data.status in ['active', 'on_route', 'in_queue', 'maintenance', 'suspended']
        && data.lat is number && data.lat >= -90 && data.lat <= 90
        && data.lng is number && data.lng >= -180 && data.lng <= 180
        && data.visibility in ['public', 'private']
        && isValidId(data.createdBy)
        && data.createdAt is timestamp
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp
        && (!('year' in data) || (data.year is number && data.year >= 1950 && data.year <= 2100))
        && (!('capacity' in data) || (data.capacity is number && data.capacity >= 0 && data.capacity <= 500))
        && (!('ownerId' in data) || isValidStr(data.ownerId, 0, 128))
        && (!('ownerName' in data) || isValidStr(data.ownerName, 0, 160))
        && (!('driverId' in data) || isValidStr(data.driverId, 0, 128))
        && (!('driverName' in data) || isValidStr(data.driverName, 0, 120))
        && (!('driverPhone' in data) || isValidStr(data.driverPhone, 0, 40))
        && (!('speedLimiterId' in data) || isValidStr(data.speedLimiterId, 0, 64))
        && (!('inspectionStatus' in data) || data.inspectionStatus in ['passed', 'warning', 'expired'])
        && (!('inspectionExpiry' in data) || isValidStr(data.inspectionExpiry, 0, 40))
        && (!('fuelLevel' in data) || (data.fuelLevel is number && data.fuelLevel >= 0 && data.fuelLevel <= 100))
        && (!('currentSpeed' in data) || (data.currentSpeed is number && data.currentSpeed >= 0 && data.currentSpeed <= 300))
        && (!('speedLimit' in data) || (data.speedLimit is number && data.speedLimit >= 10 && data.speedLimit <= 200))
        && (!('heading' in data) || (data.heading is number && data.heading >= 0 && data.heading <= 360))
        && (!('currentLocationName' in data) || isValidStr(data.currentLocationName, 0, 200))
        && (!('currentLocationImage' in data) || isValidStr(data.currentLocationImage, 0, 500))
        && (!('origin' in data) || isValidStr(data.origin, 0, 160))
        && (!('destination' in data) || isValidStr(data.destination, 0, 160))
        && (!('routeId' in data) || isValidStr(data.routeId, 0, 128))
        && (!('routeName' in data) || isValidStr(data.routeName, 0, 200))
        && (!('lastUpdate' in data) || isValidStr(data.lastUpdate, 0, 64));
    }

    function isValidDriver(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['id', 'fullName', 'licenseNumber', 'status', 'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly([
          'id', 'fullName', 'licenseNumber', 'licenseGrade', 'phone',
          'experienceYears', 'rating', 'status', 'penaltyPoints',
          'assignedPlate', 'avatarUrl', 'healthCertExpiry',
          'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
        ])
        && isValidId(data.id)
        && isValidStr(data.fullName, 1, 120)
        && isValidStr(data.licenseNumber, 1, 64)
        && data.status in ['active', 'on_route', 'resting', 'suspended']
        && isValidId(data.createdBy)
        && data.createdAt is timestamp
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp
        && (!('licenseGrade' in data) || isValidStr(data.licenseGrade, 0, 80))
        && (!('phone' in data) || isValidStr(data.phone, 0, 40))
        && (!('experienceYears' in data) || (data.experienceYears is number && data.experienceYears >= 0 && data.experienceYears <= 80))
        && (!('rating' in data) || (data.rating is number && data.rating >= 0 && data.rating <= 5))
        && (!('penaltyPoints' in data) || (data.penaltyPoints is number && data.penaltyPoints >= 0 && data.penaltyPoints <= 1000))
        && (!('assignedPlate' in data) || isValidStr(data.assignedPlate, 0, 40))
        && (!('avatarUrl' in data) || isValidStr(data.avatarUrl, 0, 500))
        && (!('healthCertExpiry' in data) || isValidStr(data.healthCertExpiry, 0, 40));
    }

    function isValidOwner(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['id', 'name', 'type', 'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly([
          'id', 'name', 'type', 'tin', 'phone', 'email', 'city',
          'bankName', 'bankAccountNo', 'telebirrPhone',
          'vehiclesCount', 'activeVehicles', 'fleetRevenueEtb',
          'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
        ])
        && isValidId(data.id)
        && isValidStr(data.name, 1, 160)
        && data.type in ['association', 'company', 'individual', 'cooperative']
        && isValidId(data.createdBy)
        && data.createdAt is timestamp
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp
        && (!('tin' in data) || isValidStr(data.tin, 0, 40))
        && (!('phone' in data) || isValidStr(data.phone, 0, 40))
        && (!('email' in data) || isValidStr(data.email, 0, 120))
        && (!('city' in data) || isValidStr(data.city, 0, 80))
        && (!('bankName' in data) || isValidStr(data.bankName, 0, 80))
        && (!('bankAccountNo' in data) || isValidStr(data.bankAccountNo, 0, 64))
        && (!('telebirrPhone' in data) || isValidStr(data.telebirrPhone, 0, 40))
        && (!('vehiclesCount' in data) || data.vehiclesCount is number)
        && (!('activeVehicles' in data) || data.activeVehicles is number)
        && (!('fleetRevenueEtb' in data) || data.fleetRevenueEtb is number);
    }

    function isValidQueue(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['id', 'terminalName', 'routeName', 'plateNumber', 'turnNumber', 'status', 'visibility', 'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly([
          'id', 'terminalId', 'terminalName', 'routeId', 'routeName',
          'vehicleId', 'plateNumber', 'vehicleType', 'driverName', 'driverPhone',
          'turnNumber', 'entryTime', 'estimatedDeparture', 'status',
          'capacity', 'bookedSeats', 'ticketCode', 'tariffPerSeat',
          'exitFeePaid', 'totalCollectedEtb', 'visibility',
          'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
        ])
        && isValidId(data.id)
        && isValidStr(data.terminalName, 1, 160)
        && isValidStr(data.routeName, 1, 160)
        && isValidStr(data.plateNumber, 1, 40)
        && data.turnNumber is number && data.turnNumber >= 0 && data.turnNumber <= 10000
        && data.status in ['waiting', 'calling', 'boarding', 'dispatched', 'cancelled']
        && data.visibility in ['public', 'private']
        && isValidId(data.createdBy)
        && data.createdAt is timestamp
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp
        && (!('terminalId' in data) || isValidStr(data.terminalId, 0, 80))
        && (!('routeId' in data) || isValidStr(data.routeId, 0, 80))
        && (!('vehicleId' in data) || isValidStr(data.vehicleId, 0, 80))
        && (!('vehicleType' in data) || isValidStr(data.vehicleType, 0, 40))
        && (!('driverName' in data) || isValidStr(data.driverName, 0, 120))
        && (!('driverPhone' in data) || isValidStr(data.driverPhone, 0, 40))
        && (!('entryTime' in data) || isValidStr(data.entryTime, 0, 40))
        && (!('estimatedDeparture' in data) || isValidStr(data.estimatedDeparture, 0, 40))
        && (!('capacity' in data) || data.capacity is number)
        && (!('bookedSeats' in data) || data.bookedSeats is number)
        && (!('ticketCode' in data) || isValidStr(data.ticketCode, 0, 64))
        && (!('tariffPerSeat' in data) || data.tariffPerSeat is number)
        && (!('exitFeePaid' in data) || data.exitFeePaid is bool)
        && (!('totalCollectedEtb' in data) || data.totalCollectedEtb is number);
    }

    function isValidRoute(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['id', 'name', 'origin', 'destination', 'standardTariffBirr', 'visibility', 'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly([
          'id', 'code', 'name', 'origin', 'destination',
          'distanceKm', 'standardTariffBirr', 'estimatedHours',
          'checkpointsCount', 'tollFee', 'roadQuality',
          'visibility', 'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
        ])
        && isValidId(data.id)
        && isValidStr(data.name, 1, 160)
        && isValidStr(data.origin, 1, 160)
        && isValidStr(data.destination, 1, 160)
        && data.standardTariffBirr is number && data.standardTariffBirr >= 0
        && data.visibility in ['public', 'private']
        && isValidId(data.createdBy)
        && data.createdAt is timestamp
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp
        && (!('code' in data) || isValidStr(data.code, 0, 40))
        && (!('distanceKm' in data) || data.distanceKm is number)
        && (!('estimatedHours' in data) || data.estimatedHours is number)
        && (!('checkpointsCount' in data) || data.checkpointsCount is number)
        && (!('tollFee' in data) || data.tollFee is number)
        && (!('roadQuality' in data) || data.roadQuality in ['expressway', 'asphalt_good', 'mountain_corridor', 'gravel']);
    }

    function isValidSafetyReport(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['id', 'trackingNumber', 'vehiclePlate', 'incidentType', 'severity', 'status', 'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly([
          'id', 'trackingNumber', 'vehiclePlate', 'reportedBy', 'reporterPhone',
          'incidentType', 'severity', 'timestamp', 'location', 'description',
          'hasAudio', 'audioNoteDuration', 'photoUrl', 'status',
          'speedRecorded', 'actionTaken', 'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
        ])
        && isValidId(data.id)
        && isValidStr(data.trackingNumber, 1, 64)
        && isValidStr(data.vehiclePlate, 1, 40)
        && data.incidentType in ['overspeed', 'overloading', 'reckless_driving', 'tariff_extortion', 'accident', 'mechanical_issue']
        && data.severity in ['low', 'medium', 'high', 'critical']
        && data.status in ['pending', 'under_investigation', 'resolved', 'fine_issued']
        && isValidId(data.createdBy)
        && data.createdAt is timestamp
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp
        && (!('reportedBy' in data) || isValidStr(data.reportedBy, 0, 120))
        && (!('reporterPhone' in data) || isValidStr(data.reporterPhone, 0, 40))
        && (!('timestamp' in data) || isValidStr(data.timestamp, 0, 64))
        && (!('location' in data) || isValidStr(data.location, 0, 200))
        && (!('description' in data) || isValidStr(data.description, 0, 1500))
        && (!('hasAudio' in data) || data.hasAudio is bool)
        && (!('audioNoteDuration' in data) || isValidStr(data.audioNoteDuration, 0, 20))
        && (!('photoUrl' in data) || isValidStr(data.photoUrl, 0, 500))
        && (!('speedRecorded' in data) || data.speedRecorded is number)
        && (!('actionTaken' in data) || isValidStr(data.actionTaken, 0, 600));
    }

    function isValidPayment(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['id', 'ticketNumber', 'vehiclePlate', 'amountBirr', 'paymentStatus', 'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly([
          'id', 'ticketNumber', 'passengerName', 'passengerPhone',
          'vehiclePlate', 'routeName', 'seatNumber', 'amountBirr',
          'paymentMethod', 'paymentRefNumber', 'paymentStatus', 'issuedAt',
          'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
        ])
        && isValidId(data.id)
        && isValidStr(data.ticketNumber, 1, 64)
        && isValidStr(data.vehiclePlate, 1, 40)
        && data.amountBirr is number && data.amountBirr >= 0
        && data.paymentStatus in ['completed', 'pending', 'refunded']
        && isValidId(data.createdBy)
        && data.createdAt is timestamp
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp
        && (!('passengerName' in data) || isValidStr(data.passengerName, 0, 120))
        && (!('passengerPhone' in data) || isValidStr(data.passengerPhone, 0, 40))
        && (!('routeName' in data) || isValidStr(data.routeName, 0, 160))
        && (!('seatNumber' in data) || data.seatNumber is number)
        && (!('paymentMethod' in data) || data.paymentMethod in ['telebirr', 'cbe_birr', 'mpesa', 'amole', 'cash', 'bank_transfer'])
        && (!('paymentRefNumber' in data) || isValidStr(data.paymentRefNumber, 0, 80))
        && (!('issuedAt' in data) || isValidStr(data.issuedAt, 0, 64));
    }

    function isValidExitFee(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['id', 'vehiclePlate', 'feeAmountBirr', 'receiptNumber', 'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly([
          'id', 'queueTurnId', 'vehiclePlate', 'vehicleType', 'ownerName',
          'driverName', 'routeName', 'feeAmountBirr', 'paymentMethod',
          'paymentRef', 'receiptNumber', 'dispatchedAt', 'clearedByInspector',
          'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
        ])
        && isValidId(data.id)
        && isValidStr(data.vehiclePlate, 1, 40)
        && data.feeAmountBirr is number && data.feeAmountBirr >= 0
        && isValidStr(data.receiptNumber, 1, 80)
        && isValidId(data.createdBy)
        && data.createdAt is timestamp
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp
        && (!('queueTurnId' in data) || isValidStr(data.queueTurnId, 0, 80))
        && (!('vehicleType' in data) || isValidStr(data.vehicleType, 0, 40))
        && (!('ownerName' in data) || isValidStr(data.ownerName, 0, 160))
        && (!('driverName' in data) || isValidStr(data.driverName, 0, 120))
        && (!('routeName' in data) || isValidStr(data.routeName, 0, 160))
        && (!('paymentMethod' in data) || data.paymentMethod in ['telebirr', 'cbe_birr', 'cash', 'bank_transfer'])
        && (!('paymentRef' in data) || isValidStr(data.paymentRef, 0, 80))
        && (!('dispatchedAt' in data) || isValidStr(data.dispatchedAt, 0, 64))
        && (!('clearedByInspector' in data) || isValidStr(data.clearedByInspector, 0, 120));
    }

    function isValidSettlement(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['id', 'settlementNumber', 'ownerName', 'vehiclePlate', 'netPayoutToOwnerBirr', 'status', 'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly([
          'id', 'settlementNumber', 'ownerId', 'ownerName', 'vehiclePlate',
          'routeName', 'periodDate', 'totalPassengersCount', 'grossFaresCollectedBirr',
          'terminalServiceFeeDeductedBirr', 'associationCommissionBirr', 'netPayoutToOwnerBirr',
          'payoutMethod', 'payoutAccountOrPhone', 'transactionRef', 'status',
          'handedOverBy', 'receivedBy', 'settledAt',
          'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
        ])
        && isValidId(data.id)
        && isValidStr(data.settlementNumber, 1, 64)
        && isValidStr(data.ownerName, 1, 160)
        && isValidStr(data.vehiclePlate, 1, 40)
        && data.netPayoutToOwnerBirr is number && data.netPayoutToOwnerBirr >= 0
        && data.status in ['paid', 'pending_handover', 'disputed']
        && isValidId(data.createdBy)
        && data.createdAt is timestamp
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp
        && (!('ownerId' in data) || isValidStr(data.ownerId, 0, 80))
        && (!('routeName' in data) || isValidStr(data.routeName, 0, 160))
        && (!('periodDate' in data) || isValidStr(data.periodDate, 0, 40))
        && (!('totalPassengersCount' in data) || data.totalPassengersCount is number)
        && (!('grossFaresCollectedBirr' in data) || data.grossFaresCollectedBirr is number)
        && (!('terminalServiceFeeDeductedBirr' in data) || data.terminalServiceFeeDeductedBirr is number)
        && (!('associationCommissionBirr' in data) || data.associationCommissionBirr is number)
        && (!('payoutMethod' in data) || data.payoutMethod in ['telebirr', 'bank_account', 'cbe_birr', 'cash_handover'])
        && (!('payoutAccountOrPhone' in data) || isValidStr(data.payoutAccountOrPhone, 0, 80))
        && (!('transactionRef' in data) || isValidStr(data.transactionRef, 0, 80))
        && (!('handedOverBy' in data) || isValidStr(data.handedOverBy, 0, 120))
        && (!('receivedBy' in data) || isValidStr(data.receivedBy, 0, 120))
        && (!('settledAt' in data) || isValidStr(data.settledAt, 0, 64));
    }

    function isValidContract(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['id', 'developerName', 'authorityOfficial', 'isSigned', 'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly([
          'id', 'developerName', 'authorityOfficial', 'contractDate',
          'contractValueBirr', 'warrantyMonths', 'isSigned',
          'createdBy', 'createdAt', 'updatedAt'
        ])
        && isValidId(data.id)
        && isValidStr(data.developerName, 1, 160)
        && isValidStr(data.authorityOfficial, 1, 160)
        && data.isSigned is bool
        && isValidId(data.createdBy)
        && data.createdAt is timestamp
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp
        && (!('contractDate' in data) || isValidStr(data.contractDate, 0, 40))
        && (!('contractValueBirr' in data) || data.contractValueBirr is number)
        && (!('warrantyMonths' in data) || data.warrantyMonths is number);
    }

    function isValidEngagement(data) {
      return data.keys().hasAll(['id', 'likeCount', 'visibility', 'updatedBy', 'updatedAt'])
        && data.keys().hasOnly(['id', 'likeCount', 'visibility', 'updatedBy', 'updatedAt'])
        && isValidId(data.id)
        && data.likeCount is number && data.likeCount >= 0
        && data.visibility == 'public'
        && isValidId(data.updatedBy)
        && data.updatedAt is timestamp;
    }

    // Health Connection Check Document
    match /test/connection {
      allow get: if isSignedIn();
    }

    // Users Collection (PII isolated to owner or admin)
    match /users/{userId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(userId) && (isOwnerUid(userId) || isAdmin());
      allow list: if isBootstrappedAdmin();
      allow create: if isValidId(userId)
        && isOwnerUid(userId)
        && isValidUserProfile(incoming())
        && incoming().uid == request.auth.uid
        && incoming().preferredRole == 'user'
        && incoming().createdAt == request.time
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time;
      allow update: if isValidId(userId)
        && isValidUserProfile(incoming())
        && (isOwnerUid(userId) || isAdmin())
        && incoming().uid == existing().uid
        && incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
        && (
          isAdmin()
          || (incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['displayName', 'updatedAt']))
        );
      allow delete: if isValidId(userId) && isAdmin();
    }

    // Admins Allowlist (Read/Write restricted to admins)
    match /admins/{adminId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(adminId) && (isOwnerUid(adminId) || isAdmin());
      allow list: if isBootstrappedAdmin();
      allow create: if isValidId(adminId)
        && isAdmin()
        && isValidAdminRecord(incoming())
        && incoming().grantedAt == request.time;
      allow update: if isValidId(adminId)
        && isAdmin()
        && isValidAdminRecord(incoming())
        && incoming().uid == existing().uid
        && incoming().grantedAt == existing().grantedAt;
      allow delete: if isValidId(adminId) && isAdmin();
    }

    // Public-Operational Fleet Vehicles
    match /vehicles/{vehicleId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(vehicleId) && isSignedIn()
        && (existing().visibility == 'public' || existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isAdmin());
      allow list: if isSignedIn()
        && (existing().visibility == 'public' || existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isBootstrappedAdmin());
      allow create: if isValidId(vehicleId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidVehicle(incoming())
        && incoming().id == vehicleId
        && incoming().createdBy == request.auth.uid
        && incoming().createdAt == request.time
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time;
      allow update: if isValidId(vehicleId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidVehicle(incoming())
        && incoming().id == existing().id
        && incoming().createdBy == existing().createdBy
        && incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
        && (
          isAdmin()
          || (
            existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid
            && incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly([
              'lat', 'lng', 'heading', 'currentSpeed', 'fuelLevel',
              'currentLocationName', 'currentLocationImage', 'lastUpdate', 'status', 'updatedAt'
            ])
          )
        );
      allow delete: if isValidId(vehicleId) && (isAdmin() || (isVerified() && existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid));
    }

    // Drivers (Contains license/phone PII -> owner or admin)
    match /drivers/{driverId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(driverId) && isVerified() && (existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isAdmin());
      allow list: if isVerified() && (existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isBootstrappedAdmin());
      allow create: if isValidId(driverId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidDriver(incoming())
        && incoming().id == driverId
        && incoming().createdBy == request.auth.uid
        && incoming().createdAt == request.time
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time;
      allow update: if isValidId(driverId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidDriver(incoming())
        && incoming().id == existing().id
        && incoming().createdBy == existing().createdBy
        && incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
        && (
          isAdmin()
          || (
            existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid
            && incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['phone', 'status', 'assignedPlate', 'updatedAt'])
          )
        );
      allow delete: if isValidId(driverId) && (isAdmin() || (isVerified() && existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid));
    }

    // Owners (Contains TIN/bank/phone PII -> owner or admin)
    match /owners/{ownerId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(ownerId) && isVerified() && (existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isAdmin());
      allow list: if isVerified() && (existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isBootstrappedAdmin());
      allow create: if isValidId(ownerId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidOwner(incoming())
        && incoming().id == ownerId
        && incoming().createdBy == request.auth.uid
        && incoming().createdAt == request.time
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time;
      allow update: if isValidId(ownerId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidOwner(incoming())
        && incoming().id == existing().id
        && incoming().createdBy == existing().createdBy
        && incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
        && (
          isAdmin()
          || (
            existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid
            && incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['phone', 'email', 'city', 'vehiclesCount', 'activeVehicles', 'updatedAt'])
          )
        );
      allow delete: if isValidId(ownerId) && (isAdmin() || (isVerified() && existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid));
    }

    // Queue Turns (Publicly visible departures for signed-in passengers; mutations by creator or admin)
    match /queues/{queueId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(queueId) && isSignedIn()
        && (existing().visibility == 'public' || existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isAdmin());
      allow list: if isSignedIn()
        && (existing().visibility == 'public' || existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isBootstrappedAdmin());
      allow create: if isValidId(queueId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidQueue(incoming())
        && incoming().id == queueId
        && incoming().createdBy == request.auth.uid
        && incoming().createdAt == request.time
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time;
      allow update: if isValidId(queueId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidQueue(incoming())
        && incoming().id == existing().id
        && incoming().createdBy == existing().createdBy
        && incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
        && (
          isAdmin()
          || (
            existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid
            && incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['status', 'bookedSeats', 'exitFeePaid', 'totalCollectedEtb', 'updatedAt'])
          )
        );
      allow delete: if isValidId(queueId) && (isAdmin() || (isVerified() && existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid));
    }

    // Routes & Tariffs (Publicly visible for signed-in users; admin/creator managed)
    match /routes/{routeId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(routeId) && isSignedIn()
        && (existing().visibility == 'public' || existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isAdmin());
      allow list: if isSignedIn()
        && (existing().visibility == 'public' || existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isBootstrappedAdmin());
      allow create: if isValidId(routeId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidRoute(incoming())
        && incoming().id == routeId
        && incoming().createdBy == request.auth.uid
        && incoming().createdAt == request.time
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time;
      allow update: if isValidId(routeId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidRoute(incoming())
        && incoming().id == existing().id
        && incoming().createdBy == existing().createdBy
        && incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
        && (
          isAdmin()
          || (
            existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid
            && incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['standardTariffBirr', 'tollFee', 'roadQuality', 'updatedAt'])
          )
        );
      allow delete: if isValidId(routeId) && (isAdmin() || (isVerified() && existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid));
    }

    // Safety Reports (Reporter sees own, Admin sees & resolves all)
    match /safetyReports/{reportId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(reportId) && isVerified() && (existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isAdmin());
      allow list: if isVerified() && (existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isBootstrappedAdmin());
      allow create: if isValidId(reportId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidSafetyReport(incoming())
        && incoming().id == reportId
        && incoming().createdBy == request.auth.uid
        && incoming().status == 'pending'
        && incoming().createdAt == request.time
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time;
      allow update: if isValidId(reportId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidSafetyReport(incoming())
        && incoming().id == existing().id
        && incoming().createdBy == existing().createdBy
        && incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
        && (
          isAdmin()
          || (
            existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid
            && existing().status == 'pending'
            && incoming().status == 'pending'
            && incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['description', 'location', 'updatedAt'])
          )
        );
      allow delete: if isValidId(reportId) && isAdmin();
    }

    // Passenger Payments (Terminal state locking on 'completed' unless admin)
    match /payments/{paymentId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(paymentId) && isVerified() && (existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isAdmin());
      allow list: if isVerified() && (existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isBootstrappedAdmin());
      allow create: if isValidId(paymentId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidPayment(incoming())
        && incoming().id == paymentId
        && incoming().createdBy == request.auth.uid
        && incoming().createdAt == request.time
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time;
      allow update: if isValidId(paymentId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidPayment(incoming())
        && incoming().id == existing().id
        && incoming().createdBy == existing().createdBy
        && incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
        && (
          isAdmin()
          || (
            existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid
            && existing().paymentStatus == 'pending'
            && incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['paymentStatus', 'paymentRefNumber', 'updatedAt'])
          )
        );
      allow delete: if isValidId(paymentId) && isAdmin();
    }

    // Terminal Gate Exit Fees
    match /exitFees/{feeId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(feeId) && isVerified() && (existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isAdmin());
      allow list: if isVerified() && (existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isBootstrappedAdmin());
      allow create: if isValidId(feeId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidExitFee(incoming())
        && incoming().id == feeId
        && incoming().createdBy == request.auth.uid
        && incoming().createdAt == request.time
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time;
      allow update: if isValidId(feeId)
        && isAdmin()
        && isValidExitFee(incoming())
        && incoming().id == existing().id
        && incoming().createdBy == existing().createdBy
        && incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time;
      allow delete: if isValidId(feeId) && isAdmin();
    }

    // Owner Settlements (Terminal state locking on 'paid' unless admin)
    match /settlements/{settlementId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(settlementId) && isVerified() && (existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isAdmin());
      allow list: if isVerified() && (existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isBootstrappedAdmin());
      allow create: if isValidId(settlementId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidSettlement(incoming())
        && incoming().id == settlementId
        && incoming().createdBy == request.auth.uid
        && incoming().createdAt == request.time
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time;
      allow update: if isValidId(settlementId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidSettlement(incoming())
        && incoming().id == existing().id
        && incoming().createdBy == existing().createdBy
        && incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
        && (
          isAdmin()
          || (
            existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid
            && existing().status != 'paid'
            && incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['status', 'transactionRef', 'receivedBy', 'settledAt', 'updatedAt'])
          )
        );
      allow delete: if isValidId(settlementId) && isAdmin();
    }

    // Smart Contract Agreements
    match /contracts/{contractId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(contractId) && isVerified() && (existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isAdmin());
      allow list: if isVerified() && (existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid || isBootstrappedAdmin());
      allow create: if isValidId(contractId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidContract(incoming())
        && incoming().id == contractId
        && incoming().createdBy == request.auth.uid
        && incoming().createdAt == request.time
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time;
      allow update: if isValidId(contractId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidContract(incoming())
        && incoming().id == existing().id
        && incoming().createdBy == existing().createdBy
        && incoming().createdAt == existing().createdAt
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
        && (
          isAdmin()
          || (
            existing().createdBy == request.auth.uid
            && existing().isSigned == false
            && incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['isSigned', 'contractDate', 'updatedAt'])
          )
        );
      allow delete: if isValidId(contractId) && isAdmin();
    }

    // Public App Engagement (Likes)
    match /engagement/{docId} {
      allow get: if isValidId(docId) && isSignedIn() && existing().visibility == 'public';
      allow list: if isSignedIn() && existing().visibility == 'public';
      allow create: if isValidId(docId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidEngagement(incoming())
        && incoming().id == docId
        && incoming().updatedBy == request.auth.uid
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time;
      allow update: if isValidId(docId)
        && isVerified()
        && isValidEngagement(incoming())
        && incoming().id == existing().id
        && incoming().updatedBy == request.auth.uid
        && incoming().updatedAt == request.time
        && incoming().diff(existing()).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['likeCount', 'updatedBy', 'updatedAt']);
      allow delete: if isValidId(docId) && isAdmin();
    }
  }
}

// FILE: public/icon.svg

// =============================================================================
// FILE: scripts/generate-icons.js
// =============================================================================

import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import zlib from 'zlib';

function createPng(width, height, r, g, b) {
  // PNG signature
  const signature = Buffer.from([137, 80, 78, 71, 13, 10, 26, 10]);

  // IHDR chunk
  const ihdrData = Buffer.alloc(13);
  ihdrData.writeUInt32BE(width, 0);
  ihdrData.writeUInt32BE(height, 4);
  ihdrData.writeUInt8(8, 8); // 8-bit depth
  ihdrData.writeUInt8(6, 9); // RGBA
  ihdrData.writeUInt8(0, 10); // Compression
  ihdrData.writeUInt8(0, 11); // Filter
  ihdrData.writeUInt8(0, 12); // Interlace
  const ihdrChunk = makeChunk('IHDR', ihdrData);

  // Raw pixel data with filter byte 0 per line
  const rawData = Buffer.alloc(height * (width * 4 + 1));
  let offset = 0;
  for (let y = 0; y < height; y++) {
    rawData[offset++] = 0; // Filter None
    for (let x = 0; x < width; x++) {
      // Create a nice gradient with gold/emerald ring
      const dx = x - width / 2;
      const dy = y - height / 2;
      const dist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
      const maxR = width / 2;

      let pr = r;
      let pg = g;
      let pb = b;

      // Inner emblem circle
      if (dist < maxR * 0.75 && dist > maxR * 0.7) {
        pr = 245; pg = 158; pb = 11; // Gold ring
      } else if (dist < maxR * 0.6) {
        pr = Math.min(255, r + 20);
        pg = Math.min(255, g + 40);
        pb = Math.min(255, b + 30);
      }

      rawData[offset++] = pr;
      rawData[offset++] = pg;
      rawData[offset++] = pb;
      rawData[offset++] = 255; // Alpha
    }
  }

  const compressedData = zlib.deflateSync(rawData);
  const idatChunk = makeChunk('IDAT', compressedData);

  // IEND chunk
  const iendChunk = makeChunk('IEND', Buffer.alloc(0));

  return Buffer.concat([signature, ihdrChunk, idatChunk, iendChunk]);
}

function makeChunk(type, data) {
  const len = data.length;
  const chunk = Buffer.alloc(4 + 4 + len + 4);
  chunk.writeUInt32BE(len, 0);
  chunk.write(type, 4, 4, 'ascii');
  data.copy(chunk, 8);

  // Calculate CRC32
  const crc = crc32(chunk.subarray(4, 8 + len));
  chunk.writeInt32BE(crc, 8 + len);
  return chunk;
}

// Simple CRC32 table & calculator
const crcTable = new Int32Array(256);
for (let n = 0; n < 256; n++) {
  let c = n;
  for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) {
    if (c & 1) c = 0xedb88320 ^ (c >>> 1);
    else c = c >>> 1;
  }
  crcTable[n] = c;
}

function crc32(buf) {
  let crc = -1;
  for (let i = 0; i < buf.length; i++) {
    crc = crcTable[(crc ^ buf[i]) & 0xff] ^ (crc >>> 8);
  }
  return crc ^ -1;
}

const publicDir = path.resolve('public');
if (!fs.existsSync(publicDir)) {
  fs.mkdirSync(publicDir, { recursive: true });
}

// Write PWA Icons
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(publicDir, 'pwa-192x192.png'), createPng(192, 192, 5, 120, 85));
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(publicDir, 'pwa-512x512.png'), createPng(512, 512, 5, 120, 85));
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(publicDir, 'pwa-maskable-512x512.png'), createPng(512, 512, 4, 90, 65));
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(publicDir, 'apple-touch-icon.png'), createPng(180, 180, 5, 120, 85));

console.log('Successfully generated PWA PNG assets in /public');


// =============================================================================
// FILE: security_spec.md
// =============================================================================

# Security Specification & Payload-First TDD (`security_spec.md`)

## 1. Data Invariants
1. **Identity Integrity**: Every client-created document (`vehicles`, `drivers`, `owners`, `queues`, `routes`, `safetyReports`, `payments`, `exitFees`, `settlements`, `contracts`) MUST bind `createdBy == request.auth.uid` and require `request.auth.token.email_verified == true`.
2. **Authority Admin Isolation**: Only verified authority admins (`zebeneasfye5@gmail.com` or UIDs listed in `/admins/{uid}`) may list/read/modify all-fleet PII collections (`drivers`, `owners`, `payments`, `exitFees`, `settlements`, `contracts`) or resolve/fine `safetyReports`.
3. **Immutable Ownership & Temporal Integrity**: `createdBy` and `createdAt` cannot be modified after creation; `createdAt == request.time` on create and `updatedAt == request.time` on update.
4. **No Blanket List Scraping**: Every `allow list` rule evaluates `resource.data` directly (either `resource.data.visibility == 'public'` or `resource.data.createdBy == request.auth.uid` or bootstrapped verified admin token) without calling `get()`/`exists()`.
5. **Terminal State Locking**: Once `payments.paymentStatus == 'completed'` or `settlements.status == 'paid'` or `contracts.isSigned == true`, non-admin users cannot modify the record.

## 2. The "Dirty Dozen" Adversarial Payloads (Must All Fail with `PERMISSION_DENIED`)
1. Unauthenticated read or write on `/vehicles/{id}` or `/payments/{id}`.
2. Spoofed `createdBy` (`createdBy: "someone_else_uid"`) during `create` on `/vehicles` or `/safetyReports`.
3. Unverified email (`email_verified: false`) attempting to pass admin or write gates.
4. Ghost/Shadow field injection (`{ ..., "isAdmin": true, "extraField": "hack" }`) rejected by `hasOnly(...)`.
5. Oversized document ID (`> 128` chars or special characters like `../admin`) rejected by `isValidId()`.
6. Oversized string payload (`> maxLength`) in `description`, `plateNumber`, or `fullName`.
7. Client-forged timestamp (`createdAt` or `updatedAt` != `request.time`).
8. Mutating `createdBy` or `createdAt` during an `update`.
9. Non-admin attempting to mark their own `/safetyReports/{id}` as `fine_issued` or `resolved`.
10. Non-admin attempting to edit a finalized `settlement` (`status == 'paid'`) or `contract` (`isSigned == true`).
11. Non-admin attempting `list` or `get` on `/users/{otherUserId}` or `/payments` belonging to another user.
12. Non-admin attempting to write directly to `/admins/{uid}`.


// =============================================================================
// FILE: server.ts
// =============================================================================

import 'dotenv/config';
import express from 'express';
import type { Request, Response } from 'express';
import path from 'path';
import fs from 'fs';
import http from 'http';
import zlib from 'zlib';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'url';
import { GoogleGenAI, ThinkingLevel } from '@google/genai';

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);
