const PORT = Number(process.env.PORT || 3000);

const LANGUAGE_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  am: 'Amharic (አማርኛ)',
  om: 'Afaan Oromoo (Oromo)',
  ti: 'Tigrinya (ትግርኛ)',
  so: 'Somali (Af-Soomaali)',
  en: 'English',
  ar: 'Arabic (العربية)',
  fr: 'French (Français)',
  zh: 'Simplified Chinese (简体中文)',
};

function getAiClient(apiVersion?: string): GoogleGenAI | null {
  const apiKey = process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY?.trim();
  if (!apiKey || apiKey === 'MY_GEMINI_API_KEY') {
    return null;
  }
  return new GoogleGenAI({
    apiKey,
    ...(apiVersion ? { apiVersion } : {}),
    httpOptions: {
      headers: {
        'User-Agent': 'aistudio-build',
      },
    },
  });
}

function getErrorStatusAndMessage(err: unknown): { status: number; message: string } {
  if (err && typeof err === 'object') {
    const record = err as Record<string, unknown>;
    const rawStatus = typeof record.status === 'number' ? record.status : 500;
    const status = rawStatus >= 400 && rawStatus < 600 ? rawStatus : 500;
    const message =
      typeof record.message === 'string' && record.message.trim()
        ? record.message
        : 'AI service request failed';
    return { status, message };
  }
  return { status: 500, message: String(err || 'Unknown server error') };
}

async function withRetry<T>(fn: () => Promise<T>, maxRetries = 2): Promise<T> {
  let lastErr: unknown;
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt <= maxRetries; attempt++) {
    try {
      return await fn();
    } catch (err) {
      lastErr = err;
      const { status } = getErrorStatusAndMessage(err);
      if ((status === 429 || status === 503 || status === 500) && attempt < maxRetries) {
        await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 700 * (attempt + 1)));
        continue;
      }
      throw err;
    }
  }
  throw lastErr;
}

async function startServer() {
  const app = express();
  app.use(express.json({ limit: '15mb' }));

  app.get('/api/health', (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
    const apiKey = process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY?.trim();
    res.json({
      ok: true,
      hasGeminiKey: Boolean(apiKey && apiKey !== 'MY_GEMINI_API_KEY'),
      languages: Object.keys(LANGUAGE_LABELS),
    });
  });

  app.post('/api/translate', async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    try {
      const { text, sourceLang, targetLang } = req.body as {
        text?: string;
        sourceLang?: string;
        targetLang?: string;
      };

      const cleanText = (text ?? '').trim();
      if (!cleanText) {
        res.status(400).json({ error: 'Text to translate is required.' });
        return;
      }

      const src = sourceLang && LANGUAGE_LABELS[sourceLang] ? sourceLang : 'auto';
      const tgt = targetLang && LANGUAGE_LABELS[targetLang] ? targetLang : 'en';

      if (src === tgt) {
        res.json({ translatedText: cleanText, sourceLang: src, targetLang: tgt });
        return;
      }

      const ai = getAiClient();
      if (!ai) {
        res.status(503).json({
          error: 'GEMINI_API_KEY is not configured on the server. Please set it in Settings > Secrets.',
        });
        return;
      }

      const srcLabel = src === 'auto' ? 'the detected language' : LANGUAGE_LABELS[src];
      const tgtLabel = LANGUAGE_LABELS[tgt];

      const response = await withRetry(() =>
        ai.models.generateContent({
          model: 'gemini-4-hydrogen',
          contents: `Translate the following message accurately and naturally from ${srcLabel} to ${tgtLabel}. Preserve numbers, vehicle license plates, Birr amounts, place names, and urgency. Output ONLY the translated text without quotes, labels, or explanations.\n\nMessage:\n${cleanText}`,
          config: {
            systemInstruction:
              'You are a professional real-time multilingual transport, dispatch, and citizen interpreter supporting Amharic, Afaan Oromoo, Tigrinya, Somali, English, Arabic, French, and Chinese. Always output ONLY the direct natural translation in the requested target language and script.',
            thinkingConfig: { thinkingLevel: ThinkingLevel.LOW },
          },
        })
      );

      const translatedText = (response.text ?? '').trim();
      if (!translatedText) {
        res.status(502).json({ error: 'Translation model returned an empty response.' });
        return;
      }

      res.json({ translatedText, sourceLang: src, targetLang: tgt });
    } catch (err) {
      const { status, message } = getErrorStatusAndMessage(err);
      res.status(status).json({ error: message });
    }
  });

  app.post('/api/transcribe', async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    try {
      const { audioBase64, mimeType, sourceLang } = req.body as {
        audioBase64?: string;
        mimeType?: string;
        sourceLang?: string;
      };

      const cleanBase64 = (audioBase64 ?? '').replace(/^data:[^;]+;base64,/, '').trim();
      if (!cleanBase64) {
        res.status(400).json({ error: 'Audio data is required for transcription.' });
        return;
      }

      const ai = getAiClient('v1beta');
      if (!ai) {
        res.status(503).json({
          error: 'GEMINI_API_KEY is not configured on the server. Please set it in Settings > Secrets.',
        });
        return;
      }

      const baseMime = (mimeType || 'audio/webm').split(';')[0].trim() || 'audio/webm';
      const langLabel = sourceLang && LANGUAGE_LABELS[sourceLang] ? LANGUAGE_LABELS[sourceLang] : '';
      const prompt = langLabel
        ? `The speaker is speaking in ${langLabel}. Transcribe the spoken audio verbatim in its native script and language. Output ONLY the transcribed words.`
        : 'Transcribe the spoken audio verbatim in its native script and language. Output ONLY the transcribed words.';

      const audioPart = {
        inlineData: {
          mimeType: baseMime,
          data: cleanBase64,
        },
      };

      let transcript = '';
      try {
        const response = await withRetry(() =>
          ai.models.generateContent({
            model: 'gemini-3.5-transcribe',
            contents: {
              parts: [audioPart, { text: prompt }],
            },
          })
        );
        transcript =
          (response.text ?? '').trim() ||
          (response.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts ?? [])
            .map((p) => (typeof p.text === 'string' ? p.text : ''))
            .join(' ')
            .trim();
      } catch {
        // Fall through to gemini-4-hydrogen multimodal transcription
      }

      if (!transcript) {
        const textAi = getAiClient() || ai;
        const fallbackResp = await withRetry(() =>
          textAi.models.generateContent({
            model: 'gemini-4-hydrogen',
            contents: {
              parts: [audioPart, { text: prompt }],
            },
            config: {
              thinkingConfig: { thinkingLevel: ThinkingLevel.LOW },
            },
          })
        );
        transcript = (fallbackResp.text ?? '').trim();
      }

      res.json({ transcript });
    } catch (err) {
      const { status, message } = getErrorStatusAndMessage(err);
      res.status(status).json({ error: message });
    }
  });

  app.post('/api/tts', async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
    try {
      const { text, langCode } = req.body as {
        text?: string;
        langCode?: string;
      };

      const cleanText = (text ?? '').trim();
      if (!cleanText) {
        res.status(400).json({ error: 'Text is required for speech synthesis.' });
        return;
      }

      const ai = getAiClient('v1beta');
      if (!ai) {
        res.status(503).json({
          error: 'GEMINI_API_KEY is not configured on the server. Please set it in Settings > Secrets.',
        });
        return;
      }

      const langLabel = langCode && LANGUAGE_LABELS[langCode] ? LANGUAGE_LABELS[langCode] : 'the text language';

      const response = await withRetry(() =>
        ai.models.generateContent({
          model: 'gemini-3.8-flash-lite-tts',
          contents: [
            {
              role: 'user',
              parts: [
                {
                  text: cleanText,
                  speechMetadata: {
                    style: `Clear, articulate, natural native ${langLabel} transport terminal announcement voice`,
                  },
                },
              ],
            },
          ],
          config: {
            responseModalities: ['AUDIO'],
            speechConfig: {
              voiceConfig: {
                prebuiltVoiceConfig: { voiceName: 'Kore' },
              },
            },
          },
        })
      );

      const audioBase64 = response.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.inlineData?.data;
      if (!audioBase64) {
        res.status(502).json({ error: 'Text-to-speech model did not return audio data.' });
        return;
      }

      res.json({ audioBase64, mimeType: 'audio/wav' });
    } catch (err) {
      const { status, message } = getErrorStatusAndMessage(err);
      res.status(status).json({ error: message });
    }
  });

  app.get('/api/export-source', (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
    try {
      const excludedDirs = new Set(['node_modules', 'dist', 'dev-dist', '.git', '.aistudio']);
      const excludedFiles = new Set(['.env', '.env.local', '.env.production', '.env.development', 'ዘበነ.txt']);

      const files: { relPath: string; data: Buffer }[] = [];
      const walk = (dir: string, prefix: string) => {
        for (const entry of fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true })) {
          if (entry.isDirectory()) {
            if (!excludedDirs.has(entry.name)) {
              walk(path.join(dir, entry.name), `${prefix}${entry.name}/`);
            }
          } else if (entry.isFile()) {
            if (excludedFiles.has(entry.name) || entry.name.endsWith('.zip')) continue;
            files.push({
              relPath: `national-transport-portal/${prefix}${entry.name}`,
              data: fs.readFileSync(path.join(dir, entry.name)),
            });
          }
        }
      };
      walk(__dirname, '');

      const crcTable = new Uint32Array(256);
      for (let i = 0; i < 256; i++) {
        let c = i;
        for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) {
          c = (c & 1) !== 0 ? 0xedb88320 ^ (c >>> 1) : c >>> 1;
        }
        crcTable[i] = c >>> 0;
      }
      const crc32 = (buf: Buffer): number => {
        let crc = 0xffffffff;
        for (let i = 0; i < buf.length; i++) {
          crc = crcTable[(crc ^ buf[i]) & 0xff] ^ (crc >>> 8);
        }
        return (crc ^ 0xffffffff) >>> 0;
      };

      const localChunks: Buffer[] = [];
      const centralChunks: Buffer[] = [];
      let offset = 0;

      for (const file of files) {
        const nameBuf = Buffer.from(file.relPath, 'utf8');
        const compressed = zlib.deflateRawSync(file.data);
        const crc = crc32(file.data);

        const localHeader = Buffer.alloc(30);
        localHeader.writeUInt32LE(0x04034b50, 0);
        localHeader.writeUInt16LE(20, 4);
        localHeader.writeUInt16LE(0x0800, 6);
        localHeader.writeUInt16LE(8, 8);
        localHeader.writeUInt16LE(0, 10);
        localHeader.writeUInt16LE(0, 12);
        localHeader.writeUInt32LE(crc, 14);
        localHeader.writeUInt32LE(compressed.length, 18);
        localHeader.writeUInt32LE(file.data.length, 22);
        localHeader.writeUInt16LE(nameBuf.length, 26);
        localHeader.writeUInt16LE(0, 28);

        localChunks.push(localHeader, nameBuf, compressed);

        const centralHeader = Buffer.alloc(46);
        centralHeader.writeUInt32LE(0x02014b50, 0);
        centralHeader.writeUInt16LE(20, 4);
        centralHeader.writeUInt16LE(20, 6);
        centralHeader.writeUInt16LE(0x0800, 8);
        centralHeader.writeUInt16LE(8, 10);
        centralHeader.writeUInt16LE(0, 12);
        centralHeader.writeUInt16LE(0, 14);
        centralHeader.writeUInt32LE(crc, 16);
        centralHeader.writeUInt32LE(compressed.length, 20);
        centralHeader.writeUInt32LE(file.data.length, 24);
        centralHeader.writeUInt16LE(nameBuf.length, 28);
        centralHeader.writeUInt16LE(0, 30);
        centralHeader.writeUInt16LE(0, 32);
        centralHeader.writeUInt16LE(0, 34);
        centralHeader.writeUInt16LE(0, 36);
        centralHeader.writeUInt32LE(0, 38);
        centralHeader.writeUInt32LE(offset, 42);

        centralChunks.push(centralHeader, nameBuf);
        offset += localHeader.length + nameBuf.length + compressed.length;
      }

      const centralDir = Buffer.concat(centralChunks);
      const endRecord = Buffer.alloc(22);
      endRecord.writeUInt32LE(0x06054b50, 0);
      endRecord.writeUInt16LE(0, 4);
      endRecord.writeUInt16LE(0, 6);
      endRecord.writeUInt16LE(files.length, 8);
      endRecord.writeUInt16LE(files.length, 10);
      endRecord.writeUInt32LE(centralDir.length, 12);
      endRecord.writeUInt32LE(offset, 16);
      endRecord.writeUInt16LE(0, 20);

      const zipBuffer = Buffer.concat([...localChunks, centralDir, endRecord]);
      res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/zip');
      res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', 'attachment; filename="national-transport-portal-source.zip"');
      res.setHeader('Content-Length', String(zipBuffer.length));
      res.send(zipBuffer);
    } catch (err) {
      const { status, message } = getErrorStatusAndMessage(err);
      res.status(status).json({ error: message });
    }
  });

  app.get('/api/export-single-file', (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
    try {
      const excludedDirs = new Set(['node_modules', 'dist', 'dev-dist', '.git', '.aistudio']);
      const excludedFiles = new Set([
        '.env',
        '.env.local',
        '.env.production',
        '.env.development',
        'bun.lock',
        'package-lock.json',
        'ዘበነ.txt',
      ]);
      const allowedExts = new Set([
        '.ts',
        '.tsx',
        '.js',
        '.jsx',
        '.css',
        '.html',
        '.json',
        '.rules',
        '.md',
        '.svg',
        '.example',
      ]);

      const collected: { relPath: string; content: string }[] = [];
      const walk = (dir: string, prefix: string) => {
        const entries = fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true }).sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
        for (const entry of entries) {
          if (entry.isDirectory()) {
            if (!excludedDirs.has(entry.name)) {
              walk(path.join(dir, entry.name), `${prefix}${entry.name}/`);
            }
          } else if (entry.isFile()) {
            if (excludedFiles.has(entry.name)) continue;
            const ext = path.extname(entry.name).toLowerCase();
            if (!allowedExts.has(ext) && entry.name !== '.gitignore' && entry.name !== '.env.example') {
              continue;
            }
            collected.push({
              relPath: `${prefix}${entry.name}`,
              content: fs.readFileSync(path.join(dir, entry.name), 'utf8'),
            });
          }
        }
      };
      walk(__dirname, '');

      const toc = collected.map((f, idx) => `  ${String(idx + 1).padStart(2, '0')}. ${f.relPath}`).join('\n');
      const header = [
        '================================================================================',
        '  የመንገድ እና ትራንስፖርት ባለስልጣን | NATIONAL TRANSPORT & FLEET COMMAND',
        '  COMPLETE SINGLE-FILE SOURCE CODE ARCHIVE (ዘበነ.txt)',
        `  Generated: ${new Date().toISOString()}`,
        `  Total Code Files Combined: ${collected.length}`,
        '================================================================================',
        '',
        'TABLE OF CONTENTS:',
        toc,
        '',
      ].join('\n');

      const body = collected
        .map(
          (f) =>
            `\n// =============================================================================\n// FILE: ${f.relPath}\n// =============================================================================\n\n${f.content}`
        )
        .join('\n');

      const combinedBuf = Buffer.from(`${header}${body}\n`, 'utf8');
      res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'text/plain; charset=utf-8');
      res.setHeader('Cache-Control', 'no-store');
      res.setHeader(
        'Content-Disposition',
        `attachment; filename="zebene.txt"; filename*=UTF-8''${encodeURIComponent('ዘበነ.txt')}`
      );
      res.setHeader('Content-Length', String(combinedBuf.length));
      res.send(combinedBuf);
    } catch (err) {
      const { status, message } = getErrorStatusAndMessage(err);
      res.status(status).json({ error: message });
    }
  });

  const httpServer = http.createServer(app);

  if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production') {
    const { createServer: createViteServer } = await import('vite');
    const vite = await createViteServer({
      server: {
        middlewareMode: true,
        hmr: { server: httpServer },
      },
      appType: 'spa',
    });
    app.use(vite.middlewares);
  } else {
    const distDir = path.resolve(__dirname, 'dist');
    app.use(express.static(distDir));
    app.get('*', (_req: Request, res: Response) => {
      const indexPath = path.join(distDir, 'index.html');
      if (fs.existsSync(indexPath)) {
        res.sendFile(indexPath);
      } else {
        res.status(404).send('Build output not found. Run npm run build first.');
      }
    });
  }

  httpServer.listen(PORT, '0.0.0.0', () => {
    console.log(`National Transport Server running at http://0.0.0.0:${PORT}`);
  });
}

startServer().catch((err) => {
  console.error('Failed to start server:', err);
  process.exit(1);
});


// =============================================================================
// FILE: src/App.tsx
// =============================================================================

/**
 * @license
 * SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
 */

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { onAuthStateChanged, User as FirebaseUser } from 'firebase/auth';
import { Navbar } from './components/Navbar';
import { DashboardOverview } from './components/DashboardOverview';
import { GpsLiveTracking } from './components/GpsLiveTracking';
import { QueueTurnManager } from './components/QueueTurnManager';
import { VehiclesManagement } from './components/VehiclesManagement';
import { DriversManagement } from './components/DriversManagement';
import { OwnersManagement } from './components/OwnersManagement';
import { RoutesTariffs } from './components/RoutesTariffs';
import { TrafficSafetyAndTips } from './components/TrafficSafetyAndTips';
import { VoiceTranslatorIntercom } from './components/VoiceTranslatorIntercom';
import { PaymentsAndSettlements } from './components/PaymentsAndSettlements';
import { ExcelReportsModal } from './components/ExcelReportsModal';
import { SocialShareModal } from './components/SocialShareModal';
import { SmartContractModal } from './components/SmartContractModal';

import {
  Driver,
  Language,
  Owner,
  OwnerSettlement,
  PassengerTicketPayment,
  QueueTurn,
  SafetyReport,
  TerminalExitFee,
  TransportRoute,
  Vehicle,
} from './types';
import { StorageService } from './utils/storage';
import { playEmergencySiren, speakText } from './utils/audio';
import { calculateBearingAndCompass, calculateDistanceKm, findDestinationCoords, resolveExactLocation } from './utils/geoLocator';
import { auth, signInWithGoogle, signOutFromCloud, testConnection } from './firebase';
import {
  buildDriverPayload,
  buildExitFeePayload,
  buildOwnerPayload,
  buildPaymentPayload,
  buildQueuePayload,
  buildReportPayload,
  buildRoutePayload,
  buildSettlementPayload,
  buildVehiclePayload,
  ensureUserProfile,
  seedCloudDatabaseIfEmpty,
  subscribeCloudCollections,
  syncArrayDiffToFirestore,
} from './utils/cloudSync';
import { AlertOctagon, WifiOff, X } from 'lucide-react';

export default function App() {
  const [currentTab, setCurrentTab] = useState<string>('dashboard');
  const [currentLang, setCurrentLang] = useState<Language>('am');
  const [audioAlertsEnabled, setAudioAlertsEnabled] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [isOnline, setIsOnline] = useState<boolean>(typeof navigator !== 'undefined' ? navigator.onLine : true);
  const [cloudUser, setCloudUser] = useState<FirebaseUser | null>(null);

  // Active Role state: user (ተጠቃሚ/መንገደኛ) vs inspector (ተቆጣጣሪ/ባለስልጣን)
  const [activeRole, setActiveRole] = useState<'user' | 'inspector'>('user');

  // Modals state
  const [shareModalOpen, setShareModalOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [contractModalOpen, setContractModalOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [emergencyModalOpen, setEmergencyModalOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);

  // Persistent state collections from StorageService (instant 0ms offline startup + cloud mirror)
  const [vehicles, setVehicles] = useState<Vehicle[]>(() => StorageService.getVehicles());
  const [drivers, setDrivers] = useState<Driver[]>(() => StorageService.getDrivers());
  const [owners, setOwners] = useState<Owner[]>(() => StorageService.getOwners());
  const [queues, setQueues] = useState<QueueTurn[]>(() => StorageService.getQueues());
  const [routes, setRoutes] = useState<TransportRoute[]>(() => StorageService.getRoutes());
  const [safetyReports, setSafetyReports] = useState<SafetyReport[]>(() => StorageService.getReports());
  const [payments, setPayments] = useState<PassengerTicketPayment[]>(() => StorageService.getPayments());
  const [exitFees, setExitFees] = useState<TerminalExitFee[]>(() => StorageService.getExitFees());
  const [settlements, setSettlements] = useState<OwnerSettlement[]>(() => StorageService.getSettlements());

  // Online / Offline connectivity listener
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleOnline = () => setIsOnline(true);
    const handleOffline = () => setIsOnline(false);

    window.addEventListener('online', handleOnline);
    window.addEventListener('offline', handleOffline);

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('online', handleOnline);
      window.removeEventListener('offline', handleOffline);
    };
  }, []);

  // Firebase Cloud Database Connection & Background Realtime Sync
  useEffect(() => {
    void testConnection();

    let unsubscribeCollections: (() => void) | null = null;

    const unsubscribeAuth = onAuthStateChanged(auth, async (user) => {
      if (unsubscribeCollections) {
        unsubscribeCollections();
        unsubscribeCollections = null;
      }

      setCloudUser(user);

      if (user) {
        await ensureUserProfile(user);
        await seedCloudDatabaseIfEmpty(user, {
          vehicles: StorageService.getVehicles(),
          drivers: StorageService.getDrivers(),
          owners: StorageService.getOwners(),
          queues: StorageService.getQueues(),
          routes: StorageService.getRoutes(),
          reports: StorageService.getReports(),
          payments: StorageService.getPayments(),
          exitFees: StorageService.getExitFees(),
          settlements: StorageService.getSettlements(),
        });

        unsubscribeCollections = subscribeCloudCollections(user, {
          onVehicles: (cloudVehicles) => {
            setVehicles((prev) => {
              const prevMap = new Map<string, Vehicle>(prev.map((p) => [p.id, p]));
              const merged = cloudVehicles.map((cv) => ({
                ...cv,
                trail: prevMap.get(cv.id)?.trail || cv.trail,
              }));
              StorageService.saveVehicles(merged);
              return merged;
            });
          },
          onQueues: (items) => {
            setQueues(items);
            StorageService.saveQueues(items);
          },
          onRoutes: (items) => {
            setRoutes(items);
            StorageService.saveRoutes(items);
          },
          onDrivers: (items) => {
            setDrivers(items);
            StorageService.saveDrivers(items);
          },
          onOwners: (items) => {
            setOwners(items);
            StorageService.saveOwners(items);
          },
          onReports: (items) => {
            setSafetyReports(items);
            StorageService.saveReports(items);
          },
          onPayments: (items) => {
            setPayments(items);
            StorageService.savePayments(items);
          },
          onExitFees: (items) => {
            setExitFees(items);
            StorageService.saveExitFees(items);
          },
          onSettlements: (items) => {
            setSettlements(items);
            StorageService.saveSettlements(items);
          },
        });
      }
    });

    return () => {
      unsubscribeAuth();
      if (unsubscribeCollections) {
        unsubscribeCollections();
      }
    };
  }, []);

  const handleCloudSignIn = async () => {
    try {
      await signInWithGoogle();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Google Sign-In error:', error);
    }
  };

  const handleCloudSignOut = async () => {
    try {
      await signOutFromCloud();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Sign-Out error:', error);
    }
  };

  // Update handlers with local persistence + automatic background Firestore cloud sync
  const handleUpdateVehicles = (updated: Vehicle[]) => {
    const prev = vehicles;
    setVehicles(updated);
    StorageService.saveVehicles(updated);
    void syncArrayDiffToFirestore('vehicles', prev, updated, cloudUser, buildVehiclePayload);
  };

  const handleUpdateDrivers = (updated: Driver[]) => {
    const prev = drivers;
    setDrivers(updated);
    StorageService.saveDrivers(updated);
    void syncArrayDiffToFirestore('drivers', prev, updated, cloudUser, buildDriverPayload);
  };

  const handleUpdateOwners = (updated: Owner[]) => {
    const prev = owners;
    setOwners(updated);
    StorageService.saveOwners(updated);
    void syncArrayDiffToFirestore('owners', prev, updated, cloudUser, buildOwnerPayload);
  };

  const handleUpdateQueues = (updated: QueueTurn[]) => {
    const prev = queues;
    setQueues(updated);
    StorageService.saveQueues(updated);
    void syncArrayDiffToFirestore('queues', prev, updated, cloudUser, buildQueuePayload);
  };

  const handleUpdateRoutes = (updated: TransportRoute[]) => {
    const prev = routes;
    setRoutes(updated);
    StorageService.saveRoutes(updated);
    void syncArrayDiffToFirestore('routes', prev, updated, cloudUser, buildRoutePayload);
  };

  const handleUpdateReports = (updated: SafetyReport[]) => {
    const prev = safetyReports;
    setSafetyReports(updated);
    StorageService.saveReports(updated);
    void syncArrayDiffToFirestore('safetyReports', prev, updated, cloudUser, buildReportPayload);
  };

  const handleUpdatePayments = (updated: PassengerTicketPayment[]) => {
    const prev = payments;
    setPayments(updated);
    StorageService.savePayments(updated);
    void syncArrayDiffToFirestore('payments', prev, updated, cloudUser, buildPaymentPayload);
  };

  const handleUpdateExitFees = (updated: TerminalExitFee[]) => {
    const prev = exitFees;
    setExitFees(updated);
    StorageService.saveExitFees(updated);
    void syncArrayDiffToFirestore('exitFees', prev, updated, cloudUser, buildExitFeePayload);
  };

  const handleUpdateSettlements = (updated: OwnerSettlement[]) => {
    const prev = settlements;
    setSettlements(updated);
    StorageService.saveSettlements(updated);
    void syncArrayDiffToFirestore('settlements', prev, updated, cloudUser, buildSettlementPayload);
  };

  // Direct hardware / manual position update for a specific vehicle
  const handleUpdateVehiclePosition = (vehicleId: string, lat: number, lng: number, speedKmh: number) => {
    const nowTime = new Date().toLocaleTimeString('en-GB', {
      hour12: false,
      timeZone: 'Africa/Addis_Ababa',
    });
    const loc = resolveExactLocation(lat, lng);

    setVehicles((prev) => {
      const next = prev.map((v) => {
        if (v.id !== vehicleId) return v;
        const bearing = calculateBearingAndCompass(v.lat, v.lng, lat, lng);
        const nextTrail = [
          ...(v.trail || []),
          {
            lat: Number(lat.toFixed(5)),
            lng: Number(lng.toFixed(5)),
            speed: speedKmh,
            timestamp: nowTime,
            placeName: loc.formattedLabel,
          },
        ].slice(-30);

        return {
          ...v,
          status: 'on_route' as const,
          lat: Number(lat.toFixed(5)),
          lng: Number(lng.toFixed(5)),
          heading: bearing.degrees,
          currentSpeed: speedKmh,
          currentLocationName: loc.formattedLabel,
          currentLocationImage: loc.imageUrl,
          trail: nextTrail,
          lastUpdate: `${nowTime} EAT`,
        };
      });
      StorageService.saveVehicles(next);
      return next;
    });
  };

  // Live 1.5-Second Precision GPS Telemetry Engine
  useEffect(() => {
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      const nowTime = new Date().toLocaleTimeString('en-GB', {
        hour12: false,
        timeZone: 'Africa/Addis_Ababa',
      });

      setVehicles((prev) =>
        prev.map((v) => {
          if (v.status !== 'on_route') return v;

          // Move smoothly along corridor towards destination (or return loop)
          const destPlace = findDestinationCoords(v.destination);
          const distToDest = calculateDistanceKm(v.lat, v.lng, destPlace.lat, destPlace.lng);

          // Step size proportional to vehicle speed (~0.0002 to 0.0008 deg per tick for smooth visual tracking)
          const speedFactor = Math.max(0.3, v.currentSpeed / 60);
          let dLat = 0;
          let dLng = 0;

          if (distToDest > 1.5) {
            const latDiff = destPlace.lat - v.lat;
            const lngDiff = destPlace.lng - v.lng;
            const mag = Math.sqrt(latDiff * latDiff + lngDiff * lngDiff) || 1;
            dLat = (latDiff / mag) * 0.00045 * speedFactor + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.0001;
            dLng = (lngDiff / mag) * 0.00045 * speedFactor + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.0001;
          } else {
            // Reached destination hub -> reverse toward origin hub seamlessly
            const origPlace = findDestinationCoords(v.origin);
            const latDiff = origPlace.lat - v.lat;
            const lngDiff = origPlace.lng - v.lng;
            const mag = Math.sqrt(latDiff * latDiff + lngDiff * lngDiff) || 1;
            dLat = (latDiff / mag) * 0.00045 * speedFactor;
            dLng = (lngDiff / mag) * 0.00045 * speedFactor;
          }

          const nextLat = Number((v.lat + dLat).toFixed(5));
          const nextLng = Number((v.lng + dLng).toFixed(5));

          let newSpeed = v.currentSpeed + Math.floor((Math.random() - 0.48) * 3);
          const minSpd = v.vehicleType === 'bajaj' ? 24 : 38;
          const maxSpd = v.vehicleType === 'bajaj' ? 52 : v.vehicleType === 'ambulance' ? 108 : 98;
          if (newSpeed < minSpd) newSpeed = minSpd;
          if (newSpeed > maxSpd) newSpeed = maxSpd;

          const bearing = calculateBearingAndCompass(v.lat, v.lng, nextLat, nextLng);
          const loc = resolveExactLocation(nextLat, nextLng);

          const nextTrail = [
            ...(v.trail || []),
            {
              lat: nextLat,
              lng: nextLng,
              speed: newSpeed,
              timestamp: nowTime,
              placeName: loc.formattedLabel,
            },
          ].slice(-30);

          return {
            ...v,
            lat: nextLat,
            lng: nextLng,
            heading: bearing.degrees,
            currentSpeed: newSpeed,
            currentLocationName: loc.formattedLabel,
            currentLocationImage: loc.imageUrl,
            trail: nextTrail,
            fuelLevel: Math.max(12, v.fuelLevel - (Math.random() > 0.96 ? 1 : 0)),
            lastUpdate: `${nowTime} EAT`,
          };
        })
      );
    }, 1500);

    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, []);

  const handleTriggerEmergency = () => {
    setEmergencyModalOpen(true);
    if (audioAlertsEnabled) {
      playEmergencySiren();
    }
    speakText('አስቸኳይ ማስጠንቀቂያ! የትራንስፖርት ባለስልጣን የአደጋ እና ደህንነት መቆጣጠሪያ ክፍል ነቅቷል።', 'am');
  };

  const emergencyCount =
    vehicles.filter((v) => v.currentSpeed > v.speedLimit).length +
    safetyReports.filter((r) => r.severity === 'critical' && r.status !== 'resolved').length;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-950 text-slate-100 flex flex-col lg:flex-row selection:bg-emerald-500 selection:text-white">
      {/* Left-Docked Navigation & Dashboard Entry Sidebar */}
      <Navbar
        currentTab={currentTab}
        setCurrentTab={setCurrentTab}
        currentLang={currentLang}
        setCurrentLang={setCurrentLang}
        audioAlertsEnabled={audioAlertsEnabled}
        setAudioAlertsEnabled={setAudioAlertsEnabled}
        emergencyCount={emergencyCount}
        onTriggerEmergency={handleTriggerEmergency}
        activeRole={activeRole}
        onRoleChange={setActiveRole}
        onOpenShareModal={() => setShareModalOpen(true)}
        onOpenContractModal={() => setContractModalOpen(true)}
        cloudUser={cloudUser}
        onCloudSignIn={handleCloudSignIn}
        onCloudSignOut={handleCloudSignOut}
      />

      {/* Right Main Content Column */}
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col min-w-0 min-h-screen">
        {/* Offline Toast Indicator */}
        {!isOnline && (
          <div className="bg-amber-600 text-white text-xs px-4 py-1.5 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 font-medium">
            <WifiOff className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>
              ከኔትወርክ ውጪ ነዎት (Offline Mode) — የተቀመጡ መረጃዎች በስልክዎ/ኮምፒውተርዎ ላይ ያለምንም ችግር እየሰሩ ናቸው።
            </span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Main Applet Content Area */}
        <main className="flex-1 max-w-7xl w-full mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8 py-6">
          {currentTab === 'dashboard' && (
            <DashboardOverview
              vehicles={vehicles}
              queues={queues}
              reports={safetyReports}
              routes={routes}
              currentLang={currentLang}
              onNavigateTab={setCurrentTab}
              audioAlertsEnabled={audioAlertsEnabled}
              activeRole={activeRole}
              onRoleChange={setActiveRole}
              onOpenShareModal={() => setShareModalOpen(true)}
              onOpenContractModal={() => setContractModalOpen(true)}
            />
          )}

          {currentTab === 'liveTracking' && (
            <GpsLiveTracking
              vehicles={vehicles}
              currentLang={currentLang}
              onUpdateVehiclePosition={handleUpdateVehiclePosition}
              audioAlertsEnabled={audioAlertsEnabled}
            />
          )}

          {currentTab === 'queueTurns' && (
            <QueueTurnManager
              queues={queues}
              vehicles={vehicles}
              currentLang={currentLang}
              onUpdateQueue={handleUpdateQueues}
              audioAlertsEnabled={audioAlertsEnabled}
            />
          )}

          {currentTab === 'vehicles' && (
            <VehiclesManagement
              vehicles={vehicles}
              currentLang={currentLang}
              onUpdateVehicles={handleUpdateVehicles}
            />
          )}

          {currentTab === 'drivers' && (
            <DriversManagement
              drivers={drivers}
              currentLang={currentLang}
              onUpdateDrivers={handleUpdateDrivers}
            />
          )}

          {currentTab === 'owners' && (
            <OwnersManagement
              owners={owners}
              currentLang={currentLang}
              onUpdateOwners={handleUpdateOwners}
            />
          )}

          {currentTab === 'routesTariffs' && (
            <RoutesTariffs
              routes={routes}
              currentLang={currentLang}
              onUpdateRoutes={handleUpdateRoutes}
            />
          )}

          {currentTab === 'paymentsSettlements' && (
            <PaymentsAndSettlements
              payments={payments}
              exitFees={exitFees}
              settlements={settlements}
              vehicles={vehicles}
              owners={owners}
              queues={queues}
              currentLang={currentLang}
              onUpdatePayments={handleUpdatePayments}
              onUpdateExitFees={handleUpdateExitFees}
              onUpdateSettlements={handleUpdateSettlements}
              onUpdateQueues={handleUpdateQueues}
              audioAlertsEnabled={audioAlertsEnabled}
            />
          )}

          {currentTab === 'safetyCitizenTips' && (
            <TrafficSafetyAndTips
              reports={safetyReports}
              vehicles={vehicles}
              currentLang={currentLang}
              onUpdateReports={handleUpdateReports}
              audioAlertsEnabled={audioAlertsEnabled}
            />
          )}

          {currentTab === 'voiceTranslator' && (
            <VoiceTranslatorIntercom
              currentLang={currentLang}
              audioAlertsEnabled={audioAlertsEnabled}
            />
          )}

          {currentTab === 'reportsExport' && (
            <ExcelReportsModal
              vehicles={vehicles}
              drivers={drivers}
              queues={queues}
              reports={safetyReports}
              currentLang={currentLang}
            />
          )}
        </main>

        {/* Global Footer */}
        <footer className="border-t border-slate-900 bg-slate-950 py-5 text-center text-xs text-slate-400">
          <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500" />
              <span className="text-slate-300 font-medium">
                የመንገድ እና ትራንስፖርት ባለስልጣን | National Transport & Fleet Command
              </span>
              <span className="text-slate-400">© 2026 PWA Live</span>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-4 text-slate-400 text-[11px]">
              <span>ባጃጅ • ታክሲ • አውቶቡስ • ጭነት</span>
              <span>•</span>
              <span>ክፍያና ርክክብ</span>
              <span>•</span>
              <span>የቀጥታ ጂፒኤስ</span>
              <span>•</span>
              <span>8 ቋንቋዎች</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </footer>
      </div>

      {/* Social Share & Like Modal */}
      <SocialShareModal
        isOpen={shareModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setShareModalOpen(false)}
        title="የመንገድ እና ትራንስፖርት ባለስልጣን | National Transport & Fleet Command"
      />

      {/* Smart Contract Agreement Modal */}
      <SmartContractModal
        isOpen={contractModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setContractModalOpen(false)}
        audioAlertsEnabled={audioAlertsEnabled}
      />

      {/* Emergency SOS Protocol Modal */}
      {emergencyModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="bg-gradient-to-b from-red-950 via-slate-900 to-slate-900 border-2 border-red-500 rounded-3xl max-w-lg w-full p-6 text-white shadow-2xl space-y-5 animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95">
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between border-b border-red-800/80 pb-3">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="p-3 bg-red-600 rounded-2xl animate-bounce shadow-lg shadow-red-950">
                  <AlertOctagon className="w-7 h-7 text-white" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h3 className="font-extrabold text-lg text-white">አስቸኳይ የትራፊክ ደህንነት ማስጠንቀቂያ (SOS)</h3>
                  <p className="text-xs text-red-300">National Road Safety Emergency Hotline 991 / 907</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => setEmergencyModalOpen(false)}
                className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-white rounded-lg"
              >
                <X className="w-5 h-5" />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-3 text-xs leading-relaxed text-slate-200">
              <div className="p-3 bg-red-900/30 border border-red-600/40 rounded-xl space-y-1">
                <span className="font-bold text-red-300 block">የተፈጠረው ማንቂያ:</span>
                <p>
                  በአገር አቀፍ አውራ ጎዳናዎች ላይ የፍጥነት ወሰን ጥሰት ወይም አደጋ ተከስቷል። በአቅራቢያ የሚገኙ የትራፊክ ፖሊስ ኬላዎች እና አምቡላንሶች በተጠንቀቅ እንዲቆሙ መልዕክት ተሰራጭቷል።
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-center">
                <div className="bg-slate-800 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-700">
                  <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 uppercase block">የትራፊክ ፖሊስ ፈጣን ጥሪ</span>
                  <span className="font-mono font-extrabold text-amber-400 text-lg">991</span>
                </div>
                <div className="bg-slate-800 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-700">
                  <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 uppercase block">አምቡላንስ / ቀይ መስቀል</span>
                  <span className="font-mono font-extrabold text-red-400 text-lg">907</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-800">
              <button
                onClick={() => setEmergencyModalOpen(false)}
                className="px-5 py-2 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 font-bold rounded-xl text-xs"
              >
                ማንቂያውን አጥፋ (Dismiss)
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  setCurrentTab('safetyCitizenTips');
                  setEmergencyModalOpen(false);
                }}
                className="px-5 py-2 bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-500 text-white font-bold rounded-xl text-xs shadow-lg"
              >
                ወደ ደህንነት ማዕከል ሂድ ➔
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


// =============================================================================
// FILE: src/components/DashboardOverview.tsx
// =============================================================================

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { 
  Activity, 
  Truck, 
  Users, 
  ShieldAlert, 
  Clock, 
  AlertTriangle, 
  ArrowUpRight,
  CheckCircle2,
  CreditCard,
  Search,
  Navigation,
  FileCheck2,
  PhoneCall,
  Volume2,
  Ticket,
  ChevronRight,
  ShieldCheck
} from 'lucide-react';
import { Language, QueueTurn, SafetyReport, TransportRoute, Vehicle } from '../types';
import { translations } from '../data/translations';
import { speakText } from '../utils/audio';

interface DashboardOverviewProps {
  vehicles: Vehicle[];
  queues: QueueTurn[];
  reports: SafetyReport[];
  routes?: TransportRoute[];
  currentLang: Language;
  onNavigateTab: (tab: string) => void;
  audioAlertsEnabled: boolean;
  activeRole?: 'user' | 'inspector';
  onRoleChange?: (role: 'user' | 'inspector') => void;
  onOpenShareModal?: () => void;
  onOpenContractModal?: () => void;
}

export const DashboardOverview: React.FC<DashboardOverviewProps> = ({
  vehicles,
  queues,
  reports,
  routes = [],
  currentLang,
  onNavigateTab,
  audioAlertsEnabled,
  activeRole = 'user',
  onRoleChange,
  onOpenShareModal,
  onOpenContractModal,
}) => {
  const t = translations[currentLang];
  const [internalRole, setInternalRole] = useState<'user' | 'inspector'>(activeRole);

  const currentRole = onRoleChange ? activeRole : internalRole;
  const setRole = (role: 'user' | 'inspector') => {
    setInternalRole(role);
    if (onRoleChange) onRoleChange(role);
  };

  // Passenger Journey Finder State (unrestricted origin & destination)
  const [searchOrigin, setSearchOrigin] = useState<string>('');
  const [searchDestination, setSearchDestination] = useState<string>('');
  const [searchResult, setSearchResult] = useState<{
    found: boolean;
    route?: string;
    fare?: number;
    distance?: string;
    availableVehicles?: number;
  } | null>(null);

  const handleSearchJourney = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!searchOrigin.trim() && !searchDestination.trim()) return;

    const origLower = searchOrigin.trim().toLowerCase();
    const destLower = searchDestination.trim().toLowerCase();

    const matchingRoute = routes.find((r) => {
      const rOrig = r.origin.toLowerCase();
      const rDest = r.destination.toLowerCase();
      const origMatch = !origLower || rOrig.includes(origLower) || origLower.includes(rOrig);
      const destMatch = !destLower || rDest.includes(destLower) || destLower.includes(rDest);
      return origMatch && destMatch;
    });

    const matchingQueues = queues.filter(
      (q) =>
        (!origLower || q.routeName.toLowerCase().includes(origLower)) &&
        (!destLower || q.routeName.toLowerCase().includes(destLower))
    );

    const matchingVehicles = vehicles.filter(
      (v) =>
        (v.status === 'in_queue' || v.status === 'on_route') &&
        (!origLower || (v.origin ?? '').toLowerCase().includes(origLower)) &&
        (!destLower || (v.destination ?? '').toLowerCase().includes(destLower))
    );

    const activeVehiclesTotal = vehicles.filter(
      (v) => v.status === 'in_queue' || v.status === 'on_route'
    ).length;

    const routeLabel = matchingRoute
      ? `${matchingRoute.origin} ➔ ${matchingRoute.destination}`
      : `${searchOrigin.trim() || 'መነሻ'} ➔ ${searchDestination.trim() || 'መድረሻ'}`;

    const fareAmount = matchingRoute
      ? matchingRoute.standardTariffBirr
      : matchingQueues[0]?.tariffPerSeat || 180;
    const distanceLabel = matchingRoute ? `${matchingRoute.distanceKm} ኪ.ሜ` : 'ክፍት መስመር';
    const availableCount =
      matchingQueues.length > 0 || matchingVehicles.length > 0
        ? Math.max(matchingQueues.length, matchingVehicles.length)
        : Math.max(1, activeVehiclesTotal);

    setSearchResult({
      found: true,
      route: routeLabel,
      fare: fareAmount,
      distance: distanceLabel,
      availableVehicles: availableCount,
    });

    if (audioAlertsEnabled) {
      speakText(`${routeLabel} የጉዞ መስመር ተገኝቷል`, 'am');
    }
  };

  // Metrics calculations
  const onRouteCount = vehicles.filter((v) => v.status === 'on_route').length;
  const inQueueCount = vehicles.filter((v) => v.status === 'in_queue').length;
  const overspeedCount = vehicles.filter((v) => v.currentSpeed > v.speedLimit).length;
  const criticalReports = reports.filter((r) => r.status === 'pending' || r.status === 'under_investigation').length;
  const todayPassengers = queues.reduce((acc, q) => acc + q.bookedSeats, 0) + 1420;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Role Switcher Tabs Header - Left-aligned, clean right side */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start justify-start gap-4 bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-800 p-2 sm:p-3 rounded-2xl shadow-xl backdrop-blur-md">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 p-1 bg-slate-950/80 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 w-full sm:w-auto">
          <button
            onClick={() => setRole('user')}
            className={`flex-1 sm:flex-none flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-all ${
              currentRole === 'user'
                ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-600 to-teal-600 text-white shadow-lg shadow-emerald-950/50 ring-1 ring-emerald-400/40'
                : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-800/60'
            }`}
          >
            <Users className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>የተጠቃሚና መንገደኛ ዳሽቦርድ (Passenger/Citizen)</span>
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={() => setRole('inspector')}
            className={`flex-1 sm:flex-none flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-all ${
              currentRole === 'inspector'
                ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-600 to-blue-700 text-white shadow-lg shadow-cyan-950/50 ring-1 ring-cyan-400/40'
                : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-800/60'
            }`}
          >
            <ShieldAlert className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>የተቆጣጣሪና ባለስልጣን ዳሽቦርድ (Inspector/Command)</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* =========================================================================
          VIEW 1: USER / CITIZEN DASHBOARD (የተጠቃሚና የመንገደኛ ዳሽቦርድ)
         ========================================================================= */}
      {currentRole === 'user' && (
        <div className="space-y-6 animate-in fade-in duration-300">
          {/* Clean User Welcome Banner (Right side clean) */}
          <div className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-900 via-emerald-950/40 to-slate-900 border border-emerald-500/30 p-6 sm:p-7 shadow-2xl">
            <div className="relative z-10 flex flex-col items-start justify-start gap-2">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 animate-pulse" />
                <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-400 uppercase tracking-wider">
                  የተጠቃሚና የመንገደኛ አገልግሎት ማዕከል (Citizen Portal)
                </span>
              </div>
              <h1 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-extrabold text-white">
                የትራንስፖርት ጉዞዎን ያቅዱ፣ ተራና ቲኬት ይከታተሉ
              </h1>
              <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-300">
                ከባጃጅ እስከ አገር አቋራጭ አውቶቡስ ያሉ ተሽከርካሪዎችን መነሻና መዳረሻ በየትኛውም ቦታ ይፈልጉ::
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Unrestricted Journey Finder Widget (መነሻና መዳረሻ ያለገደብ መፈለጊያ) */}
          <div className="bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-800 rounded-3xl p-5 sm:p-6 shadow-xl">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4 border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                <div className="p-2 bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400 rounded-xl">
                  <Navigation className="w-5 h-5" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h2 className="font-bold text-white text-sm sm:text-base">
                    የጉዞ መፈለጊያ እና የታሪፍ ማረጋገጫ (Trip & Tariff Finder)
                  </h2>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                    ያለገደብ በየትኛውም ከተማ፣ ወረዳ ወይም ገጠር መነሻና መዳረሻ ያስገቡ
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <span className="text-xs font-mono bg-slate-800 text-emerald-300 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full border border-slate-700">
                100% ያለገደብ ክፍት
              </span>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleSearchJourney} className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-12 gap-3">
              <div className="sm:col-span-4">
                <label className="block text-xs text-slate-400 mb-1 font-semibold">መነሻ ቦታ (Origin)</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={searchOrigin}
                  onChange={(e) => setSearchOrigin(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="ምሳሌ፡ አዲስ አበባ / መርካቶ / ጎጃም..."
                  className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="sm:col-span-4">
                <label className="block text-xs text-slate-400 mb-1 font-semibold">መድረሻ ቦታ (Destination)</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={searchDestination}
                  onChange={(e) => setSearchDestination(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="ምሳሌ፡ ሀዋሳ / ባህር ዳር / ጅማ / ኬላ..."
                  className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="sm:col-span-4 flex items-end">
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="w-full py-2.5 px-4 bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-600 to-teal-600 hover:from-emerald-500 hover:to-teal-500 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-lg transition-transform active:scale-95 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
                >
                  <Search className="w-4 h-4" />
                  <span>ተሽከርካሪና ታሪፍ ፈልግ</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>

            {/* Journey Search Result Box */}
            {searchResult && (
              <div className="mt-4 p-4 bg-emerald-950/30 border border-emerald-500/40 rounded-2xl flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4 animate-in fade-in">
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="font-extrabold text-sm text-emerald-300">{searchResult.route}</span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-mono">
                      {searchResult.distance}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-300">
                    ህጋዊ የትራንስፖርት ታሪፍ፡ <strong className="text-amber-400 font-mono text-sm">{searchResult.fare} ብር</strong> | በቅርብ የሚገኙ ተሽከርካሪዎች፡ <strong className="text-white font-mono">{searchResult.availableVehicles}</strong>
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
                  <button
                    onClick={() => onNavigateTab('paymentsSettlements')}
                    className="px-3.5 py-2 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold shadow flex items-center gap-1.5"
                  >
                    <Ticket className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    <span>የቲኬት ክፍያ ፈጽም</span>
                  </button>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => onNavigateTab('liveTracking')}
                    className="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold border border-slate-700"
                  >
                    በካርታ እይ ➔
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Passenger Live Terminal Board & Vehicle Types */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-6">
            {/* Terminal Live Board */}
            <div className="lg:col-span-7 bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-800 rounded-3xl p-5 shadow-xl space-y-4">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Clock className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-400" />
                  <h3 className="font-bold text-white text-sm">
                    የመናኸሪያዎች የቀጥታ መነሻ ሰሌዳ (Active Departures)
                  </h3>
                </div>
                <button
                  onClick={() => onNavigateTab('queueTurns')}
                  className="text-xs text-emerald-400 hover:text-emerald-300 font-semibold"
                >
                  ተጨማሪ ተራዎችን እይ ➔
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-2.5">
                {queues.slice(0, 4).map((q) => (
                  <div
                    key={q.id}
                    className="bg-slate-800/80 rounded-2xl p-3.5 border border-slate-700/70 flex items-center justify-between text-xs hover:border-emerald-500/40 transition-colors"
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      <span className="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 text-amber-400 font-mono font-bold flex items-center justify-center border border-slate-700 text-xs">
                        #{q.turnNumber}
                      </span>
                      <div>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                          <span className="font-mono font-bold text-white">{q.plateNumber}</span>
                          <span className="text-[10px] bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">
                            {q.vehicleType}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <p className="text-slate-400 text-xs mt-0.5">{q.routeName}</p>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="text-right space-y-1">
                      <span
                        className={`inline-block px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-semibold border ${
                          q.status === 'boarding'
                            ? 'bg-cyan-500/20 text-cyan-300 border-cyan-500/40'
                            : q.status === 'waiting'
                            ? 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border-amber-500/40'
                            : 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-500/40'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {q.status === 'boarding' ? 'በመጫን ላይ' : q.status === 'waiting' ? 'በመጠበቅ ላይ' : 'ተሸኝቷል'}
                      </span>
                      <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-mono">
                        ክፍት ወንበር፡ {Math.max(0, q.capacity - q.bookedSeats)}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Quick Vehicle Type Directory & Passenger Tools */}
            <div className="lg:col-span-5 space-y-4">
              <div className="bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-800 rounded-3xl p-5 shadow-xl space-y-3.5">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
                  <h3 className="font-bold text-white text-sm flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Truck className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400" />
                    <span>ሁሉንም ያካተተ የተሽከርካሪ መደቦች</span>
                  </h3>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => onNavigateTab('vehicles')}
                    className="text-xs text-emerald-400 hover:text-emerald-300 font-bold"
                  >
                    ሁሉንም ስሞች እይ ➔
                  </button>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-xs">
                  <div
                    onClick={() => onNavigateTab('vehicles')}
                    className="p-2.5 bg-slate-800/80 hover:border-emerald-500/50 cursor-pointer rounded-2xl border border-slate-700 flex items-center gap-2.5 transition-colors"
                  >
                    <span className="text-xl">🛺</span>
                    <div>
                      <strong className="text-white block text-xs">ባጃጅ / ሞተርሳይክል</strong>
                      <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">Bajaj RE • TVS • Boxer</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div
                    onClick={() => onNavigateTab('vehicles')}
                    className="p-2.5 bg-slate-800/80 hover:border-emerald-500/50 cursor-pointer rounded-2xl border border-slate-700 flex items-center gap-2.5 transition-colors"
                  >
                    <span className="text-xl">🚐</span>
                    <div>
                      <strong className="text-white block text-xs">ዳማስ / ሚኒባስ / EV</strong>
                      <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">HiAce • BYD EV • Corolla</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div
                    onClick={() => onNavigateTab('vehicles')}
                    className="p-2.5 bg-slate-800/80 hover:border-emerald-500/50 cursor-pointer rounded-2xl border border-slate-700 flex items-center gap-2.5 transition-colors"
                  >
                    <span className="text-xl">🚌</span>
                    <div>
                      <strong className="text-white block text-xs">አውቶቡስ / ቪአይፒ / አንበሳ</strong>
                      <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">Yutong • Higer • Scania</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div
                    onClick={() => onNavigateTab('vehicles')}
                    className="p-2.5 bg-slate-800/80 hover:border-emerald-500/50 cursor-pointer rounded-2xl border border-slate-700 flex items-center gap-2.5 transition-colors"
                  >
                    <span className="text-xl">🚛</span>
                    <div>
                      <strong className="text-white block text-xs">ጭነት / ቦቴ / አምቡላንስ</strong>
                      <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">Sinotruk • Isuzu • Volvo</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Direct Help Shortcuts */}
                <div className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-slate-300">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <PhoneCall className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400" />
                    <span>ነጻ የጥቆማ መስመር፡ <strong className="text-amber-400 font-mono">991</strong></span>
                  </div>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => onNavigateTab('voiceTranslator')}
                    className="text-xs text-cyan-400 hover:text-cyan-300 font-semibold"
                  >
                    ባለብዙ ቋንቋ ተርጓሚ ➔
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Digital Payment / Settlement Quick Card */}
              <div 
                onClick={() => onNavigateTab('paymentsSettlements')}
                className="bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-950/40 via-slate-900 to-cyan-950/40 border border-emerald-500/30 rounded-3xl p-4 shadow-lg cursor-pointer hover:border-emerald-400/50 transition-all group"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div className="p-2.5 bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400 rounded-xl group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform">
                      <CreditCard className="w-5 h-5" />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <h4 className="font-bold text-white text-xs">ዲጂታል የትራንስፖርት ክፍያና ርክክብ</h4>
                      <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">የቴሌብርና ሲቢኢ የቲኬት ክፍያ ፈጽም ወይም የርክክብ ደረሰኝ እይ</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400 group-hover:translate-x-1 transition-transform" />
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* =========================================================================
          VIEW 2: INSPECTOR & AUTHORITY COMMAND DASHBOARD (የተቆጣጣሪና ባለስልጣን ዳሽቦርድ)
         ========================================================================= */}
      {currentRole === 'inspector' && (
        <div className="space-y-6 animate-in fade-in duration-300">
          {/* Authority Command Header (Right side clean) */}
          <div className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-950 via-cyan-950/50 to-slate-950 border border-cyan-500/40 p-6 sm:p-7 shadow-2xl">
            <div className="relative z-10 flex flex-col items-start justify-start gap-2">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-cyan-400 animate-ping" />
                <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-cyan-400 uppercase tracking-wider">
                  የመንገድና ትራንስፖርት ባለስልጣን የቁጥጥርና ትእዛዝ ክፍል (Authority Command)
                </span>
              </div>
              <h1 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-extrabold text-white">
                የአገር አቀፍ የፍሊት፣ የኬላና የተራ የቀጥታ ቁጥጥር
              </h1>
              <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-300">
                የፍጥነት ወሰን ጥሰት፣ የኬላ መውጫ ክፍያ፣ የደህንነት ጥቆማዎችና ህጋዊ የውል ስምምነት ክትትል::
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Primary Inspector KPI Metrics Grid */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
            {/* Metric 1: Total Fleet */}
            <div
              onClick={() => onNavigateTab('vehicles')}
              className="bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-800 rounded-3xl p-5 shadow-lg hover:border-cyan-500/50 cursor-pointer transition-all group"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-400 mb-2">
                <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider">{t.totalVehicles}</span>
                <div className="p-2.5 bg-cyan-500/10 text-cyan-400 rounded-2xl group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform">
                  <Truck className="w-5 h-5" />
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-baseline gap-2">
                <span className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-white font-mono">{vehicles.length}</span>
                <span className="text-xs text-cyan-400 font-semibold flex items-center">
                  <ArrowUpRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> 100% በጂፒኤስ ቁጥጥር ስር
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-2">ከባጃጅ እስከ አገር አቋራጭ</p>
            </div>

            {/* Metric 2: Active on Road */}
            <div
              onClick={() => onNavigateTab('liveTracking')}
              className="bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-800 rounded-3xl p-5 shadow-lg hover:border-emerald-500/50 cursor-pointer transition-all group"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-400 mb-2">
                <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider">{t.activeOnRoute}</span>
                <div className="p-2.5 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 rounded-2xl group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform">
                  <Activity className="w-5 h-5 animate-pulse" />
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-baseline gap-2">
                <span className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-emerald-400 font-mono">{onRouteCount}</span>
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">በጉዞ ላይ</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-2">በቀጥታ ሳተላይት ክትትል</p>
            </div>

            {/* Metric 3: Safety Violations */}
            <div
              onClick={() => onNavigateTab('safetyCitizenTips')}
              className={`rounded-3xl p-5 shadow-lg cursor-pointer transition-all border ${
                overspeedCount > 0
                  ? 'bg-red-950/40 border-red-500/80 hover:border-red-400 shadow-red-950/50 animate-pulse'
                  : 'bg-slate-900/90 border-slate-800 hover:border-slate-700'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-400 mb-2">
                <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider">የፍጥነት ወሰን ጥሰት</span>
                <div className="p-2.5 bg-red-500/20 text-red-400 rounded-2xl">
                  <AlertTriangle className="w-5 h-5 animate-bounce" />
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-baseline gap-2">
                <span className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-red-400 font-mono">{overspeedCount}</span>
                <span className="text-xs text-red-300 font-semibold">ተሽከርካሪዎች</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-2">
                {criticalReports} አዳዲስ የተጠቃሚ ጥቆማዎች
              </p>
            </div>

            {/* Metric 4: Queue Flow */}
            <div
              onClick={() => onNavigateTab('queueTurns')}
              className="bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-800 rounded-3xl p-5 shadow-lg hover:border-purple-500/50 cursor-pointer transition-all group"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-400 mb-2">
                <span className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider">የመናኸሪያ ተራ ሁኔታ</span>
                <div className="p-2.5 bg-purple-500/10 text-purple-400 rounded-2xl group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform">
                  <Clock className="w-5 h-5" />
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-baseline gap-2">
                <span className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-purple-400 font-mono">{inQueueCount}</span>
                <span className="text-xs text-purple-300 font-semibold">በተራ መጠበቂያ</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-2">{todayPassengers} ዛሬ የተጓጓዙ ተሳፋሪዎች</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Inspector Controls Grid: Queue Dispatch & Smart Contract Bilateral Summary */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-6">
            {/* Live Queue Dispatch Control */}
            <div className="lg:col-span-7 bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-800 rounded-3xl p-5 shadow-xl space-y-4">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Clock className="w-5 h-5 text-cyan-400" />
                  <h3 className="font-bold text-white text-sm">
                    የተቆጣጣሪ የተራ ጥሪ እና የፍተሻ ሰሌዳ (Queue Dispatcher)
                  </h3>
                </div>
                <button
                  onClick={() => onNavigateTab('queueTurns')}
                  className="text-xs text-cyan-400 hover:text-cyan-300 font-semibold"
                >
                  ተራ አስተዳድር ➔
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-2.5">
                {queues.slice(0, 4).map((q) => (
                  <div
                    key={q.id}
                    className="bg-slate-800/80 rounded-2xl p-3 border border-slate-700 flex items-center justify-between text-xs"
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      <span className="w-8 h-8 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 text-amber-400 font-mono font-bold flex items-center justify-center border border-slate-700">
                        #{q.turnNumber}
                      </span>
                      <div>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                          <strong className="text-white font-mono">{q.plateNumber}</strong>
                          <span className="text-[10px] bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded">
                            {q.driverName}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <p className="text-slate-400 text-xs mt-0.5">{q.routeName}</p>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <button
                        onClick={() => {
                          if (audioAlertsEnabled) {
                            speakText(`ተራ ቁጥር ${q.turnNumber} ታርጋ ${q.plateNumber} ወደ መጫኛ መድረክ ይግቡ!`, 'am');
                          }
                        }}
                        className="px-2.5 py-1.5 bg-cyan-600/30 hover:bg-cyan-600 text-cyan-200 hover:text-white rounded-lg border border-cyan-500/40 text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1 transition-colors"
                        title="ተራ ጥሪ አሰማ"
                      >
                        <Volume2 className="w-3 h-3" />
                        <span>ጥሪ አሰማ</span>
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Smart Contract & Checkpoint Readiness Card */}
            <div className="lg:col-span-5 space-y-4">
              {/* Smart Contract Card */}
              <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-900 via-amber-950/30 to-slate-900 border border-amber-500/40 rounded-3xl p-5 shadow-xl space-y-3">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-amber-900/50 pb-2.5">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <ShieldCheck className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-400" />
                    <h4 className="font-bold text-white text-xs">ስማርት የውል ስምምነት (Active Contract)</h4>
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-[10px] bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/40 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-bold">
                    የጸደቀ / 100% ህጋዊ
                  </span>
                </div>

                <div className="text-xs text-slate-300 space-y-2 leading-relaxed">
                  <div className="flex justify-between border-b border-slate-800 pb-1.5 text-xs">
                    <span className="text-slate-400">ገንቢ / አርክቴክት፡</span>
                    <span className="font-semibold text-white">Senior Software Engineering Architect</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex justify-between border-b border-slate-800 pb-1.5 text-xs">
                    <span className="text-slate-400">ተቋም / ባለስልጣን፡</span>
                    <span className="font-semibold text-white">አቶ ዘበነ አስፋው (ዋና ዳይሬክተር)</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex justify-between text-xs">
                    <span className="text-slate-400">የውል መጠንና ዋስትና፡</span>
                    <span className="font-bold text-amber-400 font-mono">3,850,000 ብር (24 ወራት)</span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {onOpenContractModal && (
                  <button
                    onClick={onOpenContractModal}
                    className="w-full py-2 bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-500 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
                  >
                    <FileCheck2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    <span>ሙሉውን የውል ስምምነት ሰነድ ክፈት</span>
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* National Checkpoints Status */}
              <div className="bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-800 rounded-3xl p-4 shadow-xl text-xs space-y-2">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-emerald-400 font-bold">
                  <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                  <span>የአገር አቀፍ የፍተሻ ኬላዎች ዝግጁነት</span>
                </div>
                <p className="text-slate-400 text-[11px] leading-relaxed">
                  ሞጆ፣ አዋሽ፣ ቆቃ፣ ደብረ ብርሃን እና ደጀን የፍተሻ ኬላዎች ከሳተላይት መቆጣጠሪያው ጋር በተገናኘ መስመር እየሰሩ ናቸው።
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};


// =============================================================================
// FILE: src/components/DriversManagement.tsx
// =============================================================================

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { 
  Users, 
  Search, 
  Plus, 
  FileSpreadsheet, 
  Star, 
  AlertTriangle, 
  Phone, 
  ShieldCheck, 
  Award,
  Trash2,
  X
} from 'lucide-react';
import { Driver, Language } from '../types';
import { translations } from '../data/translations';
import { exportDriversToCSV } from '../utils/exportHelpers';

interface DriversManagementProps {
  drivers: Driver[];
  currentLang: Language;
  onUpdateDrivers: (drivers: Driver[]) => void;
}

export const DriversManagement: React.FC<DriversManagementProps> = ({
  drivers,
  currentLang,
  onUpdateDrivers,
}) => {
  const t = translations[currentLang];
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [filterGrade, setFilterGrade] = useState('all');
  const [filterStatus, setFilterStatus] = useState('all');
  const [isAddModalOpen, setIsAddModalOpen] = useState(false);

  // New driver form
  const [newName, setNewName] = useState('');
  const [newLicNum, setNewLicNum] = useState(`ETH-LIC-${Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000)}`);
  const [newGrade, setNewGrade] = useState('ደረጃ 4 (ህዝብ 1)');
  const [customGradeInput, setCustomGradeInput] = useState('');
  const [newPhone, setNewPhone] = useState('+251 9');
  const [newExp, setNewExp] = useState(5);
  const [newPlate, setNewPlate] = useState('1-A10482 አ.አ');

  const filteredDrivers = drivers.filter((d) => {
    const matchesSearch =
      d.fullName.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      d.licenseNumber.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      d.licenseGrade.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      d.phone.includes(searchQuery) ||
      d.assignedPlate.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase());

    const matchesGrade = filterGrade === 'all' || d.licenseGrade.toLowerCase().includes(filterGrade.toLowerCase());
    const matchesStatus = filterStatus === 'all' || d.status === filterStatus;

    return matchesSearch && matchesGrade && matchesStatus;
  });

  const handleCreateDriver = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!newName) return;

    const finalGrade = customGradeInput.trim() ? customGradeInput.trim() : newGrade;

    const newDriver: Driver = {
      id: `drv_${Date.now()}`,
      fullName: newName,
      licenseNumber: newLicNum,
      licenseGrade: finalGrade,
      phone: newPhone,
      experienceYears: Number(newExp),
      rating: 4.9,
      status: 'active',
      penaltyPoints: 0,
      assignedPlate: newPlate,
      avatarUrl: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1507003211169-0a1dd7228f2d?auto=format&fit=crop&w=150&q=80',
      healthCertExpiry: '2027-12-30',
    };

    onUpdateDrivers([newDriver, ...drivers]);
    setIsAddModalOpen(false);

    // Reset
    setNewName('');
    setCustomGradeInput('');
  };

  const handleToggleSuspend = (driverId: string) => {
    const updated = drivers.map((d) => {
      if (d.id === driverId) {
        return {
          ...d,
          status: d.status === 'suspended' ? ('active' as const) : ('suspended' as const),
        };
      }
      return d;
    });
    onUpdateDrivers(updated);
  };

  const handleDeleteDriver = (driverId: string) => {
    if (confirm('አሽከርካሪውን ከስርዓቱ መሰረዝ ይፈልጋሉ?')) {
      onUpdateDrivers(drivers.filter((d) => d.id !== driverId));
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Banner */}
      <div className="bg-slate-800/90 border border-slate-700/80 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 shadow-lg flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-2">
            <Users className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-400" />
            <span>{t.drivers} (Drivers Registry - Unlimited License Categories)</span>
          </h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
            ከሞተርና ባጃጅ ጀምሮ እስከ ህዝብ 1፣ 2፣ ከባድ ጭነትና ዓለም አቀፍ የመንጃ ፍቃድ ያለገደብ የሚመዘገብበት
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          {/* Export to Excel */}
          <button
            onClick={() => exportDriversToCSV(drivers)}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 bg-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-600 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold shadow transition-all active:scale-95"
          >
            <FileSpreadsheet className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400" />
            <span>{t.exportExcel}</span>
          </button>

          {/* Add Driver Button */}
          <button
            onClick={() => setIsAddModalOpen(true)}
