            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-600 to-teal-700 hover:from-emerald-500 hover:to-teal-600 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold shadow-lg shadow-emerald-950/50 transition-all active:scale-95"
          >
            <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>{t.addNew}</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Filter and Search */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-12 gap-3 bg-slate-800/80 p-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-700/80">
        <div className="sm:col-span-6 relative">
          <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3 top-3" />
          <input
            type="text"
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="በስም፣ ደረጃ (ሞተር/ባጃጅ/ህዝብ)፣ ፍቃድ ቁጥር ወይም በስልክ ፈልግ..."
            className="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 text-white text-xs rounded-xl pl-9 pr-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-emerald-500"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="sm:col-span-3">
          <select
            value={filterGrade}
            onChange={(e) => setFilterGrade(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 text-slate-200 text-xs rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none"
          >
            <option value="all">ሁሉም የመንጃ ፍቃድ ደረጃዎች</option>
            <option value="ባጃጅ">ሞተር / ባጃጅ ፍቃድ</option>
            <option value="ደረጃ 3">ደረጃ 3 (ታክሲ/ሚኒባስ)</option>
            <option value="ደረጃ 4">ደረጃ 4 (ህዝብ 1)</option>
            <option value="ደረጃ 5">ደረጃ 5 (ህዝብ 2)</option>
            <option value="ጭነት">የከባድ ጭነት</option>
            <option value="አለም አቀፍ">ዓለም አቀፍ (International)</option>
          </select>
        </div>

        <div className="sm:col-span-3">
          <select
            value={filterStatus}
            onChange={(e) => setFilterStatus(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 text-slate-200 text-xs rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none"
          >
            <option value="all">ሁሉም ሁኔታዎች</option>
            <option value="active">ዝግጁ / ንቁ</option>
            <option value="on_route">በጉዞ ላይ</option>
            <option value="suspended">የታገደ</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Driver Cards Grid */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        {filteredDrivers.map((driver) => (
          <div
            key={driver.id}
            className="bg-slate-800/90 border border-slate-700/80 rounded-2xl p-4 shadow-lg hover:border-slate-600 transition-all flex flex-col justify-between"
          >
            <div>
              {/* Header with avatar & info */}
              <div className="flex items-start gap-3 mb-3">
                <img
                  src={driver.avatarUrl}
                  alt={driver.fullName}
                  className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl object-cover border-2 border-slate-700 shrink-0"
                />
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <h3 className="font-bold text-white text-sm truncate">{driver.fullName}</h3>
                    <span
                      className={`text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border ${
                        driver.status === 'suspended'
                          ? 'bg-red-500/20 text-red-300 border-red-500/40'
                          : driver.status === 'on_route'
                          ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-500/40'
                          : 'bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-300 border-blue-500/40'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {driver.status === 'suspended' ? 'የታገደ' : driver.status === 'on_route' ? 'በጉዞ ላይ' : 'ዝግጁ'}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-emerald-400 font-bold">{driver.licenseGrade}</p>
                  <p className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400">{driver.licenseNumber}</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Stats & Assignment */}
              <div className="bg-slate-900/80 rounded-xl p-3 border border-slate-750 space-y-2 text-xs">
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-slate-300">
                  <span className="text-slate-400">የተመደበለት ተሽከርካሪ:</span>
                  <span className="font-mono font-bold text-amber-300 bg-slate-800 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                    {driver.assignedPlate}
                  </span>
                </div>

                <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-slate-300">
                  <span className="text-slate-400">ደህንነት ደረጃ (Rating):</span>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-amber-400 font-bold">
                    <Star className="w-3.5 h-3.5 fill-amber-400" />
                    <span>{driver.rating} / 5</span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-slate-300">
                  <span className="text-slate-400">የቅጣት ነጥብ (Penalties):</span>
                  <span
                    className={`font-mono font-bold ${
                      driver.penaltyPoints >= 10
                        ? 'text-red-400 animate-pulse'
                        : driver.penaltyPoints > 0
                        ? 'text-amber-400'
                        : 'text-emerald-400'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {driver.penaltyPoints} ነጥብ
                  </span>
                </div>

                <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-slate-300 pt-1 border-t border-slate-800">
                  <span className="text-slate-400 flex items-center gap-1">
                    <Phone className="w-3 h-3 text-slate-500" />
                    <span>ስልክ:</span>
                  </span>
                  <span className="font-mono text-slate-200">{driver.phone}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Actions footer */}
            <div className="mt-4 pt-3 border-t border-slate-700/60 flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-mono">
                ልምድ: {driver.experienceYears} ዓመት
              </span>

              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <button
                  onClick={() => handleToggleSuspend(driver.id)}
                  className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-colors ${
                    driver.status === 'suspended'
                      ? 'bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white'
                      : 'bg-red-600/30 hover:bg-red-600 text-red-300 hover:text-white border border-red-500/40'
                  }`}
                >
                  {driver.status === 'suspended' ? 'ዕገዳ አንሳ' : 'አግድ'}
                </button>

                <button
                  onClick={() => handleDeleteDriver(driver.id)}
                  className="p-1.5 hover:bg-red-900/40 text-slate-400 hover:text-red-400 rounded-lg transition-colors"
                  title="አስወግድ"
                >
                  <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Add Driver Modal */}
      {isAddModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/75 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-3xl max-w-lg w-full p-6 text-white shadow-2xl space-y-4 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Users className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-400" />
                <h3 className="font-bold text-base">አዲስ አሽከርካሪ መዝግብ (ያለደረጃ ገደብ)</h3>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => setIsAddModalOpen(false)}
                className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-white rounded-lg"
              >
                <X className="w-5 h-5" />
              </button>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleCreateDriver} className="space-y-4 text-xs">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-slate-300 font-medium mb-1">ሙሉ ስም (Full Name) *</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  placeholder="የአሽከርካሪው ሙሉ ስም"
                  value={newName}
                  onChange={(e) => setNewName(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-emerald-500"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-slate-300 font-medium mb-1">የመንጃ ፍቃድ ቁጥር</label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={newLicNum}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewLicNum(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none font-mono"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-slate-300 font-medium mb-1">ደረጃ መምረጫ</label>
                  <select
                    value={newGrade}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewGrade(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none"
                  >
                    <option value="ሞተር / ባጃጅ መንጃ ፍቃድ">ሞተር / ባጃጅ መንጃ ፍቃድ</option>
                    <option value="ደረጃ 3 (ታክሲ/ሚኒባስ)">ደረጃ 3 (ታክሲ/ሚኒባስ)</option>
                    <option value="ደረጃ 4 (ህዝብ 1)">ደረጃ 4 (ህዝብ 1)</option>
                    <option value="ደረጃ 5 (ህዝብ 2)">ደረጃ 5 (ህዝብ 2)</option>
                    <option value="ደረጃ 5 (የከባድ ጭነት)">ደረጃ 5 (የከባድ ጭነት)</option>
                    <option value="ዓለም አቀፍ መንጃ ፍቃድ (International)">ዓለም አቀፍ መንጃ ፍቃድ</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-slate-300 font-medium mb-1">
                  ወይም የፍቃድ ዓይነት በነፃነት ይፃፉ (Free-form custom grade):
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="ለምሳሌ፡ ልዩ የኤሌክትሪክ ተሽከርካሪ / የውጭ አገር መንጃ ፍቃድ..."
                  value={customGradeInput}
                  onChange={(e) => setCustomGradeInput(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-slate-300 font-medium mb-1">ስልክ ቁጥር</label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={newPhone}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewPhone(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-slate-300 font-medium mb-1">የልምድ ዓመታት</label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    value={newExp}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewExp(Number(e.target.value))}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-slate-300 font-medium mb-1">የሚመደብበት ሰሌዳ ቁጥር</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="1-A10482 አ.አ"
                  value={newPlate}
                  onChange={(e) => setNewPlate(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none font-mono"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setIsAddModalOpen(false)}
                  className="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 rounded-xl"
                >
                  {t.cancel}
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="px-5 py-2 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white font-bold rounded-xl shadow-lg"
                >
                  {t.save}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};


// =============================================================================
// FILE: src/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx
// =============================================================================

import React, { Component, ErrorInfo, ReactNode } from 'react';
import { AlertTriangle, RotateCcw } from 'lucide-react';

interface ErrorBoundaryProps {
  children: ReactNode;
}

interface ErrorBoundaryState {
  hasError: boolean;
  errorMessage: string;
}

export class ErrorBoundary extends Component<ErrorBoundaryProps, ErrorBoundaryState> {
  constructor(props: ErrorBoundaryProps) {
    super(props);
    this.state = {
      hasError: false,
      errorMessage: '',
    };
  }

  static getDerivedStateFromError(error: Error): ErrorBoundaryState {
    return {
      hasError: true,
      errorMessage: error.message || 'ያልተጠበቀ ስህተት ተፈጥሯል',
    };
  }

  componentDidCatch(error: Error, errorInfo: ErrorInfo) {
    console.error('Application ErrorBoundary caught:', error, errorInfo);
  }

  handleReload = () => {
    this.setState({ hasError: false, errorMessage: '' });
    window.location.reload();
  };

  render() {
    if (this.state.hasError) {
      return (
        <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-950 text-white flex items-center justify-center p-6">
          <div className="max-w-lg w-full bg-slate-900 border border-red-500/50 rounded-3xl p-6 shadow-2xl space-y-4">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="p-3 bg-red-500/20 text-red-400 rounded-2xl">
                <AlertTriangle className="w-6 h-6" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <h2 className="font-bold text-lg text-white">የስርዓት ማስጠንቀቂያ (System Notice)</h2>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">ስርዓቱን በሰላም ለመቀጠል ከታች ያለውን ቁልፍ ይጫኑ</p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-3 text-xs font-mono text-red-300 break-words max-h-40 overflow-y-auto">
              {this.state.errorMessage}
            </div>

            <button
              onClick={this.handleReload}
              className="w-full py-2.5 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white font-bold text-sm rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
            >
              <RotateCcw className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>ዳግም ጫን (Reload Application)</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      );
    }

    return this.props.children;
  }
}

        </div>

        {/* Safety Reports CSV */}
        <div className="bg-slate-800/90 border border-slate-700/80 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-lg space-y-3 flex flex-col justify-between">
          <div>
            <div className="p-2.5 bg-red-500/20 text-red-400 rounded-xl w-fit mb-2">
              <ShieldAlert className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <h3 className="font-bold text-white text-sm">የትራፊክ ደህንነትና ጥቆማ</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">
              የፍጥነት ወሰን ጥሰቶች፣ የዜጎች ጥቆማዎች፣ ማስረጃዎችና የተወሰዱ ውሳኔዎች
            </p>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={() => exportSafetyReportsToCSV(reports)}
            className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2.5 bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-500 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all shadow active:scale-95"
          >
            <Download className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>ኤክሴል አውርድ ({reports.length})</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Official Government Trip Manifest / Waybill Generator */}
      <div className="bg-slate-800/90 border border-slate-700/80 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-xl space-y-4">
        <div className="border-b border-slate-700 pb-3 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <FileText className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-400" />
            <h3 className="font-bold text-white text-sm sm:text-base">
              ህጋዊ የመንገድ ጉዞ ፈቃድና ማኒፌስት ማመንጫ (Official Trip Waybill & Manifest)
            </h3>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4 text-xs">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-slate-300 font-medium mb-1">ተሽከርካሪ ይምረጡ</label>
            <select
              value={selectedVehicleForManifest}
              onChange={(e) => setSelectedVehicleForManifest(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 text-white rounded-xl p-2.5 focus:outline-none"
            >
              {vehicles.map((v) => (
                <option key={v.id} value={v.plateNumber}>
                  {v.plateNumber} - {v.makeModel}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-slate-300 font-medium mb-1">የጉዞ መስመር</label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={manifestRoute}
              onChange={(e) => setManifestRoute(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 text-white rounded-xl p-2.5 focus:outline-none"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-slate-300 font-medium mb-1">የተሳፋሪዎች ቁጥር</label>
            <input
              type="number"
              value={manifestPassengerCount}
              onChange={(e) => setManifestPassengerCount(Number(e.target.value))}
              className="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 text-white rounded-xl p-2.5 focus:outline-none font-mono"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex justify-end pt-2">
          <button
            onClick={() => setShowPrintManifest(true)}
            className="flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-600 to-teal-700 hover:from-emerald-500 hover:to-teal-600 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-lg transition-transform active:scale-95"
          >
            <Printer className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>ማኒፌስቱን አዘጋጅና አትም (Generate & Print Manifest)</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Printable Manifest Preview */}
      {showPrintManifest && matchedVeh && (
        <div className="bg-white text-slate-950 p-6 sm:p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl border-4 border-slate-800 space-y-6">
          {/* Header */}
          <div className="border-b-2 border-slate-900 pb-4 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between text-center sm:text-left gap-3">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-xl font-extrabold uppercase tracking-tight">የኢትዮጵያ ፌዴራላዊ ዴሞክራሲያዊ ሪፐብሊክ</h2>
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-emerald-800">የመንገድ እና ትራንስፖርት ባለስልጣን</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-600">ህጋዊ የሕዝብ/የጭነት መጓጓዣ የመንገድ ፈቃድና የጉዞ ማኒፌስት</p>
            </div>

            <div className="border-2 border-slate-900 p-2 text-center rounded font-mono text-xs">
              <span className="block text-[9px] uppercase font-bold">የማኒፌስት መለያ ቁጥር</span>
              <span className="font-extrabold text-sm text-red-600">
                MNF-2026-{Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000)}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Details Table */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-4 text-xs border p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-50">
            <div>
              <span className="text-slate-500 block">የሰሌዳ ቁጥር:</span>
              <span className="font-mono font-bold text-sm">{matchedVeh.plateNumber}</span>
            </div>
            <div>
              <span className="text-slate-500 block">ተሽከርካሪ ሞዴል:</span>
              <span className="font-bold">{matchedVeh.makeModel}</span>
            </div>
            <div>
              <span className="text-slate-500 block">አሽከርካሪ:</span>
              <span className="font-bold">{matchedVeh.driverName}</span>
            </div>
            <div>
              <span className="text-slate-500 block">ስልክ:</span>
              <span className="font-mono">{matchedVeh.driverPhone}</span>
            </div>
            <div>
              <span className="text-slate-500 block">የባለቤት ስም:</span>
              <span className="font-semibold">{matchedVeh.ownerName}</span>
            </div>
            <div>
              <span className="text-slate-500 block">የጉዞ መስመር:</span>
              <span className="font-bold text-emerald-700">{manifestRoute}</span>
            </div>
            <div>
              <span className="text-slate-500 block">የተሳፋሪ ቁጥር:</span>
              <span className="font-mono font-bold">{manifestPassengerCount} ተሳፋሪዎች</span>
            </div>
            <div>
              <span className="text-slate-500 block">የቴክኒክ ምርመራ:</span>
              <span className="font-semibold text-emerald-600">ብቁ (Passed)</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Signature and Stamp Seals */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-4 pt-4 border-t text-xs text-center">
            <div>
              <span className="text-slate-500 block">የመናኸሪያ ተቆጣጣሪ ፊርማ:</span>
              <div className="h-10 border-b border-dashed border-slate-400 mt-2"></div>
            </div>
            <div>
              <span className="text-slate-500 block">የአሽከርካሪ ፊርማ:</span>
              <div className="h-10 border-b border-dashed border-slate-400 mt-2"></div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
              <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full border-2 border-emerald-700 flex items-center justify-center text-[9px] font-bold text-emerald-800 text-center uppercase rotate-12">
                ማህተም<br />OFFICIAL
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex justify-between items-center pt-2">
            <button
              onClick={() => setShowPrintManifest(false)}
              className="px-4 py-1.5 bg-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-300 text-slate-800 font-semibold text-xs rounded-lg"
            >
              ዝጋ
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => window.print()}
              className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-5 py-2 bg-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-600 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow"
            >
              <Printer className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>ወደ ፕሪንተር ላክ (Print Document)</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};


// =============================================================================
// FILE: src/components/GpsLiveTracking.tsx
// =============================================================================

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { 
  AlertTriangle, 
  Fuel, 
  Navigation, 
  User, 
  Volume2, 
  Search, 
  Radio, 
  Clock, 
  Compass,
  MapPin,
  Camera,
  Crosshair,
  LocateFixed,
  History,
  Gauge,
  Route,
  CheckCircle2
} from 'lucide-react';
import L from 'leaflet';
import { Language, Vehicle } from '../types';
import { translations } from '../data/translations';
import { playEmergencySiren, speakText } from '../utils/audio';
import { COMPLETE_VEHICLE_CATALOG, getVehicleEmoji } from '../data/vehicleCatalog';
import { 
  ETHIOPIA_PLACES, 
  calculateBearingAndCompass, 
  calculateDistanceKm, 
  findDestinationCoords, 
  resolveExactLocation 
} from '../utils/geoLocator';

interface GpsLiveTrackingProps {
  vehicles: Vehicle[];
  currentLang: Language;
  onUpdateVehiclePosition?: (vehicleId: string, lat: number, lng: number, speedKmh: number) => void;
  audioAlertsEnabled: boolean;
}

export const GpsLiveTracking: React.FC<GpsLiveTrackingProps> = ({
  vehicles,
  currentLang,
  onUpdateVehiclePosition,
  audioAlertsEnabled,
}) => {
  const t = translations[currentLang];
  const [selectedVehicleId, setSelectedVehicleId] = useState<string>(vehicles[0]?.id || '');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState<string>('');
  const [filterType, setFilterType] = useState<string>('all');
  const [mapLayer, setMapLayer] = useState<'satellite' | 'streets' | 'radar'>('satellite');
  const [followVehicle, setFollowVehicle] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [liveGeoActive, setLiveGeoActive] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [liveGeoError, setLiveGeoError] = useState<string>('');
  const [onlineStreetName, setOnlineStreetName] = useState<string>('');

  const mapContainerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const mapInstanceRef = useRef<L.Map | null>(null);
  const markersLayerRef = useRef<L.LayerGroup | null>(null);
  const trailLayerRef = useRef<L.LayerGroup | null>(null);
  const tileLayerRef = useRef<L.TileLayer | null>(null);
  const watchIdRef = useRef<number | null>(null);

  const selectedVehicle = vehicles.find((v) => v.id === selectedVehicleId) || vehicles[0];

  // Filtered vehicles
  const filteredVehicles = vehicles.filter((v) => {
    const matchesSearch =
      v.plateNumber.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      v.driverName.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      v.makeModel.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      (v.currentLocationName && v.currentLocationName.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase())) ||
      (v.origin && v.origin.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase())) ||
      (v.destination && v.destination.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()));

    const matchesType =
      filterType === 'all'
        ? true
        : filterType === 'overspeed'
        ? v.currentSpeed > v.speedLimit
        : v.vehicleType === filterType;

    return matchesSearch && matchesType;
  });

  // Resolve exact live location + ETA for selected vehicle
  const resolvedLoc = selectedVehicle
    ? resolveExactLocation(selectedVehicle.lat, selectedVehicle.lng)
    : null;
  const destTarget = selectedVehicle ? findDestinationCoords(selectedVehicle.destination) : null;
  const remainingKm =
    selectedVehicle && destTarget
      ? calculateDistanceKm(selectedVehicle.lat, selectedVehicle.lng, destTarget.lat, destTarget.lng)
      : 0;
  const estimatedHours =
    selectedVehicle && selectedVehicle.currentSpeed > 5
      ? remainingKm / selectedVehicle.currentSpeed
      : remainingKm / 55;
  const etaMinutes = Math.max(1, Math.round(estimatedHours * 60));

  const compassInfo =
    selectedVehicle && destTarget
      ? calculateBearingAndCompass(selectedVehicle.lat, selectedVehicle.lng, destTarget.lat, destTarget.lng)
      : { degrees: 0, compassAm: 'ሰሜን', compassEn: 'N' };

  // Optional online street-level reverse geocode (non-blocking, gracefully silent offline)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedVehicle || !navigator.onLine) {
      setOnlineStreetName('');
      return;
    }

    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timer = setTimeout(async () => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(
          `https://nominatim.openstreetmap.org/reverse?format=jsonv2&lat=${selectedVehicle.lat}&lon=${selectedVehicle.lng}&zoom=16`,
          { signal: controller.signal }
        );
        if (res.ok) {
          const data = await res.json();
          if (data?.display_name) {
            const parts = String(data.display_name).split(',').slice(0, 3).join(', ');
            setOnlineStreetName(parts);
          }
        }
      } catch {
        // Silent offline fallback
      }
    }, 1200);

    return () => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      controller.abort();
    };
  }, [selectedVehicle?.id, Math.round((selectedVehicle?.lat || 0) * 200), Math.round((selectedVehicle?.lng || 0) * 200)]);

  // Real Hardware Device GPS Live Broadcast (navigator.geolocation.watchPosition)
  const toggleLiveDeviceGps = () => {
    if (liveGeoActive) {
      if (watchIdRef.current !== null && navigator.geolocation) {
        navigator.geolocation.clearWatch(watchIdRef.current);
      }
      watchIdRef.current = null;
      setLiveGeoActive(false);
      setLiveGeoError('');
      return;
    }

    if (!navigator.geolocation) {
      setLiveGeoError('ይህ መሳሪያ ቀጥታ የጂፒኤስ ሃርድዌርን አይደግፍም።');
      return;
    }

    setLiveGeoActive(true);
    setLiveGeoError('');
    watchIdRef.current = navigator.geolocation.watchPosition(
      (pos) => {
        const { latitude, longitude, speed } = pos.coords;
        const speedKmh = speed !== null && !isNaN(speed) ? Math.max(0, Math.round(speed * 3.6)) : 45;
        if (selectedVehicle && onUpdateVehiclePosition) {
          onUpdateVehiclePosition(selectedVehicle.id, latitude, longitude, speedKmh);
          mapInstanceRef.current?.flyTo([latitude, longitude], 14, { duration: 0.8 });
        }
      },
      () => {
        setLiveGeoError('የጂፒኤስ ፍቃድ አልተሰጠም ወይም ሲግናል አልተገኘም — የሳተላይት መስመር ክትትል ቀጥሏል።');
        setLiveGeoActive(false);
      },
      { enableHighAccuracy: true, maximumAge: 1000, timeout: 8000 }
    );
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (watchIdRef.current !== null && navigator.geolocation) {
        navigator.geolocation.clearWatch(watchIdRef.current);
      }
    };
  }, []);

  // Initialize Leaflet Map
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mapContainerRef.current) return;
    if (mapInstanceRef.current) return;

    const map = L.map(mapContainerRef.current, {
      center: [selectedVehicle?.lat || 9.02, selectedVehicle?.lng || 38.75],
      zoom: 8,
      zoomControl: false,
    });

    L.control.zoom({ position: 'topright' }).addTo(map);

    const tileLayer = L.tileLayer(
      'https://server.arcgisonline.com/ArcGIS/rest/services/World_Imagery/MapServer/tile/{z}/{y}/{x}',
      {
        attribution: '&copy; Esri World Imagery',
        maxZoom: 19,
      }
    ).addTo(map);

    tileLayerRef.current = tileLayer;
    trailLayerRef.current = L.layerGroup().addTo(map);
    markersLayerRef.current = L.layerGroup().addTo(map);
    mapInstanceRef.current = map;

    return () => {
      map.remove();
      mapInstanceRef.current = null;
    };
  }, []);

  // Switch Map Layer (Satellite vs Street vs Dark Command Radar)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mapInstanceRef.current || !tileLayerRef.current) return;

    mapInstanceRef.current.removeLayer(tileLayerRef.current);

    const newUrl =
      mapLayer === 'satellite'
        ? 'https://server.arcgisonline.com/ArcGIS/rest/services/World_Imagery/MapServer/tile/{z}/{y}/{x}'
        : mapLayer === 'radar'
        ? 'https://{s}.basemaps.cartocdn.com/dark_all/{z}/{x}/{y}{r}.png'
        : 'https://{s}.tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png';

    const newLayer = L.tileLayer(newUrl, {
      attribution: mapLayer === 'satellite' ? '&copy; Esri Satellite' : '&copy; OpenStreetMap',
      maxZoom: 19,
    }).addTo(mapInstanceRef.current);

    tileLayerRef.current = newLayer;
  }, [mapLayer]);

  // Render Landmarks, Checkpoints, Breadcrumb Trail Polyline, and Vehicles
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!mapInstanceRef.current || !markersLayerRef.current || !trailLayerRef.current) return;

    markersLayerRef.current.clearLayers();
    trailLayerRef.current.clearLayers();

    // 1. Plot City Hubs & Safety Checkpoints with real photos
    ETHIOPIA_PLACES.forEach((place) => {
      const isCheckpoint = place.type === 'checkpoint';
      const pinColor = isCheckpoint ? '#f59e0b' : '#10b981';

      const landmarkIcon = L.divIcon({
        className: 'custom-city-pin',
        html: `
          <div style="background: rgba(15,23,42,0.88); border: 1.5px solid ${pinColor}; border-radius: 8px; padding: 2px 6px; color: #fff; font-size: 10px; font-weight: bold; white-space: nowrap; box-shadow: 0 4px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.45); display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 4px;">
            <span style="width: 6px; height: 6px; border-radius: 50%; background: ${pinColor}; display: inline-block;"></span>
            <span>${place.nameAm.split('(')[0].trim()}</span>
          </div>
        `,
        iconSize: [110, 22],
        iconAnchor: [55, 11],
      });

      const m = L.marker([place.lat, place.lng], { icon: landmarkIcon });
      m.bindPopup(`
        <div style="font-family: sans-serif; font-size: 12px; max-width: 220px;">
          <img src="${place.imageUrl}" style="width: 100%; height: 100px; object-fit: cover; border-radius: 8px; margin-bottom: 6px;" />
          <strong style="color: #065f46; font-size: 13px; display: block;">${place.nameAm}</strong>
          <span style="font-size: 10px; color: #6b7280; display: block; margin-top: 2px;">${place.region} • ${place.lat.toFixed(4)}°N, ${place.lng.toFixed(4)}°E</span>
          <p style="margin: 4px 0 0; color: #374151; font-size: 11px;">${place.description}</p>
        </div>
      `);
      markersLayerRef.current?.addLayer(m);
    });

    // 2. Draw Live Breadcrumb Trail & Destination Corridor Vector for Selected Vehicle
    if (selectedVehicle) {
      const trailPoints = (selectedVehicle.trail || []).map((pt) => [pt.lat, pt.lng] as [number, number]);
      if (trailPoints.length > 1) {
        const trailLine = L.polyline(trailPoints, {
          color: '#38bdf8',
          weight: 4,
          opacity: 0.9,
        });
        trailLayerRef.current.addLayer(trailLine);

        // Plot individual second-by-second breadcrumb dots
        (selectedVehicle.trail || []).forEach((pt, idx) => {
          if (idx === (selectedVehicle.trail?.length || 0) - 1) return;
          const dot = L.circleMarker([pt.lat, pt.lng], {
            radius: 3.5,
            color: '#0ea5e9',
            fillColor: '#e0f2fe',
            fillOpacity: 0.9,
            weight: 1.5,
          }).bindTooltip(`${pt.timestamp} | ${pt.speed} km/h | ${pt.placeName}`, { direction: 'top' });
          trailLayerRef.current?.addLayer(dot);
        });
      }

      // Dashed projected line to destination
      if (destTarget) {
        const projectedLine = L.polyline(
          [
            [selectedVehicle.lat, selectedVehicle.lng],
            [destTarget.lat, destTarget.lng],
          ],
          {
            color: '#10b981',
            weight: 2,
            dashArray: '6, 8',
            opacity: 0.65,
          }
        );
        trailLayerRef.current.addLayer(projectedLine);
      }
    }

    // 3. Plot All Vehicles with Exact Icon, Speed, Place Badge & Click Popup
    filteredVehicles.forEach((v) => {
      const isSelected = v.id === selectedVehicleId;
      const isOverspeed = v.currentSpeed > v.speedLimit;
      const vehicleEmoji = getVehicleEmoji(v.vehicleType);
      const locInfo = resolveExactLocation(v.lat, v.lng);

      const bgClr = isOverspeed ? '#dc2626' : isSelected ? '#0284c7' : '#0f172a';
      const borderClr = isOverspeed ? '#ef4444' : isSelected ? '#38bdf8' : '#10b981';

      const vehIcon = L.divIcon({
        className: 'custom-veh-marker',
        html: `
          <div style="cursor: pointer; transform: ${isSelected ? 'scale(1.22)' : 'scale(1)'}; transition: transform 0.25s ease;">
            <div style="background: ${bgClr}; border: 2.5px solid ${borderClr}; border-radius: 12px; width: 38px; height: 38px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; font-size: 18px; box-shadow: 0 6px 14px rgba(0,0,0,0.65); position: relative;">
              ${vehicleEmoji}
              ${
                v.status === 'on_route'
                  ? '<span style="position: absolute; bottom: -4px; right: -4px; width: 10px; height: 10px; border-radius: 50%; background: #10b981; border: 1.5px solid #fff;"></span>'
                  : ''
              }
              ${
                isOverspeed
                  ? '<span style="position: absolute; top: -4px; right: -4px; width: 11px; height: 11px; border-radius: 50%; background: #ef4444; border: 1.5px solid #fff;"></span>'
                  : ''
              }
            </div>
            <div style="background: rgba(15,23,42,0.95); color: ${isOverspeed ? '#fca5a5' : '#38bdf8'}; font-family: monospace; font-size: 9px; font-weight: bold; padding: 1px 5px; border-radius: 5px; text-align: center; margin-top: 2px; border: 1px solid rgba(56,189,248,0.35); white-space: nowrap;">
              ${v.plateNumber.split(' ')[0]} • ${v.currentSpeed}km/h
            </div>
          </div>
        `,
        iconSize: [54, 56],
        iconAnchor: [27, 28],
      });

      const marker = L.marker([v.lat, v.lng], { icon: vehIcon });
      marker.bindPopup(`
        <div style="font-family: sans-serif; font-size: 12px; min-width: 210px;">
          <img src="${locInfo.imageUrl}" style="width: 100%; height: 90px; object-fit: cover; border-radius: 8px; margin-bottom: 6px;" />
          <div style="display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center;">
            <strong style="color: #0f172a; font-size: 13px;">${v.plateNumber}</strong>
            <span style="background:${isOverspeed ? '#fee2e2' : '#d1fae5'}; color:${isOverspeed ? '#991b1b' : '#065f46'}; font-size:10px; font-weight:bold; padding:2px 6px; border-radius:99px;">${v.currentSpeed} km/h</span>
          </div>
          <div style="font-size: 11px; color: #334155; margin-top: 3px;"><strong>${v.makeModel}</strong></div>
          <div style="font-size: 11px; color: #047857; margin-top: 4px; font-weight: 600;">📍 ${locInfo.formattedLabel}</div>
          <div style="font-size: 10px; color: #64748b; font-family: monospace; margin-top: 3px;">${v.lat.toFixed(5)}°N, ${v.lng.toFixed(5)}°E • ${v.lastUpdate}</div>
        </div>
      `);

      marker.on('click', () => {
        setSelectedVehicleId(v.id);
      });

      markersLayerRef.current?.addLayer(marker);
    });
  }, [filteredVehicles, selectedVehicleId]);

  // Smooth Follow-Cam when selected vehicle moves
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!followVehicle || !selectedVehicle || !mapInstanceRef.current) return;
    mapInstanceRef.current.panTo([selectedVehicle.lat, selectedVehicle.lng], {
      animate: true,
      duration: 0.9,
    });
  }, [selectedVehicle?.lat, selectedVehicle?.lng, followVehicle]);

  const handleZoomToVehicle = (v: Vehicle) => {
    setSelectedVehicleId(v.id);
    mapInstanceRef.current?.flyTo([v.lat, v.lng], 13, { duration: 1.0 });
  };

  const handleVoiceWarningToDriver = (vehicle: Vehicle) => {
    if (audioAlertsEnabled) {
      playEmergencySiren();
    }
    const locLabel = resolveExactLocation(vehicle.lat, vehicle.lng).formattedLabel;
    const warningMsg = `ማስጠንቀቂያ ለአሽከርካሪ ${vehicle.driverName}፤ አሁን የሚገኙበት ቦታ ${locLabel} ሲሆን ፍጥነትዎ ${vehicle.currentSpeed} ኪሎሜትር በሰዓት ሆኗል! እባክዎ አሁኑኑ ፍጥነትዎን ይቀንሱ።`;
    speakText(warningMsg, 'am');
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {/* Control Banner */}
      <div className="bg-slate-900/95 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 shadow-xl flex flex-col lg:flex-row lg:items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <span className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-500/30">
            <Radio className="w-5 h-5 animate-pulse" />
          </span>
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-extrabold text-white flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              <span>{t.liveTracking}</span>
              <span className="text-xs font-mono bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-400 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-emerald-700/60">
                {vehicles.length} ተሽከርካሪዎች በሰከንድ ክትትል
              </span>
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
              እያንዳንዷ የጉዞ እንቅስቃሴ በሳተላይት ካርታ፣ በእውነተኛ የቦታ ምስል፣ በሰዓትና በኮርዲኔት
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Controls: Search, Follow Cam, Real Hardware GPS, Layers & Filters */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          {/* Search */}
          <div className="relative">
            <Search className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400 absolute left-2.5 top-2.5" />
            <input
              type="text"
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="ሰሌዳ፣ ሞዴል፣ ከተማ..."
              className="bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white text-xs rounded-xl pl-8 pr-3 py-1.5 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-emerald-500 w-40 sm:w-44"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Real Device Hardware GPS Broadcast */}
          <button
            onClick={toggleLiveDeviceGps}
            className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold border transition-all ${
              liveGeoActive
                ? 'bg-cyan-600 text-white border-cyan-400 shadow-lg shadow-cyan-950/60 animate-pulse'
                : 'bg-slate-800 text-cyan-300 border-slate-700 hover:border-cyan-500/50'
            }`}
            title="የስልክዎን ወይም የኮምፒውተርዎን እውነተኛ ጂፒኤስ በቀጥታ ያስተላልፉ"
          >
            <LocateFixed className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span className="hidden sm:inline">{liveGeoActive ? 'ቀጥታ ጂፒኤስ እየተላለፈ ነው' : 'የራሴን ጂፒኤስ አገናኝ'}</span>
          </button>

          {/* Auto-Follow Toggle */}
          <button
            onClick={() => setFollowVehicle(!followVehicle)}
            className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold border transition-colors ${
              followVehicle
                ? 'bg-emerald-600/20 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-500/50'
                : 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 border-slate-700'
            }`}
          >
            <Crosshair className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>ተከተል</span>
          </button>

          {/* Map Layer Toggle */}
          <div className="flex items-center bg-slate-800 rounded-xl p-1 border border-slate-700 text-xs">
            <button
              onClick={() => setMapLayer('satellite')}
              className={`px-2 py-1 rounded-lg font-semibold transition-colors ${
                mapLayer === 'satellite' ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white' : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white'
              }`}
            >
              ሳተላይት
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setMapLayer('streets')}
              className={`px-2 py-1 rounded-lg font-semibold transition-colors ${
                mapLayer === 'streets' ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white' : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white'
              }`}
            >
              ጎዳና
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setMapLayer('radar')}
              className={`px-2 py-1 rounded-lg font-semibold transition-colors ${
                mapLayer === 'radar' ? 'bg-cyan-600 text-white' : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white'
              }`}
            >
              ራዳር
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Unlimited Vehicle Type Filter */}
          <select
            value={filterType}
            onChange={(e) => setFilterType(e.target.value)}
            className="bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-slate-200 text-xs rounded-xl px-2.5 py-1.5 focus:outline-none"
          >
            <option value="all">ሁሉም ({vehicles.length})</option>
            <option value="overspeed">⚠️ የፍጥነት ወሰን ያለፉ</option>
            {COMPLETE_VEHICLE_CATALOG.map((cat) => (
              <option key={cat.type} value={cat.type}>
                {cat.emoji} {cat.amharicName}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      {liveGeoError && (
        <div className="bg-amber-950/60 border border-amber-500/50 text-amber-200 text-xs px-4 py-2 rounded-xl flex items-center gap-2">
          <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400 shrink-0" />
          <span>{liveGeoError}</span>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Main Grid: Interactive Satellite Map + Live Location Imagery & Second-by-Second HUD */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-4">
        {/* Left: Real Leaflet Map + Floating Live Position Snapshot Overlay */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-8 space-y-4">
          <div className="bg-slate-950 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 p-2 relative shadow-2xl overflow-hidden">
            <div ref={mapContainerRef} className="w-full h-[520px] rounded-xl z-0 overflow-hidden" />

            {/* Top Floating Telemetry Status Pill */}
            <div className="absolute top-4 left-4 z-10 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 pointer-events-none">
              <div className="bg-slate-900/95 backdrop-blur-md px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-emerald-500/50 pointer-events-auto text-xs font-mono text-emerald-400 font-bold flex items-center gap-2 shadow-xl">
                <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 animate-ping" />
                <span>LIVE GPS • 1.5s PRECISION TELEMETRY</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Bottom-Left Floating Real-Time Exact Location Image & Place Card */}
            {selectedVehicle && resolvedLoc && (
              <div className="absolute bottom-4 left-4 right-4 sm:right-auto sm:max-w-sm z-10 bg-slate-950/92 backdrop-blur-md border border-cyan-500/40 rounded-2xl p-3 shadow-2xl flex items-center gap-3">
                <img
                  src={selectedVehicle.currentLocationImage || resolvedLoc.imageUrl}
                  alt={resolvedLoc.place.nameAm}
                  className="w-20 h-20 rounded-xl object-cover border border-slate-700 shrink-0"
                />
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 space-y-1">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                    <span className="font-mono font-extrabold text-amber-400 text-xs truncate">
                      {getVehicleEmoji(selectedVehicle.vehicleType)} {selectedVehicle.plateNumber}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-mono bg-cyan-500/20 text-cyan-300 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded border border-cyan-500/30">
                      {selectedVehicle.lastUpdate}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-xs font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-1 truncate">
                    <MapPin className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400 shrink-0" />
                    <span className="truncate">{onlineStreetName || selectedVehicle.currentLocationName || resolvedLoc.formattedLabel}</span>
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-300 font-mono">
                    {selectedVehicle.lat.toFixed(5)}°N, {selectedVehicle.lng.toFixed(5)}°E • አቅጣጫ {compassInfo.degrees}° ({compassInfo.compassAm})
                  </p>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px]">
                    <span className="text-emerald-400 font-bold">
                      ቀሪ ርቀት፡ {remainingKm.toFixed(1)} ኪ.ሜ
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-slate-500">|</span>
                    <span className="text-cyan-300 font-bold">መድረሻ ግምት፡ ~{etaMinutes} ደቂቃ</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Second-by-Second Live Movement Audit Log (እያንዳንዷ እንቅስቃሴ በሰዓትና በቦታ) */}
          {selectedVehicle && (
            <div className="bg-slate-900/95 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 shadow-xl space-y-3">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800 pb-2.5">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <History className="w-4 h-4 text-cyan-400" />
                  <h3 className="font-bold text-white text-xs sm:text-sm">
                    የተሽከርካሪው እያንዳንዷ የእንቅስቃሴ ታሪክ በሰዓት፣ በካርታና በቦታ ({selectedVehicle.plateNumber})
                  </h3>
                </div>
                <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-emerald-400 bg-emerald-950/60 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-emerald-700/40">
                  {(selectedVehicle.trail || []).length} የቅርብ ነጥቦች
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="max-h-44 overflow-y-auto">
                <table className="w-full text-left text-[11px] text-slate-300">
                  <thead className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 uppercase bg-slate-950/80 sticky top-0">
                    <tr>
                      <th className="px-3 py-2">ሰዓት (EAT)</th>
                      <th className="px-3 py-2">ትክክለኛ ቦታ (Place / Street)</th>
                      <th className="px-3 py-2">ኬክሮስ / ኬንትሮስ</th>
                      <th className="px-3 py-2 text-right">ፍጥነት</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-800/80">
                    {[...(selectedVehicle.trail || [])].reverse().slice(0, 12).map((pt, idx) => (
                      <tr key={`${pt.timestamp}-${idx}`} className={idx === 0 ? 'bg-cyan-950/30 text-white font-semibold' : ''}>
                        <td className="px-3 py-1.5 font-mono text-cyan-300 whitespace-nowrap">{pt.timestamp}</td>
                        <td className="px-3 py-1.5 truncate max-w-[200px]">
                          {idx === 0 ? '🟢 ' : '• '}
                          {pt.placeName}
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-3 py-1.5 font-mono text-slate-400 whitespace-nowrap">
                          {pt.lat.toFixed(5)}°, {pt.lng.toFixed(5)}°
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-3 py-1.5 text-right font-mono font-bold text-emerald-400">
                          {pt.speed} km/h
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Right: Selected Vehicle Telemetry HUD + Location Photo & Quick Fleet Selector */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-4 space-y-3">
          {selectedVehicle && resolvedLoc && (
            <div className="bg-slate-900/95 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 p-4 shadow-xl space-y-3">
              {/* Header */}
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
                <div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="text-lg">{getVehicleEmoji(selectedVehicle.vehicleType)}</span>
                    <span className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-amber-600 text-slate-950 font-extrabold rounded-md font-mono text-sm shadow">
                      {selectedVehicle.plateNumber}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-200 mt-1.5 font-bold">{selectedVehicle.makeModel}</p>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">{selectedVehicle.ownerName}</p>
                </div>

                <span
                  className={`text-[11px] px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full font-bold border ${
                    selectedVehicle.status === 'on_route'
                      ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-500/40'
                      : selectedVehicle.status === 'in_queue'
                      ? 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border-amber-500/40'
                      : 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 border-slate-700'
                  }`}
                >
                  {selectedVehicle.status === 'on_route' ? 'በጉዞ ላይ' : selectedVehicle.status === 'in_queue' ? 'በተራ ላይ' : 'ዝግጁ'}
                </span>
              </div>

              {/* Exact Real-Time Visual Location Photo Banner */}
              <div className="relative rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-slate-700">
                <img
                  src={selectedVehicle.currentLocationImage || resolvedLoc.imageUrl}
                  alt={resolvedLoc.place.nameAm}
                  className="w-full h-32 object-cover"
                />
                <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-slate-950 via-slate-950/30 to-transparent" />
                <div className="absolute bottom-2 left-2.5 right-2.5 flex items-end justify-between gap-2">
                  <div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-emerald-400 font-bold flex items-center gap-1">
                      <Camera className="w-3 h-3" /> የአሁኑ ቦታ ምስልና ስም
                    </span>
                    <p className="text-xs font-extrabold text-white truncate">
                      {onlineStreetName || selectedVehicle.currentLocationName || resolvedLoc.formattedLabel}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-mono bg-slate-900/90 text-cyan-300 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-cyan-500/40">
                    {resolvedLoc.place.region}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Speedometer & Heading Gauge */}
              <div
                className={`p-3 rounded-xl border relative overflow-hidden ${
                  selectedVehicle.currentSpeed > selectedVehicle.speedLimit
                    ? 'bg-red-950/40 border-red-500/70'
                    : 'bg-slate-950/80 border-slate-800'
                }`}
              >
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1 text-xs">
                  <span className="font-semibold text-slate-400 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <Gauge className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-cyan-400" />
                    <span>የቀጥታ ፍጥነት</span>
                  </span>
                  <span className="font-mono text-slate-400">ወሰን: {selectedVehicle.speedLimit} km/h</span>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between">
                  <div className="flex items-baseline gap-1.5">
                    <span
                      className={`text-3xl font-extrabold font-mono ${
                        selectedVehicle.currentSpeed > selectedVehicle.speedLimit
                          ? 'text-red-400 animate-pulse'
                          : 'text-emerald-400'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {selectedVehicle.currentSpeed}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">km/h</span>
                  </div>

                  <div className="text-right text-[11px]">
                    <span className="text-cyan-300 font-bold flex items-center justify-end gap-1">
                      <Compass className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      {selectedVehicle.heading ?? compassInfo.degrees}° {compassInfo.compassAm}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-mono">
                      ቀሪ: {remainingKm.toFixed(1)} ኪ.ሜ (~{etaMinutes} ደቂቃ)
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="w-full bg-slate-800 h-1.5 rounded-full overflow-hidden mt-2">
                  <div
                    className={`h-full transition-all duration-300 ${
                      selectedVehicle.currentSpeed > selectedVehicle.speedLimit
                        ? 'bg-red-500'
                        : selectedVehicle.currentSpeed > 65
                        ? 'bg-amber-400'
                        : 'bg-emerald-500'
                    }`}
                    style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, (selectedVehicle.currentSpeed / 120) * 100)}%` }}
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Action: Voice Warning to Driver */}
              <button
                onClick={() => handleVoiceWarningToDriver(selectedVehicle)}
                className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2 px-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-red-600 via-rose-600 to-amber-600 hover:from-red-500 hover:to-amber-500 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow transition-transform active:scale-95"
              >
                <Volume2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-pulse" />
                <span>ለአሽከርካሪው የድምፅ ማስጠንቀቂያ አሰማ</span>
              </button>

              {/* Corridor & Driver Specs */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-xs">
                <div className="bg-slate-950/80 rounded-xl p-2.5 border border-slate-800">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-slate-400 mb-0.5 text-[11px]">
                    <User className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
                    <span>አሽከርካሪ</span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="font-semibold text-slate-100 truncate">{selectedVehicle.driverName}</p>
                  <p className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400">{selectedVehicle.driverPhone}</p>
                </div>

                <div className="bg-slate-950/80 rounded-xl p-2.5 border border-slate-800">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-slate-400 mb-0.5 text-[11px]">
                    <Fuel className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-cyan-400" />
                    <span>ነዳጅ / ባትሪ</span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="font-bold text-slate-100 text-sm font-mono">{selectedVehicle.fuelLevel}%</p>
                  <p className="text-[10px] text-emerald-400 truncate">
                    {selectedVehicle.origin} ➔ {selectedVehicle.destination}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Quick Click-to-Zoom List for all vehicles */}
          <div className="bg-slate-900/95 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 p-3 shadow-lg space-y-2">
            <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center justify-between">
              <span>ሁሉም ተሽከርካሪዎች ({filteredVehicles.length})</span>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-emerald-400 font-mono">ካርታ ላይ ለማየት ይጫኑ</span>
            </h3>

            <div className="max-h-56 overflow-y-auto space-y-1.5 pr-1">
              {filteredVehicles.map((v) => {
                const loc = resolveExactLocation(v.lat, v.lng);
                return (
                  <div
                    key={v.id}
                    onClick={() => handleZoomToVehicle(v)}
                    className={`flex items-center justify-between p-2 rounded-xl text-xs cursor-pointer transition-colors border ${
                      selectedVehicleId === v.id
                        ? 'bg-slate-800 border-cyan-500 text-white'
                        : 'bg-slate-950/70 border-slate-800 text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-800/70'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
                      <span className="text-base">{getVehicleEmoji(v.vehicleType)}</span>
                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <span className="font-mono font-bold text-amber-300 block">{v.plateNumber}</span>
                        <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 truncate">{v.currentLocationName || loc.formattedLabel}</p>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="text-right font-mono shrink-0 ml-2">
                      <span
                        className={`font-bold block ${
                          v.currentSpeed > v.speedLimit ? 'text-red-400 animate-pulse' : 'text-emerald-400'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {v.currentSpeed} km/h
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-[9px] text-slate-500">{v.lastUpdate}</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


// =============================================================================
// FILE: src/components/Navbar.tsx
// =============================================================================

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { 
  ShieldAlert, 
  Radio, 
  MapPin, 
  Truck, 
  Users, 
  Building2, 
  Clock, 
  Route, 
  AlertTriangle, 
  Languages, 
  Volume2, 
  VolumeX, 
  FileSpreadsheet, 
  Mic2, 
  Activity,
  CreditCard,
  Smartphone,
  Menu,
  X,
  Heart,
  Share2,
  FileCheck2,
  Cloud,
  LogIn,
  LogOut,
  LayoutDashboard,
  Download
} from 'lucide-react';
import { User as FirebaseUser } from 'firebase/auth';
import { Language } from '../types';
import { translations } from '../data/translations';
import { playEmergencySiren, speakText } from '../utils/audio';
import { usePWAInstall } from '../hooks/usePWAInstall';
import { SourceExportModal } from './SourceExportModal';

interface NavbarProps {
  currentTab: string;
  setCurrentTab: (tab: string) => void;
  currentLang: Language;
  setCurrentLang: (lang: Language) => void;
  audioAlertsEnabled: boolean;
  setAudioAlertsEnabled: (enabled: boolean) => void;
  emergencyCount: number;
  onTriggerEmergency: () => void;
  activeRole?: 'user' | 'inspector';
  onRoleChange?: (role: 'user' | 'inspector') => void;
  onOpenShareModal?: () => void;
  onOpenContractModal?: () => void;
  cloudUser?: FirebaseUser | null;
  onCloudSignIn?: () => void;
  onCloudSignOut?: () => void;
}

export const Navbar: React.FC<NavbarProps> = ({
  currentTab,
  setCurrentTab,
  currentLang,
  setCurrentLang,
  audioAlertsEnabled,
  setAudioAlertsEnabled,
  emergencyCount,
  onTriggerEmergency,
  activeRole = 'user',
  onRoleChange,
  onOpenShareModal,
  onOpenContractModal,
  cloudUser,
  onCloudSignIn,
  onCloudSignOut,
}) => {
  const t = translations[currentLang];
  const [currentTime, setCurrentTime] = useState<string>('');
  const [mobileMenuOpen, setMobileMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [showIOSGuide, setShowIOSGuide] = useState(false);

  // Like state
  const [likeCount, setLikeCount] = useState<number>(() => {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem('transport_et_likes');
      return saved ? parseInt(saved, 10) : 1482;
    }
    return 1482;
  });
  const [hasLiked, setHasLiked] = useState<boolean>(() => {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      return localStorage.getItem('transport_et_user_has_liked') === 'true';
    }
    return false;
  });

  const handleToggleLike = () => {
    if (hasLiked) {
      const next = Math.max(0, likeCount - 1);
      setLikeCount(next);
      setHasLiked(false);
      localStorage.setItem('transport_et_likes', next.toString());
      localStorage.setItem('transport_et_user_has_liked', 'false');
    } else {
      const next = likeCount + 1;
      setLikeCount(next);
      setHasLiked(true);
      localStorage.setItem('transport_et_likes', next.toString());
      localStorage.setItem('transport_et_user_has_liked', 'true');
      if (audioAlertsEnabled) {
        speakText('እናመሰግናለን! ስርዓቱን ወደዱት!', 'am');
      }
    }
  };

  const { isInstallable, isInstalled, install } = usePWAInstall();
  const [isDownloadingSource, setIsDownloadingSource] = useState<'single' | 'zip' | null>(null);
  const [sourceModalOpen, setSourceModalOpen] = useState(false);

  const handleDownloadSource = async (format: 'single' | 'zip') => {
    if (format === 'single') {
      setSourceModalOpen(true);
    }
    if (isDownloadingSource) return;
    setIsDownloadingSource(format);
    try {
      const endpoint = format === 'single' ? '/api/export-single-file' : '/api/export-source';
      const filename =
        format === 'single'
          ? 'ዘበነ.txt'
          : 'national-transport-portal-source.zip';
      const res = await fetch(endpoint);
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Download failed');
      const blob = await res.blob();
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const link = document.createElement('a');
      link.href = url;
      link.download = filename;
      document.body.appendChild(link);
      link.click();
      document.body.removeChild(link);
      setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 3000);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Source export error:', err);
    } finally {
      setIsDownloadingSource(null);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const updateTime = () => {
      const now = new Date();
      setCurrentTime(
        now.toLocaleTimeString('en-GB', {
          hour: '2-digit',
          minute: '2-digit',
          second: '2-digit',
          timeZone: 'Africa/Addis_Ababa',
        }) + ' EAT'
      );
    };
    updateTime();
    const interval = setInterval(updateTime, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);

  const navItems = [
    { id: 'dashboard', label: t.dashboard, icon: LayoutDashboard },
    { id: 'liveTracking', label: t.liveTracking, icon: MapPin, highlight: true },
    { id: 'queueTurns', label: t.queueTurns, icon: Clock },
    { id: 'vehicles', label: t.vehicles, icon: Truck },
    { id: 'drivers', label: t.drivers, icon: Users },
    { id: 'owners', label: t.owners, icon: Building2 },
    { id: 'routesTariffs', label: t.routesTariffs, icon: Route },
    { id: 'paymentsSettlements', label: t.paymentsSettlements, icon: CreditCard, highlight: true },
    { id: 'safetyCitizenTips', label: t.safetyCitizenTips, icon: ShieldAlert, badge: emergencyCount },
    { id: 'voiceTranslator', label: t.voiceTranslator, icon: Mic2, pulse: true },
    { id: 'reportsExport', label: t.reportsExport, icon: FileSpreadsheet },
  ];

  const languagesList: { code: Language; label: string; sub: string }[] = [
    { code: 'am', label: 'አማርኛ', sub: 'Amharic' },
    { code: 'om', label: 'Afaan Oromoo', sub: 'Oromo' },
    { code: 'ti', label: 'ትግርኛ', sub: 'Tigrinya' },
    { code: 'so', label: 'Soomaali', sub: 'Somali' },
    { code: 'en', label: 'English', sub: 'Global' },
    { code: 'ar', label: 'العربية', sub: 'Arabic' },
    { code: 'fr', label: 'Français', sub: 'French' },
    { code: 'zh', label: '中文 (Chinese)', sub: '汉语' },
  ];

  const handleEmergencyClick = () => {
    if (audioAlertsEnabled) {
      playEmergencySiren();
    }
    onTriggerEmergency();
  };

  const handleInstallClick = async () => {
    if (isInstallable) {
      await install();
    } else {
      setShowIOSGuide(true);
    }
  };

  const sidebarContent = (
    <div className="flex flex-col h-full bg-slate-900 border-r border-slate-800 text-white">
      {/* Left-Docked Dashboard Entry Logo & Identity */}
      <div className="p-4 border-b border-slate-800 space-y-3">
        <button
          onClick={() => {
            setCurrentTab('dashboard');
            setMobileMenuOpen(false);
          }}
          className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 text-left group p-2 rounded-2xl bg-slate-950/70 hover:bg-slate-800/80 border border-emerald-500/30 transition-all"
          title="ወደ ዳሽቦርድ መግቢያ"
        >
          <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-600 via-teal-700 to-cyan-800 flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg shrink-0 group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform">
            <LayoutDashboard className="w-6 h-6 text-white" />
          </div>
          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span className="font-extrabold text-sm bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-400 to-amber-300 bg-clip-text text-transparent truncate">
                {t.appName}
              </span>
            </div>
            <span className="text-xs text-emerald-400 font-semibold flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-0.5">
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 animate-pulse" />
              <span>የዳሽቦርድ መግቢያ • {currentTime}</span>
            </span>
          </div>
        </button>

        {/* Left Role Switcher */}
        {onRoleChange && (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-1 bg-slate-950 p-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 text-xs">
            <button
              onClick={() => onRoleChange('user')}
              className={`py-1.5 px-2 rounded-lg font-bold transition-all ${
                activeRole === 'user' ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white shadow' : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white'
              }`}
            >
              ተጠቃሚ
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => onRoleChange('inspector')}
              className={`py-1.5 px-2 rounded-lg font-bold transition-all ${
                activeRole === 'inspector' ? 'bg-cyan-600 text-white shadow' : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white'
              }`}
            >
              ተቆጣጣሪ
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Cloud Database Auth Control (Backend Sync) */}
        <div className="bg-slate-950/70 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-2.5 text-xs">
          {cloudUser ? (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
                <Cloud className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400 shrink-0" />
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <span className="block font-bold text-emerald-300 truncate">
                    {cloudUser.displayName || 'Cloud Sync Active'}
                  </span>
                  <span className="block text-[11px] text-slate-400 truncate">{cloudUser.email}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              {onCloudSignOut && (
                <button
                  onClick={onCloudSignOut}
                  className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-red-400 rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-800"
                  title="ውጣ (Sign Out)"
                >
                  <LogOut className="w-4 h-4" />
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          ) : (
            onCloudSignIn && (
              <button
                onClick={onCloudSignIn}
                className="w-full py-2 px-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-600 to-blue-700 hover:from-cyan-500 hover:to-blue-600 text-white font-bold rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow transition-all"
              >
                <LogIn className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>በGoogle ግባ (Cloud Sync)</span>
              </button>
            )
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Left Vertical Navigation Menu */}
      <nav className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-3 space-y-1">
        {navItems.map((item) => {
          const Icon = item.icon;
          const isActive = currentTab === item.id;
          return (
            <button
              key={item.id}
              onClick={() => {
                setCurrentTab(item.id);
                setMobileMenuOpen(false);
              }}
              className={`w-full flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all ${
                isActive
                  ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-600 to-teal-700 text-white shadow-md ring-1 ring-emerald-400/40'
                  : 'text-slate-300 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-800/80'
              } ${item.pulse && !isActive ? 'ring-1 ring-amber-500/30 text-amber-300' : ''}`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
                <Icon className={`w-4 h-4 shrink-0 ${isActive ? 'text-white' : item.pulse ? 'text-amber-400' : 'text-slate-400'}`} />
                <span className="truncate">{item.label}</span>
              </div>
              {item.badge !== undefined && item.badge > 0 && (
                <span className="bg-red-500 text-white text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-bold ml-1 animate-pulse">
                  {item.badge}
                </span>
              )}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </nav>

      {/* Left Footer Tools (Language, Audio, SOS, Like/Share, Contract, Install PWA) */}
      <div className="p-3 border-t border-slate-800 space-y-2.5 bg-slate-950/60">
        {/* Language & Audio */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="flex-1 flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs">
            <Languages className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400 shrink-0" />
            <select
              value={currentLang}
              onChange={(e) => setCurrentLang(e.target.value as Language)}
              className="w-full bg-transparent text-xs text-slate-100 font-semibold focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
            >
              {languagesList.map((l) => (
                <option key={l.code} value={l.code} className="bg-slate-900 text-white">
                  {l.label} ({l.sub})
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={() => setAudioAlertsEnabled(!audioAlertsEnabled)}
            title={audioAlertsEnabled ? 'ድምፅ አጥፋ' : 'ድምፅ አብራ'}
            className={`p-2 rounded-xl border transition-colors ${
              audioAlertsEnabled
                ? 'text-emerald-400 bg-emerald-950/60 border-emerald-700/50'
                : 'text-slate-400 bg-slate-800 border-slate-700'
            }`}
          >
            {audioAlertsEnabled ? <Volume2 className="w-4 h-4" /> : <VolumeX className="w-4 h-4" />}
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Like, Share, Smart Contract */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1.5 text-xs">
          <button
            onClick={handleToggleLike}
            className={`flex items-center justify-center gap-1 py-1.5 rounded-xl font-bold border transition-all ${
              hasLiked
                ? 'bg-rose-600 text-white border-rose-500'
                : 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 border-slate-700 hover:text-rose-400'
            }`}
          >
            <Heart className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${hasLiked ? 'fill-white text-white' : 'text-rose-400'}`} />
            <span className="font-mono">{likeCount}</span>
          </button>

          {onOpenShareModal && (
            <button
              onClick={onOpenShareModal}
              className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1 py-1.5 rounded-xl font-bold bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 border border-slate-700"
            >
              <Share2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
              <span>ሸር</span>
            </button>
          )}

          {onOpenContractModal && (
            <button
              onClick={onOpenContractModal}
              className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1 py-1.5 rounded-xl font-bold bg-amber-600/20 hover:bg-amber-600/30 text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/40"
            >
              <FileCheck2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>ውል</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Emergency SOS & Install App */}
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <button
            onClick={handleEmergencyClick}
            className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 py-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-red-600 to-rose-700 hover:from-red-500 hover:to-rose-600 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold shadow border border-red-500"
          >
            <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>SOS 991</span>
          </button>

          {!isInstalled && (
            <button
              onClick={handleInstallClick}
              className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-cyan-600 hover:from-blue-500 hover:to-cyan-500 text-white font-bold text-xs shadow border border-cyan-400/40"
            >
              <Smartphone className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>አፑን ጫን</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Download All Codes in 1 File or ZIP */}
        <div className="flex gap-1.5">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => void handleDownloadSource('single')}
            disabled={isDownloadingSource !== null}
            className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 py-2 px-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 disabled:opacity-60 text-emerald-300 hover:text-white font-bold text-xs border border-emerald-500/40 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            title="ሁሉንም ኮዶች በአንድ ፋይል (.txt) አውርድ"
          >
            <Download className={`w-4 h-4 shrink-0 ${isDownloadingSource === 'single' ? 'animate-bounce' : ''}`} />
            <span className="truncate">
              {isDownloadingSource === 'single' ? 'በማዘጋጀት ላይ...' : 'ዘበነ.txt (ሁሉም ኮድ)'}
            </span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => void handleDownloadSource('zip')}
            disabled={isDownloadingSource !== null}
            className="px-2.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 disabled:opacity-60 text-cyan-300 hover:text-white font-bold text-xs border border-cyan-500/40 transition-colors cursor-pointer shrink-0"
            title="በአንድ ፎልደር (.zip) አውርድ"
          >
            {isDownloadingSource === 'zip' ? '...' : '.zip'}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

  return (
    <>
      {/* Mobile Left-Aligned Minimal Top Bar (Right side completely clean) */}
      <header className="lg:hidden sticky top-0 z-40 bg-slate-900/95 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-slate-800 px-4 h-14 flex items-center justify-start gap-3 text-white shadow-lg">
        <button
          onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(!mobileMenuOpen)}
          className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600/20 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/40 hover:bg-emerald-600/30"
          aria-label="Open Dashboard Menu"
        >
          {mobileMenuOpen ? <X className="w-5 h-5" /> : <Menu className="w-5 h-5" />}
        </button>

        <button
          onClick={() => setCurrentTab('dashboard')}
          className="flex items-center gap-2 text-left"
        >
          <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-600 to-cyan-700 flex items-center justify-center">
            <Radio className="w-4 h-4 text-white animate-pulse" />
          </div>
          <span className="font-extrabold text-sm bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-400 to-amber-300 bg-clip-text text-transparent truncate">
            {t.appName}
          </span>
        </button>
      </header>

      {/* Desktop Fixed Left Sidebar */}
      <aside className="hidden lg:block w-72 shrink-0 sticky top-0 h-screen z-40">
        {sidebarContent}
      </aside>

      {/* Mobile Slide-Out Left Drawer */}
      {mobileMenuOpen && (
        <div className="lg:hidden fixed inset-0 z-50 flex">
          <div className="w-72 max-w-[85vw] h-full shadow-2xl">
            {sidebarContent}
          </div>
          <div
            className="flex-1 bg-black/70 backdrop-blur-sm"
