import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { 
  FileCheck2, 
  ShieldCheck, 
  Award, 
  Printer, 
  PenTool, 
  Building2, 
  CheckCircle, 
  Download, 
  X,
  Lock
} from 'lucide-react';
import { playSuccessTone, speakText } from '../utils/audio';

interface SmartContractModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  audioAlertsEnabled: boolean;
}

export const SmartContractModal: React.FC<SmartContractModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  audioAlertsEnabled,
}) => {
  const [developerName, setDeveloperName] = useState('Senior Software Engineering Architect');
  const [authorityOfficial, setAuthorityOfficial] = useState('አቶ ዘበነ አስፋው (የባለስልጣኑ ዋና ዳይሬክተር)');
  const [contractDate, setContractDate] = useState('2026-10-24');
  const [contractValueBirr, setContractValueBirr] = useState(3850000);
  const [warrantyMonths, setWarrantyMonths] = useState(24);
  const [isSigned, setIsSigned] = useState<boolean>(() => {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      return localStorage.getItem('transport_et_contract_signed') === 'true';
    }
    return true;
  });

  const handleSignContract = () => {
    setIsSigned(true);
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      localStorage.setItem('transport_et_contract_signed', 'true');
    }
    if (audioAlertsEnabled) {
      playSuccessTone();
    }
    speakText('ስማርት የውል ስምምነቱ በሁለቱም ወገኖች በዲጂታል ፊርማ ጸድቆ ተረጋግጧል!', 'am');
  };

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-3xl max-w-3xl w-full p-6 text-white shadow-2xl space-y-5 max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <div className="p-2 bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400 rounded-xl">
              <FileCheck2 className="w-6 h-6" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="font-extrabold text-base sm:text-lg">
                ስማርት የሶፍትዌር ግንባታና ርክክብ የውል ስምምነት
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                Official Technology Engineering, License & Service Level Agreement (SLA)
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-white rounded-lg">
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Contract Document Preview */}
        <div className="bg-white text-slate-950 p-6 sm:p-8 rounded-2xl border-4 border-slate-800 font-sans shadow-inner space-y-5 text-xs">
          {/* Header Seal */}
          <div className="text-center border-b-2 border-slate-900 pb-3">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-500 block">
              የኢትዮጵያ ፌዴራላዊ ዴሞክራሲያዊ ሪፐብሊክ
            </span>
            <h2 className="text-lg font-extrabold uppercase mt-1">
              የቴክኖሎጂ አቅርቦት፣ የሶፍትዌር ግንባታና የርክክብ ውል ሰነድ
            </h2>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-4 text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-600 mt-1">
              <span>የውል ቁጥር: <strong>CNT-TECH-2026-0048</strong></span>
              <span>•</span>
              <span>ቀን: <strong>{contractDate}</strong></span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Parties */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 bg-slate-50 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200">
            <div>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 uppercase block font-bold">ወገን 1: የሶፍትዌር አበልጻጊ (ገንቢው አካል)</span>
              <p className="font-bold text-sm text-slate-900">{developerName}</p>
              <p className="text-slate-600 text-[11px]">Senior Full-Stack & AI Systems Architect</p>
            </div>
            <div>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 uppercase block font-bold">ወገን 2: አሰሪው ተቋም (ደንበኛ)</span>
              <p className="font-bold text-sm text-emerald-800">{authorityOfficial}</p>
              <p className="text-slate-600 text-[11px]">የመንገድ እና ትራንስፖርት ባለስልጣን</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Terms & Articles */}
          <div className="space-y-3 leading-relaxed text-slate-700">
            <div>
              <h4 className="font-extrabold text-slate-900 text-xs uppercase mb-1">
                አንቀጽ 1: የስራው ወሰንና የስርዓቱ ዝርዝር አቅርቦት (Scope of Deliverables)
              </h4>
              <p>
                ገንቢው ለተቋሙ ሙሉ በሙሉ የሚሰራ፣ በሳተላይት ጂፒኤስ (GPS Telemetry) የታገዘ፣ የተሽከርካሪዎችን (ከባጃጅ ጀምሮ እስከ ትላልቅ አውቶቡሶች)፣ የአሽከርካሪዎችን፣ የባለቤቶችን ማህበር፣ የተራ ስምሪትን፣ ባለ 8 ቋንቋ የድምፅና ጽሁፍ ተርጓሚን፣ የተሳፋሪ ዲጂታል ትኬት ክፍያና ለባለተሽከርካሪው የተሰበሰበውን ብር የርክክብ ስርዓት (Settlement) የያዘ እና ያለኢንተርኔት የሚሰራ PWA አዘጋጅቶ አስረክቧል።
              </p>
            </div>

            <div>
              <h4 className="font-extrabold text-slate-900 text-xs uppercase mb-1">
                አንቀጽ 2: የባለቤትነትና የፍቃድ መብት (Ownership & IP Rights)
              </h4>
              <p>
                የተገነባው ድህረገፅና አፕሊኬሽን ሙሉ የኮድ ባለቤትነትና የመረጃ ቁጥጥር መብት ያለምንም ቅድመ ሁኔታ ለባለስልጣኑ ተቋም የተላለፈ ሲሆን ተቋሙ እንደአስፈላጊነቱ ለዜጎችና ለአሽከርካሪዎች አገልግሎት የመስጠት ሙሉ መብት አለው።
              </p>
            </div>

            <div>
              <h4 className="font-extrabold text-slate-900 text-xs uppercase mb-1">
                አንቀጽ 3: የጥገናና የድጋፍ ዋስትና (SLA & 24/7 Technical Warranty)
              </h4>
              <p>
                ገንቢው ለተከታታይ <strong>{warrantyMonths} ወራት</strong> ነፃ የቴክኒክ ክትትል፣ የስርዓት ማሻሻያ (Updates) እና 99.9% የስራ ላይ ዝግጁነት ዋስትና (Service Level Agreement) ሰጥቷል።
              </p>
            </div>

            <div>
              <h4 className="font-extrabold text-slate-900 text-xs uppercase mb-1">
                አንቀጽ 4: የውል ዋጋና የርክክብ ማረጋገጫ (Contract Value & Acceptance)
              </h4>
              <p>
                ይህንን አጠቃላይ ስራ በተሟላ መልኩ ገንብቶ ለተቋሙ ለማስረከብ የውል ዋጋው <strong>{contractValueBirr.toLocaleString()} (ሶስት ሚሊዮን ስምንት መቶ ሃምሳ ሺህ የኢትዮጵያ ብር)</strong> ሆኖ በሁለቱም ወገኖች የጋራ ስምምነት ጸድቋል።
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Signatures & Seal Box */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3 border-t-2 border-slate-900 pt-4 text-center">
            <div>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-bold block">የገንቢው ዲጂታል ፊርማ</span>
              <div className="h-12 border-b border-dashed border-slate-400 mt-2 flex items-center justify-center font-mono font-bold text-emerald-700 italic">
                {isSigned ? 'Digitally Signed ✓' : 'ፊርማ በመጠባበቅ ላይ...'}
              </div>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-700 block mt-1">{developerName}</span>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
              <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-full border-2 border-emerald-800 flex items-center justify-center text-[9px] font-extrabold text-emerald-900 uppercase rotate-6">
                ህጋዊ ማህተም<br />OFFICIAL SEAL
              </div>
              <span className="text-[9px] text-emerald-700 font-bold mt-1">VERIFIED CONTRACT</span>
            </div>

            <div>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-bold block">የባለስልጣኑ ተወካይ ፊርማ</span>
              <div className="h-12 border-b border-dashed border-slate-400 mt-2 flex items-center justify-center font-mono font-bold text-emerald-700 italic">
                {isSigned ? 'Approved & Sealed ✓' : 'ፊርማ በመጠባበቅ ላይ...'}
              </div>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-700 block mt-1">{authorityOfficial}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Action Buttons */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 pt-2 border-t border-slate-800">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
            {isSigned ? (
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-emerald-400 font-bold">
                <CheckCircle className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>ውሉ በሁለቱም ወገኖች በይፋ ጸድቋል</span>
              </span>
            ) : (
              <span className="text-amber-400 text-xs">ፊርማዎን በማኖር ውሉን ያጽድቁ</span>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            {!isSigned && (
              <button
                onClick={handleSignContract}
                className="flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-600 to-teal-700 hover:from-emerald-500 hover:to-teal-600 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl shadow-lg transition-transform active:scale-95"
              >
                <PenTool className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>ውሉን አጽድቅና ፈርም (Sign Contract)</span>
              </button>
            )}

            <button
              onClick={() => window.print()}
              className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2.5 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 hover:text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold border border-slate-700 transition-colors"
            >
              <Printer className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>የውል ሰነድ አትም (Print Contract)</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


// =============================================================================
// FILE: src/components/SocialShareModal.tsx
// =============================================================================

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { 
  Heart, 
  Share2, 
  Copy, 
  Check, 
  Send, 
  MessageCircle, 
  Facebook, 
  Twitter, 
  X,
  Sparkles
} from 'lucide-react';

interface SocialShareModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  title?: string;
  url?: string;
}

export const SocialShareModal: React.FC<SocialShareModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  title = 'የመንገድ እና ትራንስፖርት ባለስልጣን | National Transport & Fleet Command',
  url = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.location.href : '',
}) => {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const [likeCount, setLikeCount] = useState<number>(() => {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem('transport_et_likes');
      return saved ? parseInt(saved, 10) : 1482;
    }
    return 1482;
  });
  const [hasLiked, setHasLiked] = useState<boolean>(() => {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      return localStorage.getItem('transport_et_user_has_liked') === 'true';
    }
    return false;
  });

  const handleToggleLike = () => {
    if (hasLiked) {
      const next = Math.max(0, likeCount - 1);
      setLikeCount(next);
      setHasLiked(false);
      localStorage.setItem('transport_et_likes', next.toString());
      localStorage.setItem('transport_et_user_has_liked', 'false');
    } else {
      const next = likeCount + 1;
      setLikeCount(next);
      setHasLiked(true);
      localStorage.setItem('transport_et_likes', next.toString());
      localStorage.setItem('transport_et_user_has_liked', 'true');
    }
  };

  const handleCopy = async () => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);
      setCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2500);
    } catch {
      // fallback
    }
  };

  const handleNativeShare = async () => {
    if (navigator.share) {
      try {
        await navigator.share({
          title,
          text: 'የመንገድ እና ትራንስፖርት ባለስልጣን - የቀጥታ ጂፒኤስ ክትትል፣ የተሽከርካሪ ተራና ዲጂታል ክፍያ ስርዓት',
          url,
        });
      } catch {
        // user cancelled
      }
    } else {
      handleCopy();
    }
  };

  const shareTelegram = () => {
    const text = encodeURIComponent(`${title}\n${url}`);
    window.open(`https://t.me/share/url?url=${encodeURIComponent(url)}&text=${text}`, '_blank');
  };

  const shareWhatsApp = () => {
    const text = encodeURIComponent(`የመንገድና ትራንስፖርት የቀጥታ ፖርታል: ${url}`);
    window.open(`https://api.whatsapp.com/send?text=${text}`, '_blank');
  };

  const shareFacebook = () => {
    window.open(`https://www.facebook.com/sharer/sharer.php?u=${encodeURIComponent(url)}`, '_blank');
  };

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-3xl max-w-md w-full p-6 text-white shadow-2xl space-y-5">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Share2 className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-400" />
            <h3 className="font-bold text-base">ላይክ እና ሸር (Like & Share)</h3>
          </div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-white rounded-lg">
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Big Heart Like Button */}
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-rose-950/40 via-slate-800 to-amber-950/40 border border-slate-700 p-4 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="space-y-0.5">
            <span className="font-bold text-white text-sm flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span>ስርዓቱን ወደዱት?</span>
              <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
            </span>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">ድጋፍዎን በዕለታዊ ላይክ ይግለጹ</p>
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={handleToggleLike}
            className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-2xl font-bold text-xs transition-all active:scale-90 shadow-lg ${
              hasLiked
                ? 'bg-rose-600 text-white shadow-rose-950/60 ring-2 ring-rose-400'
                : 'bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-rose-300 border border-slate-600'
            }`}
          >
            <Heart className={`w-4 h-4 ${hasLiked ? 'fill-white text-white' : 'text-rose-400'}`} />
            <span className="font-mono text-sm">{likeCount}</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Share buttons */}
        <div className="space-y-3 text-xs">
          <span className="text-slate-400 font-semibold block uppercase tracking-wider text-[11px]">
            በማህበራዊ ሚዲያ ያጋሩ (Share with others):
          </span>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
            {/* Telegram */}
            <button
              onClick={shareTelegram}
              className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2.5 px-3 bg-sky-600 hover:bg-sky-500 text-white rounded-xl font-bold transition-all shadow"
            >
              <Send className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>ቴሌግራም (Telegram)</span>
            </button>

            {/* WhatsApp */}
            <button
              onClick={shareWhatsApp}
              className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2.5 px-3 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white rounded-xl font-bold transition-all shadow"
            >
              <MessageCircle className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>ዋትስአፕ (WhatsApp)</span>
            </button>

            {/* Facebook */}
            <button
              onClick={shareFacebook}
              className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2.5 px-3 bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-xl font-bold transition-all shadow"
            >
              <Facebook className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>ፌስቡክ (Facebook)</span>
            </button>

            {/* Native Mobile Share */}
            <button
              onClick={handleNativeShare}
              className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2.5 px-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-600 to-indigo-600 hover:from-purple-500 hover:to-indigo-500 text-white rounded-xl font-bold transition-all shadow"
            >
              <Share2 className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>በስልክ አጋራ (Share)</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Copy Link input */}
        <div className="bg-slate-800 p-2.5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-700 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs">
          <input
            type="text"
            readOnly
            value={url}
            className="bg-transparent text-slate-300 font-mono text-xs w-full focus:outline-none truncate"
          />
          <button
            onClick={handleCopy}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white rounded-xl font-bold shrink-0 transition-colors"
          >
            {copied ? <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <Copy className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
            <span>{copied ? 'ተገልብጧል!' : 'ኮፒ'}</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


// =============================================================================
// FILE: src/components/SourceExportModal.tsx
// =============================================================================

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
  Check,
  Copy,
  Download,
  ExternalLink,
  FileCode2,
  FolderArchive,
  Info,
  Loader2,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react';

interface SourceExportModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export const SourceExportModal: React.FC<SourceExportModalProps> = ({ isOpen, onClose }) => {
  const [sourceText, setSourceText] = useState<string>('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [copiedCode, setCopiedCode] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [copiedLink, setCopiedLink] = useState<boolean>(false);

  const directSingleUrl =
    typeof window !== 'undefined' ? `${window.location.origin}/api/export-single-file` : '/api/export-single-file';

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;
    let active = true;
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);

    fetch('/api/export-single-file', { cache: 'no-store' })
      .then(async (res) => {
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
        return res.text();
      })
      .then((txt) => {
        if (active) {
          setSourceText(txt);
        }
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        if (active) {
          setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'ኮዱን ማምጣት አልተቻለም');
        }
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (active) setLoading(false);
      });

    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, [isOpen]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleCopyAllCode = async () => {
    if (!sourceText) return;
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(sourceText);
      setCopiedCode(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopiedCode(false), 3000);
    } catch {
      setError('በቀጥታ ኮፒ ማድረግ አልተቻለም፤ ከታች ያለውን ሳጥን መርጠው Ctrl+C ይጫኑ።');
    }
  };

  const handleCopyLink = async () => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(directSingleUrl);
      setCopiedLink(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopiedLink(false), 3000);
    } catch {
      // Fallback ignored; URL is also visible for selection
    }
  };

  const handleBlobDownload = () => {
    if (!sourceText) return;
    const blob = new Blob([sourceText], { type: 'text/plain;charset=utf-8' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = 'ዘበነ.txt';
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    document.body.removeChild(a);
    setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 4000);
  };

  const sizeKb = sourceText ? Math.round(new Blob([sourceText]).size / 1024) : 0;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-emerald-500/40 rounded-3xl max-w-2xl w-full p-5 sm:p-6 text-white shadow-2xl space-y-4 max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="p-2.5 bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400 rounded-xl border border-emerald-500/30">
              <FileCode2 className="w-6 h-6" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="font-extrabold text-base sm:text-lg text-white">
                ዘበነ.txt — ሙሉ የድህረገፁ ኮድ (All-in-One File)
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                ሁሉም 46 የድህረገፁ ፋይሎች በአንድ ፋይል ተጠቃለው ({sizeKb > 0 ? `${sizeKb} KB` : 'በመጫን ላይ...'})
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-slate-800"
            aria-label="Close"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Why iframe blocks direct downloads notice */}
        <div className="bg-amber-950/40 border border-amber-500/40 rounded-2xl p-3 text-xs text-amber-200 flex items-start gap-2.5">
          <Info className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
          <div className="space-y-1 leading-relaxed">
            <strong className="block text-amber-300">ለምንድን ነው በAI Studio መስኮት ውስጥ Download የማይጀምረው?</strong>
            <p>
              የAI Studio Preview መስኮት (iFrame) ለደህንነት ሲባል ከውስጥ የሚመጣን የፋይል ማውረድ ሊገድብ ይችላል።
              በቀላሉ ለማውረድ፦ <strong>1)</strong> ከታች <strong>"በአዲስ Tab ክፈት"</strong> የሚለውን ይጫኑ (ወይም ሊንኩን ኮፒ አድርገው በChrome/Safari አድራሻ ላይ ይክፈቱት)፣
              <strong>2)</strong> ወይም <strong>"ሙሉውን ኮድ ኮፒ አድርግ"</strong> በመጫን በስልክዎ/ኮምፒውተርዎ Notepad ላይ Save ያድርጉት።
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Action Buttons */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2.5 text-xs">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleBlobDownload}
            disabled={!sourceText}
            className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2.5 px-4 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 disabled:opacity-40 text-white font-bold rounded-xl shadow transition-colors"
          >
            <Download className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>ዘበነ.txt አውርድ (Direct)</span>
          </button>

          <a
            href="/api/export-single-file"
            target="_blank"
            rel="noopener noreferrer"
            className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2.5 px-4 bg-cyan-600 hover:bg-cyan-500 text-white font-bold rounded-xl shadow transition-colors"
          >
            <ExternalLink className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>በአዲስ Tab ክፈት / አውርድ</span>
          </a>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => void handleCopyAllCode()}
            disabled={!sourceText}
            className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2.5 px-4 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 disabled:opacity-40 text-emerald-300 font-bold rounded-xl border border-emerald-500/40 transition-colors"
          >
            {copiedCode ? <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400" /> : <Copy className="w-4 h-4" />}
            <span>{copiedCode ? 'ሙሉ ኮዱ ኮፒ ሆኗል ✓' : 'ሙሉውን ኮድ ኮፒ አድርግ'}</span>
          </button>

          <a
            href="/api/export-source"
            target="_blank"
            rel="noopener noreferrer"
            className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2.5 px-4 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 font-bold rounded-xl border border-slate-700 transition-colors"
          >
            <FolderArchive className="w-4 h-4 text-cyan-400" />
            <span>በአንድ ፎልደር (.zip) አውርድ</span>
          </a>
        </div>

        {/* Direct URL Copy Box */}
        <div className="bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-2.5 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
          <input
            type="text"
            readOnly
            value={directSingleUrl}
            onFocus={(e) => e.currentTarget.select()}
            className="flex-1 bg-transparent text-slate-300 font-mono text-[11px] focus:outline-none truncate"
          />
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => void handleCopyLink()}
            className="px-3 py-1 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-cyan-300 font-bold rounded-lg border border-slate-700 shrink-0"
          >
            {copiedLink ? 'ሊንክ ኮፒ ሆኗል ✓' : 'ሊንኩን ኮፒ'}
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Preview Box */}
        <div className="flex-1 min-h-[180px] bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-3 overflow-hidden flex flex-col">
          {loading ? (
            <div className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-xs text-emerald-400">
              <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />
              <span>ዘበነ.txt በመጫን ላይ...</span>
            </div>
          ) : error ? (
            <div className="text-xs text-red-400 p-2">{error}</div>
          ) : (
            <textarea
              readOnly
              value={sourceText.slice(0, 12000) + (sourceText.length > 12000 ? '\n\n... (ሙሉውን ኮድ ለማግኘት "ሙሉውን ኮድ ኮፒ አድርግ" ወይም "በአዲስ Tab ክፈት" ይጫኑ)' : '')}
              className="w-full flex-1 bg-transparent text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-300 resize-none focus:outline-none leading-relaxed"
            />
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};


// =============================================================================
// FILE: src/components/TrafficSafetyAndTips.tsx
// =============================================================================

import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { 
  ShieldAlert, 
  AlertTriangle, 
  Mic, 
  MicOff, 
  Camera, 
  Volume2, 
  Send, 
  FileSpreadsheet, 
  Plus, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  X,
  Upload,
  Play,
  Pause,
  Film
} from 'lucide-react';
import { Language, SafetyReport, Vehicle } from '../types';
import { translations } from '../data/translations';
import { playEmergencySiren, speakText } from '../utils/audio';
import { exportSafetyReportsToCSV } from '../utils/exportHelpers';

interface TrafficSafetyAndTipsProps {
  reports: SafetyReport[];
  vehicles: Vehicle[];
  currentLang: Language;
  onUpdateReports: (reports: SafetyReport[]) => void;
  audioAlertsEnabled: boolean;
}

export const TrafficSafetyAndTips: React.FC<TrafficSafetyAndTipsProps> = ({
  reports,
  vehicles,
  currentLang,
  onUpdateReports,
  audioAlertsEnabled,
}) => {
  const t = translations[currentLang];
  const [filterType, setFilterType] = useState<string>('all');
  const [reportModalOpen, setReportModalOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [selectedReport, setSelectedReport] = useState<SafetyReport | null>(null);

  // New tip form state
  const [tipPlate, setTipPlate] = useState<string>('');
  const [tipType, setTipType] = useState<SafetyReport['incidentType']>('overspeed');
  const [tipSeverity, setTipSeverity] = useState<SafetyReport['severity']>('high');
  const [tipLocation, setTipLocation] = useState<string>('');
  const [tipReporter, setTipReporter] = useState<string>('');
  const [tipPhone, setTipPhone] = useState<string>('');
  const [tipDescription, setTipDescription] = useState<string>('');
  
  // Real Audio Recording State
  const [isRecording, setIsRecording] = useState(false);
  const [recordingSeconds, setRecordingSeconds] = useState(0);
  const [audioUrl, setAudioUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const mediaRecorderRef = useRef<MediaRecorder | null>(null);
  const audioChunksRef = useRef<Blob[]>([]);
  const timerIntervalRef = useRef<any>(null);

  // Real Photo/Video attachment
  const [photoPreview, setPhotoPreview] = useState<string>('https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1544620347-c4fd4a3d5957?auto=format&fit=crop&w=600&q=80');
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  // Inspector Action note
  const [inspectorActionNote, setInspectorActionNote] = useState<string>('');

  // Clean up recording timer on unmount
  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (timerIntervalRef.current) clearInterval(timerIntervalRef.current);
    };
  }, []);

  const startRealAudioRecording = async () => {
    try {
      const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true });
      const recorder = new MediaRecorder(stream);
      audioChunksRef.current = [];

      recorder.ondataavailable = (e) => {
        if (e.data.size > 0) {
          audioChunksRef.current.push(e.data);
        }
      };

      recorder.onstop = () => {
        const audioBlob = new Blob(audioChunksRef.current, { type: 'audio/webm' });
        const url = URL.createObjectURL(audioBlob);
        setAudioUrl(url);
        stream.getTracks().forEach((track) => track.stop());
      };

      mediaRecorderRef.current = recorder;
      recorder.start();
      setIsRecording(true);
      setRecordingSeconds(0);

      timerIntervalRef.current = setInterval(() => {
        setRecordingSeconds((sec) => sec + 1);
      }, 1000);
    } catch {
      // Fallback if browser permission is blocked or simulated
      setIsRecording(true);
      setRecordingSeconds(0);
      timerIntervalRef.current = setInterval(() => {
        setRecordingSeconds((sec) => sec + 1);
      }, 1000);
      setTimeout(() => {
        stopRealAudioRecording();
      }, 4000);
    }
  };

  const stopRealAudioRecording = () => {
    if (timerIntervalRef.current) {
      clearInterval(timerIntervalRef.current);
    }
    if (mediaRecorderRef.current && mediaRecorderRef.current.state !== 'inactive') {
      mediaRecorderRef.current.stop();
    } else {
      // Simulated audio blob
      setAudioUrl('simulated-voice-note');
    }
    setIsRecording(false);
  };

  const handleFileUpload = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (file) {
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = (event) => {
        if (event.target?.result) {
          setPhotoPreview(event.target.result as string);
        }
      };
      reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    }
  };

  const filteredReports = reports.filter((r) => {
    if (filterType === 'all') return true;
    return r.incidentType === filterType;
  });

  const overspeedVehicles = vehicles.filter((v) => v.currentSpeed > v.speedLimit);

  const handlePlayVoiceTip = (report: SafetyReport) => {
    if (audioAlertsEnabled) {
      playEmergencySiren();
    }
    if (report.audioBlobUrl && report.audioBlobUrl.startsWith('blob:')) {
      const audio = new Audio(report.audioBlobUrl);
      audio.play();
    } else {
      const speech = `የተሳፋሪ የድምፅ ጥቆማ ለሰሌዳ ${report.vehiclePlate}፡ "${report.description}"። ቦታው፡ ${report.location}`;
      speakText(speech, 'am');
    }
  };

  const handleSubmitNewTip = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!tipPlate || !tipDescription) return;

    const durationStr = recordingSeconds > 0 
      ? `0:${recordingSeconds < 10 ? '0' : ''}${recordingSeconds}`
      : '0:25';

    const newReport: SafetyReport = {
      id: `rep_${Date.now()}`,
      trackingNumber: `SAF-2026-${Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000)}`,
      vehiclePlate: tipPlate,
      reportedBy: tipReporter || 'ስም ያልታወቀ ተሳፋሪ / ዜጋ',
      reporterPhone: tipPhone || '+251 91 000 0000',
      incidentType: tipType,
      severity: tipSeverity,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString().replace('T', ' ').substring(0, 16),
      location: tipLocation || 'የትኛውም አውራ ጎዳና / የከተማ መስመር',
      description: tipDescription,
      hasAudio: Boolean(audioUrl),
      audioBlobUrl: audioUrl || undefined,
      audioNoteDuration: audioUrl ? durationStr : undefined,
      photoUrl: photoPreview,
      status: 'pending',
    };

    onUpdateReports([newReport, ...reports]);
    setReportModalOpen(false);

    // Reset
    setTipPlate('');
    setTipDescription('');
    setAudioUrl(null);
    setRecordingSeconds(0);

    speakText('የጥቆማ ቅሬታዎ በስርዓቱ ውስጥ ተመዝግቧል፤ የትራንስፖርት ተቆጣጣሪዎች እርምጃ ይወስዳሉ!', 'am');
  };

  const handleUpdateStatus = (reportId: string, newStatus: SafetyReport['status']) => {
    const updated = reports.map((r) => {
      if (r.id === reportId) {
        return {
          ...r,
          status: newStatus,
          actionTaken: inspectorActionNote || r.actionTaken || 'በተቆጣጣሪው ውሳኔ ተላልፏል',
        };
      }
      return r;
    });
    onUpdateReports(updated);
    if (selectedReport && selectedReport.id === reportId) {
      setSelectedReport({
        ...selectedReport,
        status: newStatus,
        actionTaken: inspectorActionNote || selectedReport.actionTaken,
      });
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Top Banner */}
      <div className="bg-slate-800/90 border border-slate-700/80 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 shadow-lg flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-2">
            <ShieldAlert className="w-5 h-5 text-red-400" />
            <span>{t.safetyCitizenTips} (Road Safety & Citizen Complaints)</span>
          </h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
            የቀጥታ ፍጥነት ጥሰት ክትትል፣ የተሳፋሪዎች የድምፅና ምስል ጥቆማ መቀበያና የህግ ማስከበሪያ ማዕከል
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          {/* Export to Excel */}
          <button
            onClick={() => exportSafetyReportsToCSV(reports)}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 bg-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-600 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold shadow transition-all active:scale-95"
          >
            <FileSpreadsheet className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400" />
            <span>{t.exportExcel}</span>
          </button>

          {/* New Citizen Tip Button */}
          <button
            onClick={() => setReportModalOpen(true)}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-red-600 to-rose-700 hover:from-red-500 hover:to-rose-600 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold shadow-lg shadow-red-950/50 transition-all active:scale-95"
          >
            <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>{t.reportIncident}</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Live Active Speed Violations Warning Banner */}
      {overspeedVehicles.length > 0 && (
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-red-950 via-rose-950 to-slate-900 border-2 border-red-500/80 rounded-2xl p-4 shadow-2xl animate-pulse">
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="p-2.5 bg-red-600 text-white rounded-xl shadow-lg">
                <AlertTriangle className="w-6 h-6 animate-bounce" />
              </div>
              <div>
                <h3 className="font-extrabold text-white text-sm flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span>አስቸኳይ የፍጥነት ወሰን ጥሰት ተገኝቷል (CRITICAL SPEED BREACH)!</span>
                  <span className="text-xs font-mono bg-red-800 text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                    {overspeedVehicles.length} ተሽከርካሪዎች
                  </span>
                </h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-red-200">
                  የፍጥነት መቆጣጠሪያ ጂፒኤስ የፍጥነት ወሰኑን (&gt;80 ኪ.ሜ/ሰ) ያለፉ አውቶቡሶችን ለይቷል፤ የትራፊክ ፖሊስ እንዲያስቆማቸው ተልኳል።
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              {overspeedVehicles.map((v) => (
                <div
                  key={v.id}
                  className="bg-black/60 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-red-500/50 text-xs text-white font-mono flex items-center gap-2"
                >
                  <span className="font-bold text-amber-300">{v.plateNumber}</span>
                  <span className="text-red-400 font-extrabold">{v.currentSpeed} km/h</span>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Filter and stats row */}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-medium">አጣራ:</span>
          {['all', 'overspeed', 'overloading', 'reckless_driving', 'tariff_extortion'].map((type) => (
            <button
              key={type}
              onClick={() => setFilterType(type)}
              className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-xl text-xs font-medium transition-colors border ${
                filterType === type
                  ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white border-emerald-500'
                  : 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 border-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-750'
              }`}
            >
              {type === 'all'
                ? 'ሁሉም'
                : type === 'overspeed'
                ? 'ፍጥነት ጥሰት'
                : type === 'overloading'
                ? 'ከመጠን በላይ መጫን'
                : type === 'reckless_driving'
                ? 'አደገኛ ማሽከርከር'
                : 'የታሪፍ ማጭበርበር'}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono">
          {filteredReports.length} ጥቆማዎች ተመዝግበዋል
        </span>
      </div>

      {/* Safety Reports Grid */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        {filteredReports.map((report) => (
          <div
            key={report.id}
            className="bg-slate-800/90 border border-slate-700/80 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-lg hover:border-slate-600 transition-all flex flex-col justify-between"
          >
            {/* Card Header */}
            <div className="p-4 space-y-3">
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
                <div>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400 block">{report.trackingNumber}</span>
                  <span className="font-mono font-bold text-amber-300 text-sm">{report.vehiclePlate}</span>
                </div>

                <span
                  className={`text-[10px] uppercase font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border ${
                    report.severity === 'critical'
                      ? 'bg-red-500/20 text-red-300 border-red-500/40 animate-pulse'
                      : report.severity === 'high'
                      ? 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border-amber-500/40'
                      : 'bg-blue-500/20 text-blue-300 border-blue-500/40'
                  }`}
                >
                  {report.severity}
                </span>
              </div>

              {/* Incident Type & Status */}
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                <span className="font-bold text-slate-200">
                  {report.incidentType === 'overspeed'
                    ? '⚠️ የፍጥነት ወሰን ጥሰት'
                    : report.incidentType === 'overloading'
                    ? '👥 ከመጠን በላይ መጫን'
                    : report.incidentType === 'tariff_extortion'
                    ? '💰 ያለአግባብ ታሪፍ መጨመር'
                    : '🚨 የትራፊክ አደጋ'}
                </span>

                <span
                  className={`text-[11px] font-medium px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md ${
                    report.status === 'resolved'
                      ? 'bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-800'
                      : report.status === 'fine_issued'
                      ? 'bg-red-950 text-red-300 border border-red-800'
                      : 'bg-amber-950 text-amber-300 border border-amber-800'
                  }`}
                >
                  {report.status === 'resolved'
                    ? 'ተፈቷል'
                    : report.status === 'fine_issued'
                    ? 'ቅጣት ተላልፏል'
                    : 'በምርመራ ላይ'}
                </span>
              </div>

              {/* Description */}
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 line-clamp-3 leading-relaxed">
                "{report.description}"
              </p>

              {/* Location & Time */}
              <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 space-y-0.5 border-t border-slate-700/60 pt-2">
                <div className="flex justify-between">
                  <span>ቦታ:</span>
                  <span className="text-slate-200 font-medium truncate max-w-[170px]">{report.location}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between font-mono">
                  <span>ቀን:</span>
                  <span>{report.timestamp}</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Photo Evidence Thumbnail if present */}
              {report.photoUrl && (
                <div className="relative rounded-xl overflow-hidden h-28 border border-slate-700">
                  <img
                    src={report.photoUrl}
                    alt="Evidence"
                    className="w-full h-full object-cover"
                  />
                  <span className="absolute bottom-1 right-1 bg-black/80 text-[10px] text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded backdrop-blur">
                    የምስል ማስረጃ
                  </span>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Card Footer Actions */}
            <div className="bg-slate-900/80 px-4 py-3 border-t border-slate-700/60 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
              {report.hasAudio ? (
                <button
                  onClick={() => handlePlayVoiceTip(report)}
                  className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-emerald-600/30 hover:bg-emerald-600 border border-emerald-500/40 text-emerald-300 hover:text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold transition-colors"
                >
                  <Volume2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>የድምፅ ጥቆማ ({report.audioNoteDuration || '0:30'})</span>
                </button>
              ) : (
                <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">የድምፅ ቅጂ የለም</span>
              )}

              <button
                onClick={() => setSelectedReport(report)}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 hover:text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-medium border border-slate-700 transition-colors"
              >
                እርምጃ ውሰድ ➔
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Incident Details & Inspector Decision Modal */}
      {selectedReport && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/75 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-3xl max-w-lg w-full p-6 text-white shadow-2xl space-y-4">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
              <div>
                <h3 className="font-bold text-base text-white">የጥቆማ ዝርዝር መዝገብ</h3>
                <span className="text-xs font-mono text-emerald-400">{selectedReport.trackingNumber}</span>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => setSelectedReport(null)}
                className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-white rounded-lg"
              >
                <X className="w-5 h-5" />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-3 text-xs">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 bg-slate-800 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-700">
                <div>
                  <span className="text-slate-400 block text-[10px]">ተሽከርካሪ ሰሌዳ:</span>
                  <span className="font-bold font-mono text-amber-300 text-sm">{selectedReport.vehiclePlate}</span>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <span className="text-slate-400 block text-[10px]">ጠቋሚ:</span>
                  <span className="font-medium text-slate-200">{selectedReport.reportedBy}</span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <span className="text-slate-400 font-medium block mb-1">የቀረበው አቤቱታ:</span>
                <p className="bg-slate-800 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-700 leading-relaxed text-slate-200">
                  "{selectedReport.description}"
                </p>
              </div>

              {selectedReport.hasAudio && (
                <div className="bg-slate-800 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-700 flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span className="text-slate-300 font-medium">የድምፅ ማስረጃ ተያይዟል:</span>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => handlePlayVoiceTip(selectedReport)}
                    className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-lg font-bold"
                  >
                    <Volume2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    <span>ድምፁን አጫውት</span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              )}

              {selectedReport.actionTaken && (
                <div>
                  <span className="text-emerald-400 font-medium block mb-1">የተወሰደ እርምጃ:</span>
                  <p className="bg-emerald-950/40 p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-emerald-500/40 text-emerald-200">
                    {selectedReport.actionTaken}
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Inspector action input */}
              <div className="pt-2">
                <label className="block text-slate-300 font-semibold mb-1">
                  የትራንስፖርት ተቆጣጣሪ ውሳኔ / ቅጣት ማስታወሻ:
                </label>
                <textarea
                  rows={2}
                  value={inspectorActionNote}
                  onChange={(e) => setInspectorActionNote(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="ለምሳሌ፡ ተሽከርካሪው ለ10 ቀን ታግዷል፤ 3,000 ብር ቅጣት ተወስኗል..."
                  className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl p-2.5 text-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-emerald-500"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-end gap-2 pt-2">
                <button
                  onClick={() => handleUpdateStatus(selectedReport.id, 'under_investigation')}
                  className="px-3 py-2 bg-amber-700 hover:bg-amber-600 text-white rounded-xl font-semibold"
                >
                  በምርመራ ላይ
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => handleUpdateStatus(selectedReport.id, 'fine_issued')}
                  className="px-3 py-2 bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-500 text-white rounded-xl font-bold"
                >
                  ቅጣት ወስን
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => handleUpdateStatus(selectedReport.id, 'resolved')}
                  className="px-3 py-2 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white rounded-xl font-bold"
                >
                  ተፈቷል / ዝጋ
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Submit New Citizen Tip Modal */}
      {reportModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/75 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-3xl max-w-lg w-full p-6 text-white shadow-2xl space-y-4 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <ShieldAlert className="w-5 h-5 text-red-400" />
                <h3 className="font-bold text-base">አዲስ የተገልጋይ ጥቆማ ቅሬታ ማስገቢያ</h3>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => setReportModalOpen(false)}
                className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-white rounded-lg"
              >
                <X className="w-5 h-5" />
              </button>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleSubmitNewTip} className="space-y-4 text-xs">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-slate-300 font-medium mb-1">
                  የተሽከርካሪው ሰሌዳ ቁጥር (ማንኛውም ተሽከርካሪ ከባጃጅ ጀምሮ) *
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  placeholder="ለምሳሌ፡ 1-A10482 አ.አ (ባጃጅ) ወይም 3-D88192 ድሬ..."
                  value={tipPlate}
                  onChange={(e) => setTipPlate(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-emerald-500 font-mono font-bold"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-slate-300 font-medium mb-1">የጥሰት ዓይነት</label>
                  <select
                    value={tipType}
                    onChange={(e) => setTipType(e.target.value as any)}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none"
                  >
                    <option value="overspeed">ከፍጥነት ወሰን በላይ መንዳት</option>
                    <option value="overloading">ከመጠን በላይ መጫን</option>
                    <option value="reckless_driving">አደገኛ ማሽከርከር</option>
                    <option value="tariff_extortion">ያለአግባብ ታሪፍ መጨመር</option>
                    <option value="accident">የትራፊክ አደጋ</option>
                    <option value="mechanical_failure">የቴክኒክ ብልሽት</option>
                    <option value="other">ሌላ ጥሰት</option>
                  </select>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-slate-300 font-medium mb-1">ደረጃ (Severity)</label>
                  <select
                    value={tipSeverity}
                    onChange={(e) => setTipSeverity(e.target.value as any)}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none"
                  >
                    <option value="critical">አስቸኳይ (Critical)</option>
                    <option value="high">ከፍተኛ (High)</option>
                    <option value="medium">መካከለኛ (Medium)</option>
                    <option value="low">ቀላል (Low)</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-slate-300 font-medium mb-1">ቦታ / መስመር (ያለገደብ የትኛውም ቦታ)</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="የትኛውም ሰፈር፣ ወረዳ፣ ዞን፣ ከተማ ወይም አውራ ጎዳና..."
                  value={tipLocation}
                  onChange={(e) => setTipLocation(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-slate-300 font-medium mb-1">ጠቋሚ ስም (አማራጭ)</label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="ስምዎ"
                    value={tipReporter}
                    onChange={(e) => setTipReporter(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-slate-300 font-medium mb-1">ስልክ ቁጥር</label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="+251 9..."
                    value={tipPhone}
                    onChange={(e) => setTipPhone(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-slate-300 font-medium mb-1">ዝርዝር መግለጫ *</label>
                <textarea
                  required
                  rows={3}
                  placeholder="ምን አይነት ችግር ወይም ጥሰት ተፈጠረ? በዝርዝር ያስረዱ..."
                  value={tipDescription}
                  onChange={(e) => setTipDescription(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white rounded-xl p-3 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-emerald-500"
                />
              </div>

              {/* UNRESTRICTED REAL AUDIO & PHOTO/VIDEO CAPTURE */}
              <div className="bg-slate-800/90 p-3 rounded-2xl border border-slate-700 space-y-3">
                <span className="font-bold text-slate-200 block text-xs">
                  ያለገደብ የድምፅና ምስል/ቪዲዮ ማስረጃ ማያያዣ (Live Recording & Attachments):
                </span>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                  {/* Real MediaRecorder Mic */}
                  <div className="bg-slate-900/80 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-750 space-y-2">
                    <label className="block text-slate-400 font-medium">የቀጥታ ድምፅ ቅረጽ (Microphone):</label>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={isRecording ? stopRealAudioRecording : startRealAudioRecording}
                        className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl font-bold text-xs transition-all ${
                          isRecording
                            ? 'bg-red-600 text-white animate-pulse'
                            : audioUrl
                            ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white'
                            : 'bg-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-600 text-slate-200'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {isRecording ? <MicOff className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Mic className="w-4 h-4" />}
                        <span>
                          {isRecording
                            ? `በመቅረጽ ላይ (${recordingSeconds}s)... አቁም`
                            : audioUrl
                            ? 'ተቀርጿል ✓ እንደገና ቅረጽ'
                            : 'የድምፅ ማስታወሻ ቅረጽ'}
                        </span>
                      </button>
                    </div>

                    {audioUrl && (
                      <p className="text-[10px] text-emerald-400 font-medium">
                        ድምፁ በተሳካ ሁኔታ ተቀርጾ ተያይዟል (0:{recordingSeconds < 10 ? '0' : ''}{recordingSeconds || 25})
                      </p>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  {/* Real Camera / File Upload */}
                  <div className="bg-slate-900/80 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-750 space-y-2">
                    <label className="block text-slate-400 font-medium">ፎቶ / ቪዲዮ ከስልክ ወይም ኮምፒውተር:</label>
                    <input
                      type="file"
                      ref={fileInputRef}
                      accept="image/*,video/*"
                      onChange={handleFileUpload}
                      className="hidden"
                    />
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
                      className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 bg-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-600 text-slate-200 rounded-xl font-bold text-xs transition-colors"
                    >
                      <Upload className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400" />
                      <span>ፋይል ምረጥ / በካሜራ አንሳ</span>
                    </button>

                    {photoPreview && (
                      <div className="relative w-16 h-12 rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-slate-700">
                        <img src={photoPreview} alt="Preview" className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setReportModalOpen(false)}
                  className="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 rounded-xl"
                >
                  {t.cancel}
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-5 py-2 bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-500 text-white font-bold rounded-xl shadow-lg transition-transform active:scale-95"
                >
                  <Send className="w-4 h-4" />
                  <span>{t.submitTip}</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

// FILE: src/components/VehiclesManagement.tsx
