import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { 
  Truck, 
  Search, 
  Plus, 
  FileSpreadsheet, 
  User, 
  Fuel,
  Trash2,
  X,
  BookOpen,
  QrCode,
  ShieldCheck,
  MapPin,
  Printer,
  CheckCircle2
} from 'lucide-react';
import { Language, Vehicle } from '../types';
import { translations } from '../data/translations';
import { exportVehiclesToCSV } from '../utils/exportHelpers';
import { 
  ALL_VEHICLE_MODELS, 
  COMPLETE_VEHICLE_CATALOG, 
  getVehicleEmoji, 
  getVehicleTypeLabel 
} from '../data/vehicleCatalog';
import { ETHIOPIA_PLACES, resolveExactLocation } from '../utils/geoLocator';

interface VehiclesManagementProps {
  vehicles: Vehicle[];
  currentLang: Language;
  onUpdateVehicles: (vehicles: Vehicle[]) => void;
}

export const VehiclesManagement: React.FC<VehiclesManagementProps> = ({
  vehicles,
  currentLang,
  onUpdateVehicles,
}) => {
  const t = translations[currentLang];
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [filterType, setFilterType] = useState('all');
  const [filterStatus, setFilterStatus] = useState('all');
  const [isAddModalOpen, setIsAddModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isDirectoryOpen, setIsDirectoryOpen] = useState(false);
  const [directorySearch, setDirectorySearch] = useState('');
  const [qrModalVehicle, setQrModalVehicle] = useState<Vehicle | null>(null);

  // New vehicle form state
  const [newPlate, setNewPlate] = useState('');
  const [newMakeModel, setNewMakeModel] = useState('Bajaj RE 4-Stroke (Compact)');
  const [newType, setNewType] = useState<string>('bajaj');
  const [newYear, setNewYear] = useState(2025);
  const [newCapacity, setNewCapacity] = useState(3);
  const [newOwner, setNewOwner] = useState('የግል ባለንብረት');
  const [newDriver, setNewDriver] = useState('');
  const [newPhone, setNewPhone] = useState('+251 9');
  const [newOrigin, setNewOrigin] = useState('አዲስ አበባ');
  const [newDestination, setNewDestination] = useState('መዳረሻ');
  const [newSpeedLimit, setNewSpeedLimit] = useState(50);
  const [newLimiterId] = useState(`GPS-${Math.floor(10000 + Math.random() * 90000)}`);

  const handleTypeChange = (typeVal: string) => {
    setNewType(typeVal);
    const cat = COMPLETE_VEHICLE_CATALOG.find((c) => c.type === typeVal);
    if (cat) {
      setNewCapacity(cat.defaultCapacity);
      setNewSpeedLimit(cat.defaultSpeedLimit);
      setNewMakeModel(cat.models[0] || '');
    }
  };

  const handlePickFromCatalog = (typeVal: string, modelName: string) => {
    const cat = COMPLETE_VEHICLE_CATALOG.find((c) => c.type === typeVal);
    setNewType(typeVal);
    setNewMakeModel(modelName);
    if (cat) {
      setNewCapacity(cat.defaultCapacity);
      setNewSpeedLimit(cat.defaultSpeedLimit);
    }
    setIsDirectoryOpen(false);
    setIsAddModalOpen(true);
  };

  const filteredVehicles = vehicles.filter((v) => {
    const matchesSearch =
      v.plateNumber.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      v.driverName.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      v.makeModel.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      v.ownerName.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      (v.origin && v.origin.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase())) ||
      (v.destination && v.destination.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()));

    const matchesType = filterType === 'all' || v.vehicleType === filterType;
    const matchesStatus = filterStatus === 'all' || v.status === filterStatus;

    return matchesSearch && matchesType && matchesStatus;
  });

  const handleCreateVehicle = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!newPlate || !newMakeModel) return;

    // Match origin city coordinates if typed
    const matchedOriginPlace = ETHIOPIA_PLACES.find((p) =>
      p.nameAm.toLowerCase().includes(newOrigin.toLowerCase()) ||
      p.nameEn.toLowerCase().includes(newOrigin.toLowerCase())
    );
    const startLat = matchedOriginPlace ? matchedOriginPlace.lat : 9.0222;
    const startLng = matchedOriginPlace ? matchedOriginPlace.lng : 38.7468;
    const locInfo = resolveExactLocation(startLat, startLng);

    const nowTime = new Date().toLocaleTimeString('en-GB', {
      hour12: false,
      timeZone: 'Africa/Addis_Ababa',
    });

    const newVeh: Vehicle = {
      id: `veh_${Date.now()}`,
      plateNumber: newPlate,
      vehicleType: newType,
      makeModel: newMakeModel,
      year: Number(newYear),
      capacity: Number(newCapacity),
      ownerId: `own_${Date.now()}`,
      ownerName: newOwner || 'የግል ባለንብረት',
      driverId: `drv_${Date.now()}`,
      driverName: newDriver || 'ያልተመደበ አሽከርካሪ',
      driverPhone: newPhone,
      speedLimiterId: newLimiterId,
      status: 'on_route',
      inspectionStatus: 'passed',
      inspectionExpiry: '2028-12-30',
      fuelLevel: 90,
      currentSpeed: 42,
      speedLimit: Number(newSpeedLimit),
      lat: startLat,
      lng: startLng,
      heading: 90,
      currentLocationName: locInfo.formattedLabel,
      currentLocationImage: locInfo.imageUrl,
      trail: [
        {
          lat: startLat,
          lng: startLng,
          speed: 42,
          timestamp: nowTime,
          placeName: locInfo.formattedLabel,
        },
      ],
      origin: newOrigin || 'ያልተወሰነ',
      destination: newDestination || 'ያልተወሰነ',
      routeName: `${newOrigin || 'መነሻ'} ➔ ${newDestination || 'መዳረሻ'}`,
      lastUpdate: `${nowTime} EAT`,
    };

    onUpdateVehicles([newVeh, ...vehicles]);
    setIsAddModalOpen(false);
    setNewPlate('');
    setNewDriver('');
  };

  const handleDeleteVehicle = (id: string) => {
    if (confirm('ተሽከርካሪውን ከስርዓቱ ማውጣት ይፈልጋሉ?')) {
      onUpdateVehicles(vehicles.filter((v) => v.id !== id));
    }
  };

  const selectedCategoryObj = COMPLETE_VEHICLE_CATALOG.find((c) => c.type === newType);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Banner */}
      <div className="bg-slate-900/95 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 shadow-lg flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-2">
            <Truck className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-400" />
            <span>{t.vehicles} (ጠቅላላ የተሽከርካሪዎች ዝርዝርና መዝገብ)</span>
          </h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
            ከባጃጅ እና ሞተርሳይክል ጀምሮ ዳማስ፣ ኤሌክትሪክ ታክሲ፣ ሚኒባስ፣ አንበሳ/ሸገር ባስ፣ ቪአይፒ አውቶቡስ፣ ፒካፕ፣ ቦቴ፣ ተሳቢ ጭነት እና አምቡላንስን ሙሉ በሙሉ ያካተተ
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          {/* Complete Vehicle Name Directory Button */}
          <button
            onClick={() => setIsDirectoryOpen(true)}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 bg-cyan-600/20 hover:bg-cyan-600/30 text-cyan-300 border border-cyan-500/40 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold shadow transition-all"
          >
            <BookOpen className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>ሙሉ የተሽከርካሪዎች ስም መዝገብ ({ALL_VEHICLE_MODELS.length}+ ሞዴሎች)</span>
          </button>

          {/* Export to Excel */}
          <button
            onClick={() => exportVehiclesToCSV(vehicles)}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-white border border-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold shadow transition-all active:scale-95"
          >
            <FileSpreadsheet className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400" />
            <span>{t.exportExcel}</span>
          </button>

          {/* Add Vehicle Button */}
          <button
            onClick={() => setIsAddModalOpen(true)}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-600 to-teal-700 hover:from-emerald-500 hover:to-teal-600 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold shadow-lg shadow-emerald-950/50 transition-all active:scale-95"
          >
            <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
            <span>{t.addNew}</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Filters & Search Bar */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-12 gap-3 bg-slate-900/90 p-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800">
        <div className="sm:col-span-6 relative">
          <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3 top-3" />
          <input
            type="text"
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="በሰሌዳ፣ ሞዴል (Bajaj, HiAce, Yutong, Volvo, Sinotruk...)፣ አሽከርካሪ ወይም ባለቤት ፈልግ..."
            className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white text-xs rounded-xl pl-9 pr-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-emerald-500"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="sm:col-span-3">
          <select
            value={filterType}
            onChange={(e) => setFilterType(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-slate-200 text-xs rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none"
          >
            <option value="all">ሁሉም የተሽከርካሪ ዓይነቶች ({COMPLETE_VEHICLE_CATALOG.length} መደቦች)</option>
            {COMPLETE_VEHICLE_CATALOG.map((cat) => (
              <option key={cat.type} value={cat.type}>
                {cat.emoji} {cat.amharicName}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>

        <div className="sm:col-span-3">
          <select
            value={filterStatus}
            onChange={(e) => setFilterStatus(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-slate-200 text-xs rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none"
          >
            <option value="all">ሁሉም ሁኔታዎች (All Status)</option>
            <option value="on_route">በጉዞ ላይ (On Route)</option>
            <option value="in_queue">በተራ ላይ (In Queue)</option>
            <option value="active">ስራ ዝግጁ (Active)</option>
            <option value="suspended">የታገደ (Suspended)</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Vehicles Table */}
      <div className="bg-slate-900/95 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl shadow-xl overflow-hidden">
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-left text-xs text-slate-300">
            <thead className="bg-slate-950 text-slate-400 text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider border-b border-slate-800">
              <tr>
                <th className="px-4 py-3">{t.plateNumber}</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3">ዓይነትና ሞዴል</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3">ባለቤት / ማህበር</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3">አሽከርካሪ</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3">አሁን ያለበት ቦታ / መስመር</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3">ፍጥነትና ቴሌሜትሪ</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3">{t.status}</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-right">QR / ድርጊት</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-800/80">
              {filteredVehicles.map((v) => {
                const loc = resolveExactLocation(v.lat, v.lng);
                return (
                  <tr key={v.id} className="hover:bg-slate-800/40 transition-colors">
                    <td className="px-4 py-3.5">
                      <span className="font-mono font-bold text-amber-300 bg-slate-950 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md border border-slate-700">
                        {v.plateNumber}
                      </span>
                      <span className="block text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-mono mt-0.5">
                        {v.speedLimiterId}
                      </span>
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-4 py-3.5">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="text-lg">{getVehicleEmoji(v.vehicleType)}</span>
                        <div>
                          <p className="font-semibold text-slate-100">{v.makeModel}</p>
                          <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">
                            {getVehicleTypeLabel(v.vehicleType)} • {v.capacity}
                          </p>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-4 py-3.5">
                      <span className="font-medium text-slate-200">{v.ownerName}</span>
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-4 py-3.5">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                        <User className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                        <span className="font-medium text-slate-200">{v.driverName}</span>
                      </div>
                      <span className="text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-400 pl-5">{v.driverPhone}</span>
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-4 py-3.5 text-slate-300">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-emerald-400 font-semibold text-[11px]">
                        <MapPin className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0" />
                        <span className="truncate max-w-[160px]">{v.currentLocationName || loc.formattedLabel}</span>
                      </div>
                      <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block">
                        {v.origin || 'መነሻ'} ➔ {v.destination || 'ክፍት'}
                      </span>
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-4 py-3.5">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span
                          className={`font-mono font-bold text-xs ${
                            v.currentSpeed > v.speedLimit ? 'text-red-400 animate-pulse' : 'text-emerald-400'
                          }`}
                        >
                          {v.currentSpeed} km/h
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-mono">/ {v.speedLimit}</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                        <Fuel className="w-3 h-3 text-cyan-400" />
                        <span>{v.fuelLevel}%</span>
                      </div>
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-4 py-3.5">
                      <span
                        className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-semibold border ${
                          v.status === 'on_route'
                            ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-500/40'
                            : v.status === 'in_queue'
                            ? 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border-amber-500/40'
                            : v.status === 'suspended'
                            ? 'bg-red-500/20 text-red-300 border-red-500/40'
                            : 'bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 border-slate-600'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {v.status === 'on_route'
                          ? 'በጉዞ ላይ'
                          : v.status === 'in_queue'
                          ? 'በተራ ላይ'
                          : v.status === 'suspended'
                          ? 'የታገደ'
                          : 'ዝግጁ'}
                      </span>
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-4 py-3.5 text-right">
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1">
                        <button
                          onClick={() => setQrModalVehicle(v)}
                          className="p-1.5 hover:bg-cyan-900/40 text-cyan-400 rounded-lg transition-colors"
                          title="የዲጂታል QR ሰርተፍኬት"
                        >
                          <QrCode className="w-4 h-4" />
                        </button>
                        <button
                          onClick={() => handleDeleteVehicle(v.id)}
                          className="p-1.5 hover:bg-red-900/40 text-slate-400 hover:text-red-400 rounded-lg transition-colors"
                          title="አስወግድ"
                        >
                          <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Complete Vehicle Encyclopedia / Name Catalog Modal */}
      {isDirectoryOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-3xl max-w-4xl w-full p-6 text-white shadow-2xl space-y-4 max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                <BookOpen className="w-5 h-5 text-cyan-400" />
                <div>
                  <h3 className="font-bold text-base">ጠቅላላ የተሽከርካሪዎች ስም እና መደብ መዝገብ (Complete Fleet Directory)</h3>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                    በኢትዮጵያና በአለም አቀፍ ደረጃ የሚገኙ ሁሉም የተሽከርካሪ ዓይነቶችና ሞዴሎች — ለመመዝገብ የፈለጉትን ሞዴል ይጫኑ
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <button onClick={() => setIsDirectoryOpen(false)} className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-white">
                <X className="w-5 h-5" />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="relative">
              <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3 top-3" />
              <input
                type="text"
                value={directorySearch}
                onChange={(e) => setDirectorySearch(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="የተሽከርካሪ ስም ፈልግ (Bajaj, Damas, BYD, HiAce, Anbessa, Yutong, Sinotruk, Volvo, Ambulance...)"
                className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 rounded-xl pl-9 pr-3 py-2.5 text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-500"
              />
            </div>

            <div className="overflow-y-auto space-y-4 pr-1">
              {COMPLETE_VEHICLE_CATALOG.map((cat) => {
                const matchingModels = cat.models.filter(
                  (m) =>
                    !directorySearch ||
                    m.toLowerCase().includes(directorySearch.toLowerCase()) ||
                    cat.amharicName.toLowerCase().includes(directorySearch.toLowerCase()) ||
                    cat.englishName.toLowerCase().includes(directorySearch.toLowerCase())
                );
                if (matchingModels.length === 0) return null;

                return (
                  <div key={cat.type} className="bg-slate-950/80 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 space-y-2.5">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <h4 className="font-bold text-sm text-emerald-300 flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="text-lg">{cat.emoji}</span>
                        <span>{cat.amharicName}</span>
                        <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 font-normal">({cat.englishName})</span>
                      </h4>
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400 bg-slate-900 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-slate-800">
                        መደበኛ ወሰን: {cat.defaultSpeedLimit} km/h
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                      {matchingModels.map((model) => (
                        <button
                          key={model}
                          onClick={() => handlePickFromCatalog(cat.type, model)}
                          className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-emerald-600 text-slate-200 hover:text-white border border-slate-700 hover:border-emerald-400 rounded-xl text-xs font-medium transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5"
                        >
                          <span>{model}</span>
                          <Plus className="w-3 h-3 opacity-70" />
                        </button>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Digital QR Verification Certificate Modal */}
      {qrModalVehicle && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-cyan-500/50 rounded-3xl max-w-md w-full p-6 text-white shadow-2xl space-y-4">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <ShieldCheck className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-400" />
                <h3 className="font-bold text-sm">የብሔራዊ ትራንስፖርት ዲጂታል መታወቂያ (QR Pass)</h3>
              </div>
              <button onClick={() => setQrModalVehicle(null)} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-white">
                <X className="w-5 h-5" />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="bg-white text-slate-900 rounded-2xl p-5 space-y-3 text-center">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200 pb-2 text-left">
                <div>
                  <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-emerald-700 block">የተረጋገጠ ተሽከርካሪ</span>
                  <span className="font-mono font-black text-lg text-slate-950">{qrModalVehicle.plateNumber}</span>
                </div>
                <span className="text-3xl">{getVehicleEmoji(qrModalVehicle.vehicleType)}</span>
              </div>

              {/* Clean SVG Deterministic QR Verification Pattern */}
              <div className="mx-auto w-36 h-36 bg-slate-950 p-3 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center">
                <QrCode className="w-full h-full text-white" />
              </div>

              <div className="text-xs space-y-1 text-left bg-slate-100 p-3 rounded-xl">
                <p><strong>ሞዴል፡</strong> {qrModalVehicle.makeModel}</p>
                <p><strong>አሽከርካሪ፡</strong> {qrModalVehicle.driverName}</p>
                <p><strong>ባለቤት፡</strong> {qrModalVehicle.ownerName}</p>
                <p><strong>GPS ID:</strong> <span className="font-mono">{qrModalVehicle.speedLimiterId}</span></p>
                <p className="text-emerald-700 font-bold flex items-center gap-1 pt-1">
                  <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>የቴክኒክ ምርመራ የጸደቀ እስከ {qrModalVehicle.inspectionExpiry}</span>
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button
                onClick={() => window.print()}
                className="flex-1 py-2.5 bg-cyan-600 hover:bg-cyan-500 text-white font-bold text-xs rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
              >
                <Printer className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>አትም (Print QR Pass)</span>
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => setQrModalVehicle(null)}
                className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl"
              >
                ዝጋ
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Add Vehicle Modal */}
      {isAddModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/75 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 rounded-3xl max-w-lg w-full p-6 text-white shadow-2xl space-y-4 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Truck className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-400" />
                <h3 className="font-bold text-base">አዲስ ተሽከርካሪ መዝግብ (ሁሉም መደቦች ክፍት)</h3>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => setIsAddModalOpen(false)}
                className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-white rounded-lg"
              >
                <X className="w-5 h-5" />
              </button>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleCreateVehicle} className="space-y-4 text-xs">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-slate-300 font-medium mb-1">
                    የተሽከርካሪ ዓይነት *
                  </label>
                  <select
                    value={newType}
                    onChange={(e) => handleTypeChange(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none"
                  >
                    {COMPLETE_VEHICLE_CATALOG.map((cat) => (
                      <option key={cat.type} value={cat.type}>
                        {cat.emoji} {cat.amharicName}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-slate-300 font-medium mb-1">
                    የሰሌዳ ቁጥር (Plate No) *
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    placeholder="ለምሳሌ፡ 1-A10482 አ.አ"
                    value={newPlate}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewPlate(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-emerald-500 font-mono font-bold"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-slate-300 font-medium mb-1">ሞዴል / የተሽከርካሪ ስም *</label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    list="vehicle-models-datalist"
                    value={newMakeModel}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewMakeModel(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="ስም ይምረጡ ወይም ይጻፉ..."
                    className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none"
                  />
                  <datalist id="vehicle-models-datalist">
                    {(selectedCategoryObj ? selectedCategoryObj.models : ALL_VEHICLE_MODELS).map((m) => (
                      <option key={m} value={m} />
                    ))}
                    {ALL_VEHICLE_MODELS.map((m) => (
                      <option key={`all-${m}`} value={m} />
                    ))}
                  </datalist>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-slate-300 font-medium mb-1">
                    የመጫን አቅም ({selectedCategoryObj?.unitLabel || 'Seats / Tons'})
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    value={newCapacity}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewCapacity(Number(e.target.value))}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none font-mono"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-slate-300 font-medium mb-1">መነሻ ቦታ (የትኛውም ቦታ)</label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="ለምሳሌ፡ መርካቶ፣ ቃሊቲ..."
                    value={newOrigin}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewOrigin(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-slate-300 font-medium mb-1">መዳረሻ ቦታ (የትኛውም ቦታ)</label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="ለምሳሌ፡ ፒያሳ፣ ሀዋሳ..."
                    value={newDestination}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewDestination(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-slate-300 font-medium mb-1">
                    ባለቤት / ማህበር *
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    placeholder="የማህበሩ ወይም የባለቤቱ ስም..."
                    value={newOwner}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewOwner(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-slate-300 font-medium mb-1">የምርት ዓመት</label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    value={newYear}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewYear(Number(e.target.value))}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none font-mono"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-slate-300 font-medium mb-1">ተመዳቢ አሽከርካሪ</label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="ሙሉ ስም"
                    value={newDriver}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewDriver(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-slate-300 font-medium mb-1">ስልክ ቁጥር</label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="+251 9..."
                    value={newPhone}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewPhone(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setIsAddModalOpen(false)}
                  className="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 rounded-xl"
                >
                  {t.cancel}
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="px-5 py-2 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white font-bold rounded-xl shadow-lg"
                >
                  {t.save}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};


// =============================================================================
// FILE: src/components/VoiceTranslatorIntercom.tsx
// =============================================================================

import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  Mic,
  MicOff,
  Volume2,
  Send,
  ArrowRightLeft,
  Sparkles,
  MessageSquare,
  Headphones,
  Loader2,
  AlertCircle,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { Language, VoiceMessage } from '../types';
import { translations } from '../data/translations';
import { speakTextSmart, playStationChime, stopAllSpeech } from '../utils/audio';
import {
  translateMessageLive,
  transcribeAudioLive,
  TRANSPORT_PHRASES,
} from '../utils/translator';

interface VoiceTranslatorIntercomProps {
  currentLang: Language;
  audioAlertsEnabled: boolean;
}

interface IntercomEntry extends VoiceMessage {
  speakerSlot: 1 | 2;
  translationMode?: 'ai' | 'preset' | 'offline';
}

function blobToBase64(blob: Blob): Promise<string> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onloadend = () => {
      const result = typeof reader.result === 'string' ? reader.result : '';
      const base64 = result.includes(',') ? result.split(',')[1] : result;
      resolve(base64);
    };
    reader.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Failed to read recorded audio.'));
    reader.readAsDataURL(blob);
  });
}

export const VoiceTranslatorIntercom: React.FC<VoiceTranslatorIntercomProps> = ({
  currentLang,
  audioAlertsEnabled,
}) => {
  const t = translations[currentLang];

  // Speaker configurations
  const [speaker1Lang, setSpeaker1Lang] = useState<Language>('am');
  const [speaker2Lang, setSpeaker2Lang] = useState<Language>('om');
  const [speaker1Role, setSpeaker1Role] = useState<'dispatcher' | 'driver' | 'traffic_officer'>('dispatcher');
  const [speaker2Role, setSpeaker2Role] = useState<'passenger' | 'driver' | 'dispatcher'>('driver');

  // Input & recording states for BOTH speakers
  const [inputText1, setInputText1] = useState<string>('');
  const [inputText2, setInputText2] = useState<string>('');
  const [recordingSpeaker, setRecordingSpeaker] = useState<1 | 2 | null>(null);
  const [recordingSeconds, setRecordingSeconds] = useState<number>(0);
  const [isProcessing, setIsProcessing] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [processingLabel, setProcessingLabel] = useState<string>('');
  const [autoSpeak, setAutoSpeak] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [speakingMsgId, setSpeakingMsgId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [errorBanner, setErrorBanner] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Message log
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<IntercomEntry[]>([
    {
      id: 'msg_01',
      speakerSlot: 1,
      senderRole: 'dispatcher',
      senderName: 'ተቆጣጣሪ (Terminal Dispatcher)',
      sourceLang: 'am',
      targetLang: 'om',
      sourceText: 'ተራዎ ደርሷል! እባክዎ ተሽከርካሪዎን ወደ መጫኛ መድረክ ያስገቡ።',
      translatedText: 'Dabareen keessan ga\'eera! Maaloo konkolaataa keessan gara bakka fe\'iinsaatti galchaa.',
      timestamp: '10:14 ጧት',
      translationMode: 'preset',
    },
    {
      id: 'msg_02',
      speakerSlot: 2,
      senderRole: 'driver',
      senderName: 'አሽከርካሪ ተስፋዬ (Driver)',
      sourceLang: 'om',
      targetLang: 'am',
      sourceText: 'Tole, daqiiqaa 3 keessatti seenaa jira.',
      translatedText: 'እሺ፣ በ3 ደቂቃ ውስጥ እየገባሁ ነው።',
      timestamp: '10:15 ጧት',
      translationMode: 'preset',
    },
  ]);

  const messagesEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const mediaRecorderRef = useRef<MediaRecorder | null>(null);
  const audioChunksRef = useRef<Blob[]>([]);
  const mediaStreamRef = useRef<MediaStream | null>(null);
  const timerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null>(null);
  const recognitionRef = useRef<any>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    messagesEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  }, [messages]);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      stopAllSpeech();
      if (timerRef.current) clearInterval(timerRef.current);
      if (mediaStreamRef.current) {
        mediaStreamRef.current.getTracks().forEach((tr) => tr.stop());
      }
    };
  }, []);

  const bcpMap: Record<Language, string> = {
    am: 'am-ET',
    om: 'om-ET',
    ti: 'ti-ET',
    so: 'so-SO',
    en: 'en-US',
    ar: 'ar-SA',
    fr: 'fr-FR',
    zh: 'zh-CN',
  };

  const handleSend = async (text: string, speakerNum: 1 | 2 = 1) => {
    const rawText = text.trim();
    if (!rawText || isProcessing) return;

    setErrorBanner(null);
    const srcLang = speakerNum === 1 ? speaker1Lang : speaker2Lang;
    const tgtLang = speakerNum === 1 ? speaker2Lang : speaker1Lang;
    const role = speakerNum === 1 ? speaker1Role : speaker2Role;

    const roleNames: Record<string, string> = {
      dispatcher: 'የጣቢያ ተቆጣጣሪ (Dispatcher)',
      driver: 'አሽከርካሪ (Driver)',
      traffic_officer: 'የትራፊክ ፖሊስ (Traffic Police)',
      passenger: 'ተሳፋሪ / መንገደኛ (Passenger)',
    };

    setIsProcessing(true);
    setProcessingLabel(
      `ከ${srcLang.toUpperCase()} ወደ ${tgtLang.toUpperCase()} በመተርጎም ላይ... (Translating)`
    );

    try {
      const { translatedText, mode } = await translateMessageLive(rawText, srcLang, tgtLang);

      const now = new Date();
      const timeStr = now.toLocaleTimeString('en-US', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
      const msgId = `msg_${Date.now()}`;

      const newMsg: IntercomEntry = {
        id: msgId,
        speakerSlot: speakerNum,
        senderRole: role,
        senderName: roleNames[role] || 'ተጠቃሚ',
        sourceLang: srcLang,
        targetLang: tgtLang,
        sourceText: rawText,
        translatedText,
        timestamp: timeStr,
        translationMode: mode,
      };

      setMessages((prev) => [...prev, newMsg]);
      if (speakerNum === 1) {
        setInputText1('');
      } else {
        setInputText2('');
      }

      if (audioAlertsEnabled) {
        playStationChime();
      }

      if (autoSpeak) {
        setSpeakingMsgId(msgId);
        const ttsResult = await speakTextSmart(translatedText, tgtLang, () => {
          setSpeakingMsgId((cur) => (cur === msgId ? null : cur));
        });
        if (ttsResult.error) {
          setErrorBanner(`የድምፅ ማሰሚያ ማስጠንቀቂያ: ${ttsResult.error}`);
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setErrorBanner(
        err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'መልዕክቱን መተርጎም አልተቻለም። እባክዎ እንደገና ይሞክሩ።'
      );
    } finally {
      setIsProcessing(false);
      setProcessingLabel('');
    }
  };

  const stopMicCapture = () => {
    if (timerRef.current) {
      clearInterval(timerRef.current);
      timerRef.current = null;
    }
    if (mediaRecorderRef.current && mediaRecorderRef.current.state !== 'inactive') {
      mediaRecorderRef.current.stop();
    }
    if (recognitionRef.current) {
      try {
        recognitionRef.current.stop();
      } catch {
        // Ignore stop errors
      }
      recognitionRef.current = null;
    }
  };

  const toggleRecordingForSpeaker = async (speakerNum: 1 | 2) => {
    setErrorBanner(null);

    if (recordingSpeaker === speakerNum) {
      stopMicCapture();
      return;
    }

    if (recordingSpeaker !== null) {
      stopMicCapture();
    }

    const srcLang = speakerNum === 1 ? speaker1Lang : speaker2Lang;

    // Offline fallback using browser Web Speech API when offline
    if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && !navigator.onLine) {
      const SpeechRecognitionClass =
        (window as any).SpeechRecognition || (window as any).webkitSpeechRecognition;
      if (!SpeechRecognitionClass) {
        setErrorBanner('ከኔትወርክ ውጪ ድምፅ ለመቅዳት በዚህ ብሮውዘር አልተደገፈም፤ እባክዎ በጽሁፍ ይፃፉ።');
        return;
      }
      try {
        const recognition = new SpeechRecognitionClass();
        recognition.continuous = false;
        recognition.interimResults = false;
        recognition.lang = bcpMap[srcLang] || 'en-US';
        recognition.onresult = (event: any) => {
          const transcript = event.results?.[0]?.[0]?.transcript;
          setRecordingSpeaker(null);
          if (transcript) {
            void handleSend(transcript, speakerNum);
          }
        };
        recognition.onerror = () => {
          setRecordingSpeaker(null);
          setErrorBanner('የድምፅ መቅጃ ችግር ተፈጥሯል፤ እባክዎ የማይክሮፎን ፍቃድ ያረጋግጡ ወይም በጽሁፍ ይላኩ።');
        };
        recognition.onend = () => {
          setRecordingSpeaker(null);
        };
        recognitionRef.current = recognition;
        recognition.start();
        setRecordingSpeaker(speakerNum);
      } catch {
        setRecordingSpeaker(null);
        setErrorBanner('ማይክሮፎን መጀመር አልተቻለም።');
      }
      return;
    }

    // Online mode: real MediaRecorder capture -> Gemini speech transcription (/api/transcribe)
    if (!navigator.mediaDevices || !navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia) {
      setErrorBanner('ይህ ብሮውዘር የቀጥታ ማይክሮፎን ቀረጻ አይደግፍም፤ እባክዎ ከታች ባለው መጻፊያ ይላኩ።');
      return;
    }

    try {
      const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true });
      mediaStreamRef.current = stream;

      const preferredTypes = ['audio/webm;codecs=opus', 'audio/webm', 'audio/mp4', 'audio/ogg'];
      const supportedMime = preferredTypes.find((mt) =>
        typeof MediaRecorder !== 'undefined' && MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported(mt)
      );

      const recorder = supportedMime
        ? new MediaRecorder(stream, { mimeType: supportedMime })
        : new MediaRecorder(stream);

      audioChunksRef.current = [];
      recorder.ondataavailable = (e) => {
        if (e.data && e.data.size > 0) {
          audioChunksRef.current.push(e.data);
        }
      };

      recorder.onstop = async () => {
        const mime = recorder.mimeType || 'audio/webm';
        stream.getTracks().forEach((tr) => tr.stop());
        mediaStreamRef.current = null;
        if (timerRef.current) {
          clearInterval(timerRef.current);
          timerRef.current = null;
        }
        setRecordingSpeaker(null);
        setRecordingSeconds(0);

        const audioBlob = new Blob(audioChunksRef.current, { type: mime });
        if (audioBlob.size < 400) {
          setErrorBanner('በቂ ድምፅ አልተቀዳም፤ እባክዎ ማይክሮፎኑን ተጭነው በግልፅ ይናገሩ።');
          return;
        }

        setIsProcessing(true);
        setProcessingLabel(`የተቀዳውን ድምፅ በ${srcLang.toUpperCase()} ወደ ጽሁፍ በመቀየር ላይ...`);
        try {
          const base64 = await blobToBase64(audioBlob);
          const transcript = await transcribeAudioLive(base64, mime, srcLang);
          setIsProcessing(false);
          setProcessingLabel('');
          if (!transcript) {
            setErrorBanner('የተናገሩትን ድምፅ መለየት አልተቻለም፤ እባክዎ ድምፅዎን ከፍ አድርገው እንደገና ይሞክሩ።');
            return;
          }
          await handleSend(transcript, speakerNum);
        } catch (err) {
          setIsProcessing(false);
          setProcessingLabel('');
          setErrorBanner(
            err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'የድምፅ ትንተና አልተሳካም።'
          );
        }
      };

      mediaRecorderRef.current = recorder;
      recorder.start();
      setRecordingSpeaker(speakerNum);
      setRecordingSeconds(0);
      timerRef.current = setInterval(() => {
        setRecordingSeconds((s) => s + 1);
      }, 1000);
    } catch (err) {
      setRecordingSpeaker(null);
      setErrorBanner(
        err instanceof Error
          ? `የማይክሮፎን ፍቃድ አልተገኘም (${err.message})። እባክዎ የማይክሮፎን ፍቃድ ይስጡ ወይም በጽሁፍ ይላኩ።`
          : 'የማይክሮፎን ፍቃድ አልተገኘም፤ እባክዎ በጽሁፍ ይላኩ።'
      );
    }
  };

  const handleReplayAudio = async (msg: IntercomEntry) => {
    setErrorBanner(null);
    setSpeakingMsgId(msg.id);
    const res = await speakTextSmart(msg.translatedText, msg.targetLang, () => {
      setSpeakingMsgId((cur) => (cur === msg.id ? null : cur));
    });
    if (res.error) {
      setErrorBanner(`የድምፅ ማሰሚያ ማስጠንቀቂያ: ${res.error}`);
    }
  };

  const handleSwapLanguages = () => {
    const temp = speaker1Lang;
    setSpeaker1Lang(speaker2Lang);
    setSpeaker2Lang(temp);
  };

  const languagesList: { code: Language; label: string }[] = [
    { code: 'am', label: 'አማርኛ (Amharic)' },
    { code: 'om', label: 'Afaan Oromoo (Oromo)' },
    { code: 'ti', label: 'ትግርኛ (Tigrinya)' },
    { code: 'so', label: 'Soomaali (Somali)' },
    { code: 'en', label: 'English (ዓለም አቀፍ)' },
    { code: 'ar', label: 'العربية (Arabic)' },
    { code: 'fr', label: 'Français (French)' },
    { code: 'zh', label: '中文 (Chinese - 汉语)' },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Title & Introduction Banner */}
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-950 via-slate-900 to-indigo-950 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-800/40 p-5 shadow-xl">
        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="p-3 bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-500/30">
              <Sparkles className="w-6 h-6" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-white flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                {t.voiceIntercomTitle}
                <span className="text-[10px] bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-400/30 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-mono uppercase">
                  Live AI Two-Way Speech & Text
                </span>
              </h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 max-w-2xl mt-0.5">
                {t.voiceIntercomDesc}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Auto Speak Audio Toggle */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-700/80 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              id="autoSpeakCheck"
              checked={autoSpeak}
              onChange={(e) => setAutoSpeak(e.target.checked)}
              className="w-4 h-4 rounded text-emerald-500 focus:ring-emerald-400 bg-slate-800 border-slate-700 cursor-pointer"
            />
            <label htmlFor="autoSpeakCheck" className="text-slate-200 font-medium cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <Headphones className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
              <span>{t.autoSpeakTranslation}</span>
            </label>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Error Alert Banner */}
      {errorBanner && (
        <div className="bg-red-950/70 border border-red-500/60 text-red-100 px-4 py-3 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-between gap-3 text-xs shadow-lg">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-red-400 shrink-0" />
            <span>{errorBanner}</span>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={() => setErrorBanner(null)}
            className="text-red-300 hover:text-white p-1"
            aria-label="Close alert"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Language & Role Dispatch Console Bar */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-12 gap-3 bg-slate-800/90 border border-slate-700/80 rounded-2xl p-4 shadow-lg">
        {/* Speaker 1 Settings */}
        <div className="md:col-span-5 bg-slate-900/80 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-700 space-y-2">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-emerald-400 flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 animate-pulse" />
              <span>{t.speaker1}</span>
            </span>
            <select
              value={speaker1Role}
              onChange={(e) => setSpeaker1Role(e.target.value as 'dispatcher' | 'driver' | 'traffic_officer')}
              className="bg-slate-800 text-[11px] text-slate-200 border border-slate-700 rounded px-2 py-0.5"
            >
              <option value="dispatcher">የጣቢያ ተቆጣጣሪ (Dispatcher)</option>
              <option value="driver">አሽከርካሪ (Driver)</option>
              <option value="traffic_officer">የትራፊክ ፖሊስ (Police)</option>
            </select>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <label className="text-xs text-slate-400">ቋንቋ:</label>
            <select
              value={speaker1Lang}
              onChange={(e) => setSpeaker1Lang(e.target.value as Language)}
              className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-emerald-500/50 text-white font-semibold text-xs rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 focus:outline-none"
            >
              {languagesList.map((lang) => (
                <option key={lang.code} value={lang.code}>
                  {lang.label}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Swap Button */}
        <div className="md:col-span-2 flex items-center justify-center">
          <button
            onClick={handleSwapLanguages}
            title="ቋንቋዎችን ቀያይር (Swap Languages)"
            className="p-3 bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-700 border border-slate-700 hover:border-emerald-500 text-slate-300 hover:text-white rounded-xl shadow-md transition-all active:scale-95"
          >
            <ArrowRightLeft className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-400" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Speaker 2 Settings */}
        <div className="md:col-span-5 bg-slate-900/80 p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-700 space-y-2">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-cyan-400 flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-cyan-400 animate-pulse" />
              <span>{t.speaker2}</span>
            </span>
            <select
              value={speaker2Role}
              onChange={(e) => setSpeaker2Role(e.target.value as 'passenger' | 'driver' | 'dispatcher')}
              className="bg-slate-800 text-[11px] text-slate-200 border border-slate-700 rounded px-2 py-0.5"
            >
              <option value="driver">አሽከርካሪ (Driver)</option>
              <option value="passenger">መንገደኛ / ተሳፋሪ (Passenger)</option>
              <option value="dispatcher">የመናኸሪያ ኦፕሬተር (Operator)</option>
            </select>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <label className="text-xs text-slate-400">ቋንቋ:</label>
            <select
              value={speaker2Lang}
              onChange={(e) => setSpeaker2Lang(e.target.value as Language)}
              className="w-full bg-slate-800 border border-cyan-500/50 text-white font-semibold text-xs rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 focus:outline-none"
            >
              {languagesList.map((lang) => (
                <option key={lang.code} value={lang.code}>
                  {lang.label}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Chat Intercom Window */}
      <div className="bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-800 rounded-2xl p-4 sm:p-5 shadow-2xl flex flex-col gap-3">
        {/* Messages Scroll Area */}
        <div className="h-[360px] overflow-y-auto space-y-4 pr-2 scrollbar-thin">
          {messages.map((msg) => {
            const isSpeaker1 = msg.speakerSlot === 1;

            return (
              <div
                key={msg.id}
                className={`flex flex-col ${isSpeaker1 ? 'items-start' : 'items-end'}`}
              >
                {/* Sender badge */}
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1 px-1 text-xs">
                  <span className={`font-semibold ${isSpeaker1 ? 'text-emerald-300' : 'text-cyan-300'}`}>
                    {msg.senderName}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-mono">{msg.timestamp}</span>
                  {msg.translationMode && (
                    <span className="text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 font-mono uppercase">
                      {msg.translationMode === 'ai'
                        ? 'AI Live'
                        : msg.translationMode === 'offline'
                        ? 'Offline'
                        : 'Verified'}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* Message Bubble */}
                <div
                  className={`max-w-[90%] sm:max-w-[78%] rounded-2xl p-4 shadow-lg border relative ${
                    isSpeaker1
                      ? 'bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-800 to-slate-800/90 border-emerald-500/40 text-slate-100 rounded-tl-none'
                      : 'bg-gradient-to-br from-teal-950 to-slate-900 border-cyan-500/40 text-slate-100 rounded-tr-none'
                  }`}
                >
                  {/* Original Spoken Text */}
                  <div className="border-b border-slate-700/60 pb-2 mb-2">
                    <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-mono tracking-wider text-slate-400 block mb-0.5">
                      የተነገረው / Original ({msg.sourceLang.toUpperCase()}):
                    </span>
                    <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-200 italic">
                      "{msg.sourceText}"
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  {/* Translated Audio & Text Block */}
                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                    <div>
                      <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-mono tracking-wider text-emerald-400 font-bold block mb-0.5">
                        የተተረጎመው / Translated ({msg.targetLang.toUpperCase()}):
                      </span>
                      <p className="text-base font-bold text-white leading-relaxed">
                        {msg.translatedText}
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    {/* Audio Replay Button */}
                    <button
                      onClick={() => handleReplayAudio(msg)}
                      title="ድምፁን እንደገና አሰማ (Replay Voice)"
                      className={`p-2 rounded-xl border transition-colors shrink-0 active:scale-95 shadow ${
                        speakingMsgId === msg.id
                          ? 'bg-emerald-500 border-emerald-300 text-white animate-pulse'
                          : 'bg-emerald-600/30 hover:bg-emerald-600 border-emerald-500/40 text-emerald-300 hover:text-white'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <Volume2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
          <div ref={messagesEndRef} />
        </div>

        {/* Live Recording or AI Processing Bar */}
        {recordingSpeaker !== null && (
          <div className="py-2 px-3 bg-red-950/50 border border-red-500/50 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-red-200">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-semibold">
              <span className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-red-500 animate-ping" />
              <span>
                ተናጋሪ {recordingSpeaker} እየተቀዳ ነው ({recordingSeconds}s) — ሲጨርሱ "አቁም" የሚለውን ይጫኑ
              </span>
            </div>
            <button
              onClick={stopMicCapture}
              className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-500 text-white font-bold rounded-lg text-[11px]"
            >
              አቁምና ተርጉም
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        {isProcessing && (
          <div className="py-2 px-3 bg-emerald-950/50 border border-emerald-500/40 rounded-xl flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-emerald-300">
            <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin shrink-0" />
            <span>{processingLabel || 'በመተርጎም ላይ...'}</span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Dual Two-Way Input Consoles (Speaker 1 & Speaker 2) */}
        <div className="pt-3 border-t border-slate-800 grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          {/* Speaker 1 Input */}
          <div className="bg-slate-950/70 border border-emerald-500/30 rounded-xl p-2.5 space-y-2">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-bold text-emerald-400 px-1">
              <span>ተናጋሪ 1 ({speaker1Lang.toUpperCase()} ➔ {speaker2Lang.toUpperCase()})</span>
              <span className="text-slate-400 font-normal">የድምፅ / ጽሁፍ መላኪያ</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <button
                onClick={() => toggleRecordingForSpeaker(1)}
                disabled={isProcessing}
                className={`p-2.5 rounded-xl border font-bold text-white transition-all shadow active:scale-95 flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0 disabled:opacity-40 ${
                  recordingSpeaker === 1
                    ? 'bg-red-600 border-red-400 animate-pulse'
                    : 'bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 border-emerald-500'
                }`}
                title={recordingSpeaker === 1 ? 'ቅጂውን አቁም' : t.pressToSpeak}
              >
                {recordingSpeaker === 1 ? <MicOff className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Mic className="w-4 h-4" />}
              </button>

              <input
                type="text"
                value={inputText1}
                onChange={(e) => setInputText1(e.target.value)}
                onKeyDown={(e) => {
                  if (e.key === 'Enter') void handleSend(inputText1, 1);
                }}
                placeholder={`በ${speaker1Lang.toUpperCase()} ይፃፉ ወይም ይናገሩ...`}
                className="flex-1 min-w-0 bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white text-xs sm:text-sm rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500"
              />

              <button
                onClick={() => void handleSend(inputText1, 1)}
                disabled={!inputText1.trim() || isProcessing}
                className="p-2.5 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 disabled:opacity-40 text-white rounded-xl shadow transition-all active:scale-95 shrink-0"
                title={t.instantTranslate}
              >
                <Send className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Speaker 2 Input */}
          <div className="bg-slate-950/70 border border-cyan-500/30 rounded-xl p-2.5 space-y-2">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-bold text-cyan-400 px-1">
              <span>ተናጋሪ 2 ({speaker2Lang.toUpperCase()} ➔ {speaker1Lang.toUpperCase()})</span>
              <span className="text-slate-400 font-normal">መልስ መስጫ (Reply)</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <button
                onClick={() => toggleRecordingForSpeaker(2)}
                disabled={isProcessing}
                className={`p-2.5 rounded-xl border font-bold text-white transition-all shadow active:scale-95 flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0 disabled:opacity-40 ${
                  recordingSpeaker === 2
                    ? 'bg-red-600 border-red-400 animate-pulse'
                    : 'bg-cyan-600 hover:bg-cyan-500 border-cyan-500'
                }`}
                title={recordingSpeaker === 2 ? 'ቅጂውን አቁም' : t.pressToSpeak}
              >
                {recordingSpeaker === 2 ? <MicOff className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Mic className="w-4 h-4" />}
              </button>

              <input
                type="text"
                value={inputText2}
                onChange={(e) => setInputText2(e.target.value)}
                onKeyDown={(e) => {
                  if (e.key === 'Enter') void handleSend(inputText2, 2);
                }}
                placeholder={`በ${speaker2Lang.toUpperCase()} መልስ ይፃፉ ወይም ይናገሩ...`}
                className="flex-1 min-w-0 bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-white text-xs sm:text-sm rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-cyan-500"
              />

              <button
                onClick={() => void handleSend(inputText2, 2)}
                disabled={!inputText2.trim() || isProcessing}
                className="p-2.5 bg-cyan-600 hover:bg-cyan-500 disabled:opacity-40 text-white rounded-xl shadow transition-all active:scale-95 shrink-0"
                title={t.instantTranslate}
              >
                <Send className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Quick Transport Presets Phrases */}
      <div className="bg-slate-800/80 rounded-2xl border border-slate-700/80 p-4 shadow-lg space-y-3">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-300 uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-2">
            <MessageSquare className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
            <span>{t.samplePhrases} (በአንድ ጠቅታ ተርጉመህ በድምፅ አሰማ)</span>
          </h3>
          <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 font-mono">
            {TRANSPORT_PHRASES.length} አገራዊ የትራንስፖርት ንግግሮች
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-2.5">
          {TRANSPORT_PHRASES.map((phrase) => {
            const currentSourceText = phrase.translations[speaker1Lang] || phrase.translations.am;
            const currentTargetText = phrase.translations[speaker2Lang] || phrase.translations.en;

            return (
              <button
                key={phrase.id}
                onClick={() => void handleSend(currentSourceText, 1)}
                disabled={isProcessing}
                className="text-left p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/80 hover:bg-slate-700/80 border border-slate-700/80 hover:border-emerald-500/70 transition-all group shadow disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[10px] text-slate-400 uppercase font-mono mb-1">
                  <span className="text-emerald-400 font-semibold">{phrase.category}</span>
                  <Volume2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-500 group-hover:text-emerald-400 group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform" />
                </div>
                <p className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-200 line-clamp-2 mb-1">
                  {currentSourceText}
                </p>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 line-clamp-1 italic">
                  ➔ {currentTargetText}
                </p>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export const ALL_VEHICLE_MODELS: string[] = COMPLETE_VEHICLE_CATALOG.flatMap((c) => c.models);

export function getVehicleEmoji(type: string): string {
  const found = COMPLETE_VEHICLE_CATALOG.find((c) => c.type === type);
  return found ? found.emoji : '🚘';
}

export function getVehicleTypeLabel(type: string): string {
  const found = COMPLETE_VEHICLE_CATALOG.find((c) => c.type === type);
  return found ? found.amharicName : type;
}


// =============================================================================
// FILE: src/firebase.ts
// =============================================================================

import { initializeApp } from 'firebase/app';
import { getAuth, GoogleAuthProvider, signInWithPopup, signOut } from 'firebase/auth';
import {
  doc,
  getDocFromServer,
  getFirestore,
  initializeFirestore,
  persistentLocalCache,
  persistentMultipleTabManager,
  Firestore,
} from 'firebase/firestore';
import firebaseConfig from '../firebase-applet-config.json';

const app = initializeApp(firebaseConfig);

let dbInstance: Firestore;
try {
  dbInstance = initializeFirestore(
    app,
    {
      localCache: persistentLocalCache({
        tabManager: persistentMultipleTabManager(),
      }),
    },
    firebaseConfig.firestoreDatabaseId
  );
} catch {
  dbInstance = getFirestore(app, firebaseConfig.firestoreDatabaseId);
}

export const db: Firestore = dbInstance;
export const auth = getAuth(app);
export const googleProvider = new GoogleAuthProvider();
googleProvider.setCustomParameters({ prompt: 'select_account' });

export enum OperationType {
  CREATE = 'create',
  UPDATE = 'update',
  DELETE = 'delete',
  LIST = 'list',
  GET = 'get',
  WRITE = 'write',
}

export interface FirestoreErrorInfo {
  error: string;
  operationType: OperationType;
  path: string | null;
  authInfo: {
    userId?: string | null;
    email?: string | null;
    emailVerified?: boolean | null;
    isAnonymous?: boolean | null;
    tenantId?: string | null;
    providerInfo?: {
      providerId?: string | null;
      email?: string | null;
    }[];
  };
}

export function handleFirestoreError(
  error: unknown,
  operationType: OperationType,
  path: string | null
): never {
  const errInfo: FirestoreErrorInfo = {
    error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
    authInfo: {
      userId: auth.currentUser?.uid,
      email: auth.currentUser?.email,
      emailVerified: auth.currentUser?.emailVerified,
      isAnonymous: auth.currentUser?.isAnonymous,
      tenantId: auth.currentUser?.tenantId,
      providerInfo:
        auth.currentUser?.providerData?.map((provider) => ({
          providerId: provider.providerId,
          email: provider.email,
        })) || [],
    },
    operationType,
    path,
  };
  console.error('Firestore Error: ', JSON.stringify(errInfo));
  throw new Error(JSON.stringify(errInfo));
}

export async function testConnection(): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    await getDocFromServer(doc(db, 'test', 'connection'));
    return true;
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof Error && error.message.includes('the client is offline')) {
      console.error('Firebase Firestore configuration check: client is offline.');
    }
    return false;
  }
}

export async function signInWithGoogle() {
  return signInWithPopup(auth, googleProvider);
}

export async function signOutFromCloud() {
  return signOut(auth);
}


// =============================================================================
// FILE: src/hooks/usePWAInstall.ts
// =============================================================================

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

interface BeforeInstallPromptEvent extends Event {
  prompt: () => Promise<void>;
  userChoice: Promise<{ outcome: 'accepted' | 'dismissed'; platform: string }>;
}

export function usePWAInstall() {
  const [deferredPrompt, setDeferredPrompt] = useState<BeforeInstallPromptEvent | null>(null);
  const [isInstalled, setIsInstalled] = useState(false);
  const [isIOS, setIsIOS] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Detect standalone mode (already installed)
    const isStandalone =
      typeof window !== 'undefined' &&
      (window.matchMedia('(display-mode: standalone)').matches ||
        (window.navigator as unknown as { standalone?: boolean }).standalone === true);
    setIsInstalled(Boolean(isStandalone));

    // Detect iOS devices
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      const userAgent = window.navigator.userAgent.toLowerCase();
      const isIOSDevice = /iphone|ipad|ipod/.test(userAgent);
      setIsIOS(isIOSDevice);
    }

    const handleBeforeInstallPrompt = (e: Event) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      setDeferredPrompt(e as BeforeInstallPromptEvent);
    };

    const handleAppInstalled = () => {
      setIsInstalled(true);
      setDeferredPrompt(null);
    };

    window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', handleBeforeInstallPrompt);
    window.addEventListener('appinstalled', handleAppInstalled);

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', handleBeforeInstallPrompt);
      window.removeEventListener('appinstalled', handleAppInstalled);
    };
  }, []);

  const install = async () => {
    if (!deferredPrompt) return false;
    await deferredPrompt.prompt();
    const { outcome } = await deferredPrompt.userChoice;
    if (outcome === 'accepted') {
      setIsInstalled(true);
      setDeferredPrompt(null);
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  };

  return {
    isInstallable: !!deferredPrompt,
    isInstalled,
    isIOS,
    install,
  };
}

// =============================================================================
// FILE: src/types.ts

export type Language = 'am' | 'om' | 'ti' | 'so' | 'en' | 'ar' | 'fr' | 'zh';

export type VehicleType = 
  | 'bajaj'          // ባጃጅ (3-Wheeler Auto Rickshaw)
  | 'motorcycle'     // ሞተርሳይክል (Motorbike Taxi)
  | 'damas'          // ዳማስ / ሱዙኪ ቫን (Small Mini Van)
  | 'taxi'           // ታክሲ (Sedan / Lada Taxi)
  | 'ev_taxi'        // ኤሌክትሪክ ታክሲ (Electric Taxi / BYD / VW ID)
  | 'minibus'        // ሚኒባስ ታክሲ (12-16 Seats)
  | 'midibus'        // ኮድ 3 መካከለኛ አውቶቡስ (27-32 Seats)
  | 'city_bus'       // የከተማ አውቶቡስ (Anbessa / Sheger City Bus)
  | 'bus'            // የረጅም ርቀት አውቶቡስ (45-65 Seats)
  | 'vip_coach'      // ቪአይፒ የቅንጦት ባስ (Luxury Coach)
  | 'pickup'         // ፒካፕ (Pickup / 4x4 Double Cab)
  | 'suv'            // ላንድ ክሩዘር / ኤስዩቪ (SUV / Field 4WD)
  | 'truck'          // አይሱዙ / ሲኖትራክ የከባድ ጭነት (Freight Truck)
  | 'tanker'         // የነዳጅ / ፈሳሽ ቦቴ (Fuel & Liquid Tanker)
  | 'trailer'        // ተሳቢ ከባድ መኪና (Heavy Semi-Trailer)
  | 'ambulance'      // አምቡላንስ (Emergency Ambulance)
  | 'special';       // ልዩ ተሽከርካሪ (Special Vehicle / Fire / Crane)

export type VehicleStatus = 'active' | 'on_route' | 'in_queue' | 'maintenance' | 'suspended';

export type InspectionStatus = 'passed' | 'warning' | 'expired';

export interface GpsTrailPoint {
  lat: number;
  lng: number;
  speed: number;
  timestamp: string;
  placeName: string;
}

export interface Vehicle {
  id: string;
  plateNumber: string; // e.g. 3-A24591 አ.አ
  vehicleType: VehicleType | string;
  makeModel: string;
  year: number;
  capacity: number; // passenger seats or cargo tons
  ownerId: string;
  ownerName: string;
  driverId: string;
  driverName: string;
  driverPhone: string;
  speedLimiterId: string;
  status: VehicleStatus;
  inspectionStatus: InspectionStatus;
  inspectionExpiry: string;
  fuelLevel: number; // 0-100%
  currentSpeed: number; // km/h
  speedLimit: number; // km/h
  lat: number;
  lng: number;
  heading?: number; // 0-359 degrees
  currentLocationName?: string;
  currentLocationImage?: string;
  trail?: GpsTrailPoint[];
  routeId?: string;
  routeName?: string;
  origin?: string;
  destination?: string;
  lastUpdate: string;
}

export interface Driver {
  id: string;
  fullName: string;
  licenseNumber: string;
  licenseGrade: string; // Any grade allowed (ደረጃ 1-5, ህዝብ 1-2, ሞተር/ባጃጅ, International, etc.)
  phone: string;
  experienceYears: number;
  rating: number; // 1-5

// =============================================================================
// FILE: src/utils/audio.ts
// =============================================================================

/**
 * Audio Synthesizer and Voice Alert Engine
 * Uses Web Audio API for offline zero-latency chimes and alerts,
 * server-side Gemini TTS (/api/tts) for accurate multilingual speech (am, om, ti, so, en, ar, fr, zh),
 * and Web Speech API fallback when offline.
 */

let audioCtx: AudioContext | null = null;
let activeAudioElement: HTMLAudioElement | null = null;

function getAudioContext(): AudioContext | null {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null;
  if (!audioCtx) {
    const AudioContextClass =
      window.AudioContext ||
      (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;
    if (AudioContextClass) {
      audioCtx = new AudioContextClass();
    }
  }
  if (audioCtx && audioCtx.state === 'suspended') {
    void audioCtx.resume();
  }
  return audioCtx;
}

export function stopAllSpeech() {
  if (activeAudioElement) {
    try {
      activeAudioElement.pause();
      activeAudioElement.currentTime = 0;
    } catch {
      // Ignore audio cleanup errors
    }
    activeAudioElement = null;
  }
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'speechSynthesis' in window) {
    window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
  }
}

/**
 * Play a two-tone terminal chime ("Ding-Dong") for queue call
 */
export function playStationChime() {
  const ctx = getAudioContext();
  if (!ctx) return;

  const now = ctx.currentTime;

  const osc1 = ctx.createOscillator();
  const gain1 = ctx.createGain();
  osc1.type = 'sine';
  osc1.frequency.setValueAtTime(587.33, now);
  gain1.gain.setValueAtTime(0.3, now);
  gain1.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + 0.8);
  osc1.connect(gain1);
  gain1.connect(ctx.destination);
  osc1.start(now);
  osc1.stop(now + 0.8);

  const osc2 = ctx.createOscillator();
  const gain2 = ctx.createGain();
  osc2.type = 'sine';
  osc2.frequency.setValueAtTime(440, now + 0.35);
  gain2.gain.setValueAtTime(0.35, now + 0.35);
  gain2.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + 1.2);
  osc2.connect(gain2);
  gain2.connect(ctx.destination);
  osc2.start(now + 0.35);
  osc2.stop(now + 1.2);
}

/**
 * Play a police/traffic emergency warning siren
 */
export function playEmergencySiren() {
  const ctx = getAudioContext();
  if (!ctx) return;

  const now = ctx.currentTime;
  const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
  const gain = ctx.createGain();
  osc.type = 'sawtooth';

  osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(700, now);
  osc.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(1100, now + 0.25);
  osc.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(700, now + 0.5);
  osc.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(1100, now + 0.75);
  osc.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(700, now + 1.0);

  gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.2, now);
  gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, now + 1.1);

  osc.connect(gain);
  gain.connect(ctx.destination);
  osc.start(now);
  osc.stop(now + 1.1);
}

/**
 * Play success / dispatch tone
 */
export function playSuccessTone() {
  const ctx = getAudioContext();
  if (!ctx) return;

  const now = ctx.currentTime;
  const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
  const gain = ctx.createGain();
  osc.type = 'triangle';

  osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(523.25, now);
  osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(659.25, now + 0.1);
  osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(783.99, now + 0.2);
  osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(1046.5, now + 0.3);

  gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.25, now);
  gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + 0.6);

  osc.connect(gain);
  gain.connect(ctx.destination);
  osc.start(now);
  osc.stop(now + 0.6);
}

/**
 * Speak text in a specified language via browser Web Speech Synthesis (offline / local)
 */
export function speakText(text: string, langCode: string = 'am', onEnd?: () => void) {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !('speechSynthesis' in window)) {
    if (onEnd) onEnd();
    return;
  }

  window.speechSynthesis.cancel();

  const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);

  const bcpMap: Record<string, string> = {
    am: 'am-ET',
    om: 'om-ET',
    ti: 'ti-ET',
    so: 'so-SO',
    en: 'en-US',
    ar: 'ar-SA',
    fr: 'fr-FR',
    zh: 'zh-CN',
  };

  const targetBcp = bcpMap[langCode] || 'en-US';
  utterance.lang = targetBcp;
  utterance.rate = 0.95;
  utterance.pitch = 1.0;

  const voices = window.speechSynthesis.getVoices();
  const matchedVoice = voices.find((v) => v.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith(targetBcp.substring(0, 2).toLowerCase()));
  if (matchedVoice) {
    utterance.voice = matchedVoice;
  }

  utterance.onend = () => {
    if (onEnd) onEnd();
  };

  utterance.onerror = () => {
    if (onEnd) onEnd();
  };

  window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
}

/**
 * Speak text aloud using server-side Gemini TTS (all 8 languages including am, om, ti, so)
 * when online, with clean offline Web Speech Synthesis fallback.
 */
export async function speakTextSmart(
  text: string,
  langCode: string = 'am',
  onEnd?: () => void
): Promise<{ provider: 'gemini-tts' | 'browser'; error?: string }> {
  const cleanText = text.trim();
  if (!cleanText) {
    if (onEnd) onEnd();
    return { provider: 'browser' };
  }

  stopAllSpeech();

  if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && !navigator.onLine) {
    speakText(cleanText, langCode, onEnd);
    return { provider: 'browser' };
  }

  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/tts', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ text: cleanText, langCode }),
    });

    const data = (await res.json().catch(() => ({}))) as {
      audioBase64?: string;
      mimeType?: string;
      error?: string;
    };

    if (!res.ok || !data.audioBase64) {
      // Fallback to native browser voice if available while reporting TTS API error
      speakText(cleanText, langCode, onEnd);
      return {
        provider: 'browser',
        error: data.error || `Speech synthesis error (HTTP ${res.status})`,
      };
    }

    const mime = data.mimeType || 'audio/wav';
    const audio = new Audio(`data:${mime};base64,${data.audioBase64}`);
    activeAudioElement = audio;

    audio.onended = () => {
      if (activeAudioElement === audio) {
        activeAudioElement = null;
      }
      if (onEnd) onEnd();
    };

    audio.onerror = () => {
      if (activeAudioElement === audio) {
        activeAudioElement = null;
      }
      speakText(cleanText, langCode, onEnd);
    };

    await audio.play();
    return { provider: 'gemini-tts' };
  } catch (err) {
    speakText(cleanText, langCode, onEnd);
    return {
      provider: 'browser',
      error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'TTS playback error',
    };
  }
}
